From Fixed to Free Cameras: Calibration-Free View-Robust Vision-Language-Action Model
Authors: Wenhao Li, Xueying Jiang, Quanhao Qian, Deli Zhao, Shijian Lu, Gongjie Zhang, Ran Xu
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-world robot deployment rarely maintains the training-stage camera setup, where cameras often experience repositioning or remounting depending on actual scenarios. Existing view-robust Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies tolerate such camera variations only when the camera extrinsics are explicitly provided, making them fragile and hard to use especially when view robustness is critical. We argue that the policy should not be told where the camera is, but rather figure it out by itself. To this end, we introduce Camera-Centric VLA (CamVLA), a new VLA model that decouples manipulation controls from camera geometry by predicting (i) a camera-centric end-effector action expressed in the local camera frame, and (ii) a 6-DoF hand-eye matrix relating cameras to the robot base. A deterministic geometric transformation composes the two predictions into a robot base-frame action. This disentangles how I should move in pose-independent camera-centric action generation from where I am looking from in camera-perspective geometric grounding. The resulting policy is calibration-free, depth-free, and single-view, requiring only a single monocular RGB image as the visual observation and task instruction at deployment. Evaluations in both simulation and real-world robot data show that CamVLA consistently improves success rates across diverse unseen viewpoints. Project page: https://alibaba-damo-academy.github.io/CamVLA/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从固定到自由相机：无需校准的视角鲁棒视觉-语言-动作模型
现实世界中的机器人部署很少保持训练阶段的相机设置，相机常因实际场景需求而重新定位或重新安装。现有视角鲁棒的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略仅在明确提供相机外参时才能容忍此类相机变化，使其脆弱且难以使用，尤其在视角鲁棒性至关重要的场景下。我们认为，策略不应被告知相机的位置，而应自行推断。为此，我们提出了相机中心VLA（CamVLA），一种新的VLA模型，通过预测（i）以相机为中心的末端执行器动作（表达在局部相机坐标系中），以及（ii）关联相机与机器人基座的6自由度手眼矩阵，将两个预测通过确定性几何变换组合成机器人基座坐标系下的动作。该方法将姿态无关的相机中心动作生成与相机视角的几何定位解耦。所得到的策略无需校准、无需深度信息、单视角即可工作，仅需单目RGB图像作为视觉观测和任务指令即可部署。仿真与真实机器人数据的评估表明，CamVLA在多种未见过的视角下持续提升成功率。项目页面：https://alibaba-damo-academy.github.io/CamVLA/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for view-robust robot control in real-world deployments where camera positions frequently change without calibration, the authors propose CamVLA, a calibration-free Vision-Language-Action model that decouples action generation from camera geometry. It predicts camera-centric end-effector actions and a hand-eye transformation matrix to map camera poses to robot base frames, enabling pose-independent manipulation. Experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that CamVLA achieves higher success rates across diverse, unseen camera viewpoints compared to baseline methods, without requiring depth or extrinsic calibration.
为解决实际部署中相机位置频繁变动导致的视觉视角不一致问题，本文提出Camera-Centric VLA（CamVLA）模型，该模型将操作控制与相机几何解耦。CamVLA预测相机坐标系下的末端执行器动作及6自由度手眼变换矩阵，将相机视角映射至机器人基座坐标系，从而实现无需标定的视角鲁棒动作生成。仿真与真实机器人实验表明，CamVLA在多种未见过的相机视角下均显著提升成功率，验证了其在动态视觉环境中的鲁棒性和实用性。
Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Shiyuan Feng, Huan-ang Gao, Haohan Chi, Hanlin Wu, Zhilong Zhang, Zheng Jiang, Bingxiang He, Wei-Ying Ma, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhou
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://bytedtsinghua-sia.github.io/Direct-OPD/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过直接在线策略蒸馏实现弱到强的泛化
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是提升语言模型推理能力的有效方法，但每次在新强模型上重复使用成本高昂，因为目标模型需在训练期间生成大量轨迹。随着模型规模扩大，训练后阶段本身已成为瓶颈。我们研究了一种弱到强的替代方案：在较小模型上运行强化学习（其轨迹成本较低），然后重用该强化学习过程所学到的知识以改进更强的目标模型。直接蒸馏强化学习后的弱教师模型不足，因为教师的最终策略混合了有用的强化学习收益与小模型的局限性。我们提出直接在线策略蒸馏（Direct-OPD），其转移的是教师的强化学习诱导的策略变化。Direct-OPD将强化学习后教师与自身强化学习前参考模型进行比较，并将二者对数比值作为学生模型的密集隐式奖励。通俗而言，检查点对告诉我们强化学习使弱模型更可能或更不可能采取哪些动作，而Direct-OPD将此信号应用于更强学生模型自身的在线策略状态。这直接重用了弱模型的强化学习监督信号，无需训练显式奖励模型或在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励强化学习。实验证明，Direct-OPD能持续利用较弱教师模型提升更强目标模型；值得注意的是，它仅用4小时在8块A100 GPU上将Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上的表现从48.3%提升至62.4%。它优于步长匹配的直接强化学习，并支持多个策略变化的顺序组合。我们的结果表明，强化学习结果可跨模型规模作为隐式奖励信号重用，而不仅限于模仿最终模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of applying reinforcement learning to large language models, this work proposes Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD) to transfer RL gains from a smaller weak teacher model to a larger target model without retraining the target. Direct-OPD uses the log-ratio between the teacher’s policy before and after RL as an implicit reward signal, applied directly to the student’s on-policy states, bypassing the need for explicit reward modeling or sparse-reward RL on the target. Experiments show Direct-OPD effectively boosts performance: Qwen3-1.7B improved from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs, outperforming step-matched direct RL and enabling sequential composition of policy shifts.
为降低在大语言模型上应用强化学习的成本，本文提出直接在线策略蒸馏（Direct-OPD），将小规模弱教师模型的强化学习收益迁移到大规模目标模型上，无需在目标模型上重新运行强化学习。Direct-OPD利用教师模型强化学习前后的策略对数比作为隐式奖励信号，直接应用于学生模型的在线状态。实验表明，该方法显著提升性能：Qwen3-1.7B在AIME 2024上的得分从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅用4小时8张A100显卡完成，优于步长匹配的直接强化学习，并支持多个策略迁移的串行组合。
Interpretable Human-Label-Free Deep Learning for Real-Bogus Classification with Uncertainty Quantification
Authors: Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini, Bruno Sanchez, Dominique Fouchez, Benjamin Racine, Maya Guy, Mariam Sabalbal, Manal Yassine, Vincenzo Piuri
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Astronomy & Astrophysics, revised after first referee report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time-domain surveys generate many transient candidates, making Real-Bogus classification a critical step in automated discovery pipelines. Reliable labels are costly, while community labels can be noisy and survey-dependent. We aim to develop a Real-Bogus classification framework that can be trained without human-labeled data using injected transients and bogus-dominated survey data, remains robust under strong class contamination, and provides calibrated uncertainty quantification. We combine simulated transient injections with a contaminated survey class and train a dual-network model using asymmetric co-teaching for classes with different label-noise levels. We evaluate performance on a benchmark subset and analyze the learned representation with latent-space visualization tools. For uncertainty quantification (UQ), we compare MC dropout and deep ensembles and propose a low-cost hybrid strategy that exploits the dual-network setting to improve calibration. We extend the evaluation to the light-curve domain to assess recovery of light-curve classes. The method achieves strong Real-Bogus performance on the labeled subset and remains stable under severe class contamination. It recovers transient light-curve classes with high fidelity, while single-source identification is limited by ambiguity in light-curve-derived labels. Our hybrid UQ approach achieves competitive calibration relative to more expensive ensemble baselines. Latent-space analyses indicate that uncertainty aligns with the decision boundary and reveal subclasses within the bogus population. Our results show that injection-driven, weakly supervised training can enable scalable and consistent Real-Bogus classification without human-labeled training data while providing calibrated uncertainties. The method is suited for transfer to forthcoming surveys by re-running the injection-based training pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需人工标注的可解释深度学习方法用于真实-虚假分类及不确定性量化
时域巡天产生大量暂现体候选体，真实-虚假分类是自动化发现流程的关键步骤。可靠标注成本高昂，而社区标注存在噪声且依赖巡天数据。我们旨在开发一种无需人工标注数据的实时-虚假分类框架，该框架通过注入暂现体和以虚假体为主导的巡天数据进行训练，在强类别污染下仍保持鲁棒性，并提供校准良好的不确定性量化。我们结合模拟暂现体注入与污染巡天类别，采用不对称协同教学训练双网络模型，以应对不同标签噪声水平的类别。我们在基准子集上评估性能，并使用潜在空间可视化工具分析学习表示。对于不确定性量化（UQ），我们比较了MC Dropout与深度集成方法，并提出一种低成本混合策略，利用双网络结构提升校准效果。我们将评估扩展至光变曲线域，以评估光变曲线类别的恢复能力。该方法在标注子集上实现优异的真实-虚假分类性能，且在严重类别污染下仍保持稳定。它能高保真恢复暂现体光变曲线类别，但单源识别受限于光变曲线衍生标签的模糊性。我们的混合UQ方法在与更昂贵的集成基线相比下仍具竞争力。潜在空间分析表明，不确定性与决策边界一致，并揭示了虚假体群体中的子类。结果表明，基于注入驱动的弱监督训练可在无需人工标注训练数据的情况下实现可扩展且一致的真实-虚假分类，并提供校准良好的不确定性。该方法适用于未来巡天的迁移，只需重新运行基于注入的训练流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable Real-Bogus classification in transient surveys without costly human labels, this work proposes a deep learning framework trained solely on injected transients and contaminated survey data. It employs a dual-network architecture with asymmetric co-teaching to handle varying label noise levels and trains models that provide calibrated uncertainty estimates. Experimental results show strong classification performance on labeled benchmarks, robustness under severe class contamination, and accurate recovery of transient light-curve classes, though single-source identification remains limited by label ambiguity. The proposed hybrid uncertainty quantification method, combining MC dropout and deep ensembles, achieves competitive calibration at lower cost, and latent-space analysis reveals uncertainty patterns aligned with decision boundaries and hidden bogus subclasses.
为解决瞬变源巡天中人工标注成本高、标签噪声大且依赖观测数据的问题，该研究提出一种无需人工标注的弱监督深度学习框架，利用注入的瞬变源和含噪声的巡天数据进行训练。方法采用双网络结构结合不对称协同教学，以应对不同标签噪声水平，同时在强类别污染环境下保持鲁棒性。不确定性量化采用融合MC Dropout与深度集成的低成本混合策略，利用双网络结构提升校准效果。实验表明，该方法在标注子集上分类性能优异，污染环境下仍保持稳定，能高保真恢复瞬变光变曲线类别，但单源识别受限于光变曲线标签的模糊性。潜在空间分析显示不确定性与决策边界一致，并揭示了伪瞬变中的子类结构，证明该方法可迁移至未来巡天项目，无需人工标注数据。
SynCity 3000: Bootstrapping Scene-Scale 3D Diffusion
Authors: Paul Engstler, Iro Laina, Christian Rupprecht, Andrea Vedaldi
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:35+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://research.paulengstler.com/syncity-3k/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SynCity 3000, a framework for generating 3D scenes that are globally coherent while enabling fine-grained layout control. Building on the ability of current image-to-3D generators to produce complex 3D assets from a single image, we extend this capability to the scale of entire scenes by adapting the generator to be applicable as a convolutional operator. We achieve this by fine-tuning the model on scene-like data generated by a new synthetic data engine, which we propose to address the scarcity of 3D scene data for training. The convolutional generator is then applied to a dimetric image of the entire scene, generated from the user prompt, resulting in 3D scenes of arbitrary size and complexity. Across diverse prompts and layouts, SynCity 3000 produces large, coherent, and detailed scenes, addressing the shortcomings of prior approaches to 3D scene generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SynCity 3000：基于场景级3D扩散的自举生成
我们提出了SynCity 3000，一个能够生成全局一致且支持精细布局控制的3D场景的框架。基于当前图像到3D生成器从单张图像生成复杂3D资产的能力，我们将其扩展至整个场景尺度，通过将生成器适配为卷积算子实现。我们通过在由新合成数据引擎生成的类似场景数据上微调模型来实现这一点，以应对3D场景数据训练稀缺的问题。随后，该卷积生成器应用于由用户提示生成的场景双视角图像，从而生成任意大小和复杂度的3D场景。在多样化的提示和布局下，SynCity 3000生成了大型、连贯且细节丰富的场景，弥补了先前3D场景生成方法的不足。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and controllable 3D scene generation, SynCity 3000 adapts image-to-3D diffusion models into convolutional operators to generate globally coherent 3D scenes from user prompts. It overcomes data scarcity by training the model on synthetic scene data produced by a new engine, enabling fine-grained layout control and arbitrary scene size. Experimental results show that the framework successfully generates large, detailed, and structurally consistent 3D scenes across diverse prompts, outperforming prior methods in scalability and coherence.
为解决大规模3D场景生成训练数据稀缺的问题，作者提出SynCity 3000框架，将图像到3D的生成器适配为卷积运算器，从而从用户提示生成全局连贯的3D场景。该方法通过新合成数据引擎生成场景数据对模型进行微调，使其能在整个场景的斜视图上运行。实验表明，SynCity 3000能在多样提示下生成大尺寸、细节丰富且可控制布局的3D场景，显著优于现有方法在可扩展性和连贯性方面的表现。
LLM-as-a-Verifier: A General-Purpose Verification Framework
Authors: Jacky Kwok, Shulu Li, Pranav Atreya, Yuejiang Liu, Yixing Jiang, Chelsea Finn, Marco Pavone, Ion Stoica, Azalia Mirhoseini
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:35+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/llm-as-a-verifier/llm-as-a-verifier Website: https://llm-as-a-verifier.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scaling pre-training, post-training, and test-time compute have become the central paradigms for improving the capabilities of LLMs. In this work, we identify verification, the ability to determine the correctness of a solution, as a new scaling axis. To unlock this and demonstrate its effectiveness, we introduce LLM-as-a-Verifier, a general-purpose verification framework that provides fine-grained feedback for agentic tasks without requiring additional training. Unlike standard LM judges that prompt LLMs to produce discrete scores for candidate solutions, LLM-as-a-Verifier computes the expectation over the distribution of scoring token logits to generate continuous scores. This probabilistic formulation enables verification to scale along multiple dimensions: (1) score granularity, (2) repeated evaluation, and (3) criteria decomposition. In particular, we show that scaling the scoring granularity leads to better separation between positive and negative solutions, resulting in more calibrated comparisons. Moreover, scaling repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition consistently lead to additional gains in verification accuracy through variance and complexity reduction. We further introduce a cost-efficient ranking algorithm for selecting the best solution among candidates using the verifier's continuous scores. LLM-as-a-Verifier achieves state-of-the-art performance on Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). Beyond verification, the fine-grained signals from LLM-as-a-Verifier can also serve as a proxy for estimating task progress. We build an extension for Claude Code, enabling developers to monitor and improve their own agentic systems. Finally, we show that LLM-as-a-Verifier can provide dense feedback for RL, improving the sample efficiency of SAC and GRPO on robotics and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLM作为验证器：一种通用验证框架
预训练、后训练和测试时计算的扩展已成为提升大语言模型能力的核心范式。本研究将验证能力——即判断解决方案正确性的能力——识别为新的扩展维度。为释放该能力并证明其有效性，我们提出了LLM-as-a-Verifier，一种无需额外训练即可为代理任务提供细粒度反馈的通用验证框架。与标准的LM评分器不同，该框架通过计算评分标记logits分布的期望值生成连续评分，而非要求LLM输出离散分数。这种概率性建模使验证可沿多个维度扩展：（1）评分粒度，（2）重复评估，（3）标准分解。特别是，扩展评分粒度可增强正负解的区分度，从而实现更校准的比较。此外，重复评估和标准分解的扩展通过降低方差和复杂度，持续提升验证准确性。我们进一步提出了一种成本高效的排序算法，用于基于验证器的连续评分在候选方案中选择最优解。LLM-as-a-Verifier在Terminal-Bench V2（86.5%）、SWE-Bench Verified（78.2%）、RoboRewardBench（87.4%）和MedAgentBench（73.3%）上达到当前最优性能。除验证外，LLM-as-a-Verifier的细粒度信号还可作为估算任务进度的代理指标。我们为Claude Code构建了扩展功能，使开发者能够监控并改进自身的代理系统。最后，我们证明LLM-as-a-Verifier可为强化学习提供密集反馈，提升SAC和GRPO在机器人和数学推理基准上的样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve LLM performance through scalable verification, this work introduces LLM-as-a-Verifier, a framework that generates continuous scores for candidate solutions without additional training. Instead of discrete judgments, it computes scores by averaging over scoring token logits, enabling fine-grained, repeatable, and decomposable evaluation. Experiments show that increasing score granularity improves solution separation, while repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition reduce variance and boost accuracy. The framework achieves top results on multiple benchmarks including Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%) and RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and also enhances RL sample efficiency in robotics and math tasks by providing dense feedback.
为提升大语言模型能力，本研究提出LLM-as-a-Verifier框架，通过无需额外训练即可生成连续概率评分，实现对候选方案的细粒度验证。与传统离散评分方法不同，该框架基于评分token的logits分布计算期望值，支持评分粒度细化、重复评估与标准分解，从而提升验证准确性。实验表明，评分粒度细化可增强正负解分离与校准，重复评估与标准分解则降低方差并持续提升性能。该框架在多个基准上达到最优表现，包括Terminal-Bench V2（86.5%）、SWE-Bench Verified（78.2%）和RoboRewardBench（87.4%），并支持低成本排序、任务进度代理评估，可扩展至Claude Code及强化学习样本效率优化。
Deform360: A Massive Multi-view Visuotactile Dataset for Deformable World Models
Authors: Hongyu Li, Wanjia Fu, Xiaoyan Cong, Zekun Li, Binghao Huang, Hanxiao Jiang, Xintong He, Yiqing Liang, Rao Fu, Tao Lu, Srinath Sridhar, Kevin A. Smith, George Konidaris, Yunzhu Li
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:59:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting object dynamics (i.e., world modeling) is a fundamental challenge for robotic manipulation, and modeling deformable objects presents a particularly difficult case due to their high-dimensional state spaces and complex material properties. While current world models approach this through two distinct paradigms: learning the dynamics over the 2D pixel space or more explicit 3D geometric space. A systematic understanding of their relative strengths and limitations remains elusive due to the lack of diverse, large-scale real-world data. To address this, we present Deform360, a large-scale visuotactile dataset featuring 198 daily-life objects, 1,980 interaction sequences, and over 215 hours of observations from 41 surround-view cameras and bimanual tactile grippers to capture both global motion and contact-induced local deformations. Leveraging a novel markerless visuotactile 3D tracking pipeline to extract dense geometry and motion, we systematically evaluate current state-of-the-art world models, comparing 2D video models against 3D particle models. Finally, we provide a preliminary demonstration indicating the real-world applicability of our dataset by performing robot planning tasks on deformable objects. Our analysis reveals key insights into the trade-offs between structural priors and scalability, providing a solid benchmark for future research in generalizable deformable object-centric world modeling. Project website: https://deform360.lhy.xyz

预测物体动力学（即世界建模）是机器人操作的基本挑战，而建模可变形物体尤为困难，因其高维状态空间和复杂材料特性。当前世界模型主要通过两种范式处理：学习2D像素空间或更明确的3D几何空间上的动力学。由于缺乏多样化的大型真实世界数据，对其相对优劣和局限性的系统理解仍不充分。为此，我们提出Deform360，一个大规模视觉触觉数据集，包含198个日常物体、1980个交互序列，以及来自41个环视摄像头和双臂触觉夹持器的超过215小时观测数据，以捕捉全局运动和接触引起的局部变形。我们利用新颖的无标记视觉触觉3D跟踪流水线提取密集几何与运动信息，系统评估当前最先进的世界模型，对比2D视频模型与3D粒子模型。最后，我们通过在可变形物体上执行机器人规划任务，初步展示了该数据集在现实世界中的应用潜力。分析揭示了结构先验与可扩展性之间的权衡，为未来可泛化可变形物体中心世界建模研究提供了坚实基准。项目网站：https://deform360.lhy.xyz

To advance robotic manipulation of deformable objects, the authors introduce Deform360, a large-scale visuotactile dataset capturing 198 everyday objects across 1,980 interaction sequences and over 215 hours of multi-view visual and tactile data. 为提升机器人对可变形物体的操控能力，研究者构建了Deform360大规模视觉触觉数据集，涵盖198种日常物品、1980个交互序列，通过41个环绕摄像头与双臂触觉传感器同步捕捉全局运动与局部形变，采用无标记3D跟踪管道提取稠密几何与运动信息。评估现有世界模型发现，2D像素模型在处理复杂形变时表现不足，而3D粒子模型虽结构更优但存在可扩展性瓶颈。该数据集支持真实机器人规划任务，揭示结构先验与可扩展性间的权衡，为未来可变形物体中心世界建模研究提供基准。
InFlux++: Real and Synthetic Data for Estimating Dynamic Camera Intrinsics
Authors: Erich Liang, Caleb Kha-Uong, Chinmaya Saran, Sreemanti Dey, David W. Liu, Junhan Ouyang, Benjamin Zhou, Jia Deng
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:58:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Camera intrinsics are vital for recovering 3D structure from 2D video. However, most 3D algorithms assume fixed intrinsics throughout a video, an assumption that often fails for real-world in-the-wild videos. Consequently, estimating per-frame intrinsics from RGB images is critical for making 3D methods robust to videos with dynamic intrinsics. InFlux previously advanced this research direction by establishing the first real-world benchmark with per-frame ground truth intrinsics for dynamic intrinsics videos. Nevertheless, existing methods remain inaccurate due to two obstacles: (i) training data is scarce and lacks intrinsics diversity; and (ii) benchmarks, including InFlux, have limited scene and camera motion diversity, making it difficult to properly evaluate methods. To address both gaps, we present InFlux++, consisting of two components. InFlux++ Synth is a large-scale procedurally generated synthetic video dataset with 441K+ annotated frames from 1841 high-resolution videos, providing accurate per-frame ground truth intrinsics for training dynamic intrinsics prediction models; a subset also includes per-frame pose, depth, and normals. The videos feature rich intrinsics diversity through changes in camera zoom and focus, as well as dynamic objects and realistic rendering effects such as lens distortion and defocus blur. InFlux++ Real is a large-scale real-world benchmark that extends InFlux with 514K+ newly captured frames across 334 high-resolution videos, spanning a wider range of scenes and camera motions. Finetuning existing intrinsics prediction methods on InFlux++ Synth consistently improves focal length estimation across both InFlux++ Real and InFlux, suggesting that synthetic supervision is promising for RGB-based intrinsics prediction. For the dataset, benchmark, code, videos, submission instructions, and live leaderboard, please visit https://influx.cs.princeton.edu/ .

标题：InFlux++：用于估计动态相机内参的真实与合成数据
相机内参对从2D视频恢复3D结构至关重要。然而，大多数3D算法假设视频中内参恒定，这一假设在真实野外视频中常不成立。因此，从RGB图像中估计每帧内参对使3D方法适应动态内参视频至关重要。InFlux此前通过建立首个具有每帧真实内参标注的现实世界基准，推动了该研究方向。但现有方法仍不准确，主要受限于两点：(i) 训练数据稀缺且缺乏内参多样性；(ii) 基准（包括InFlux）场景与相机运动多样性有限，难以充分评估方法。为解决上述问题，我们提出InFlux++，包含两个部分。InFlux++ Synth是一个大规模程序化生成的合成视频数据集，包含1841个高分辨率视频的44.1万+标注帧，提供精确的每帧真实内参用于训练动态内参预测模型；子集还包含每帧姿态、深度和法线。视频通过改变相机变焦与对焦、动态物体及镜头畸变、失焦模糊等真实渲染效果，实现丰富的内参多样性。InFlux++ Real是一个大规模真实世界基准，扩展了InFlux，包含334个高分辨率视频的51.4万+新采集帧，覆盖更广泛的场景与相机运动。在InFlux++ Synth上微调现有内参预测方法，可显著提升焦距估计精度，无论在InFlux++ Real还是InFlux上均表现优异，表明合成监督对RGB内参预测具有潜力。数据集、基准、代码、视频、提交指南及实时排行榜请访问 https://influx.cs.princeton.edu/。

Motivated by the need to improve 3D reconstruction from videos with changing camera intrinsics, this work introduces InFlux++, a dual-component dataset addressing data scarcity and evaluation limitations. InFlux++ Synth generates 441K+ annotated frames across 1841 videos with diverse intrinsics, including zoom, focus, lens distortion, and defocus blur, enabling robust training of dynamic intrinsics models. InFlux++ Real expands the real-world benchmark with 514K+ frames from 334 videos, covering broader scenes and motions. 为提升动态相机内参视频的三维重建效果，本文提出InFlux++，包含合成数据与真实数据两部分，解决训练数据稀缺与评估范围有限的问题。InFlux++ Synth提供1841个高分辨率视频的44万+带标注帧，涵盖变焦、对焦、镜头畸变与失焦模糊等丰富内参变化，支持动态内参预测模型训练；InFlux++ Real则在原InFlux基础上新增334个视频的51万+真实帧，覆盖更广的场景与运动类型。实验表明，基于InFlux++ Synth微调现有方法，在InFlux++ Real与原InFlux上均显著提升焦距估计精度，证明合成数据对RGB内参预测的有效性。
Finite-Time Queue Peak Laws in Stochastic Networks: Logarithmic Scaling After Geometric Thresholds
Authors: Hao Liang, Cheng Tang, Yunzong Xu
First: 2026-06-16T17:47:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:57:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study finite-horizon queue peaks in generalized switches, a standard stochastic-network model in which many queues share constrained service resources. Arrivals may be dependent, nonstationary, and responsive to the system history; the only load condition is uniform interior slack, meaning the conditional mean arrival vector stays in a fixed contraction of the capacity region. We show that this slack reshapes the finite-time peak law for drift-minimizing scheduling policies such as MaxWeight. The square-root envelope that is sharp without slack persists only up to a geometry-dependent threshold; beyond that threshold, the running maximum grows only logarithmically with the horizon, both with high probability and in expectation. The mechanism is self-normalization: in the current queue direction, the projected fluctuation scale is normalized by the stabilizing drift scale. This removes capacity geometry from the logarithmic coefficient, while geometry remains in the threshold. Matching lower bounds show that both the logarithmic term and a geometric threshold are unavoidable. When finite-time state-space collapse is available, the threshold can be sharpened using local bottleneck geometry. For generalized input-queued switches, we obtain finite-time peak bounds with tight logarithmic coefficients. Simulations illustrate the two-phase envelope, local geometric refinements, and variance-sensitive improvements predicted by the theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机网络中有限时间队列峰值定律：几何阈值后的对数缩放
我们研究广义交换机中的有限时间队列峰值，这是一种标准的随机网络模型，其中多个队列共享受限的服务资源。到达可能具有依赖性、非平稳性，并响应系统历史；唯一的负载条件是均匀内部余量，即条件到达向量的均值保持在容量区域的一个固定收缩内。我们证明，该余量重塑了如MaxWeight等最小漂移调度策略的有限时间峰值定律。在无余量情况下尖锐的平方根包络仅在几何依赖阈值内持续；超过该阈值，运行最大值以对数形式随时间增长，且以高概率和期望值均成立。 该机制为自归一化：在当前队列方向上，投影波动尺度由稳定漂移尺度归一化。这消除了对数系数中的容量几何结构，而几何结构仍保留在阈值中。匹配的下界表明，对数项和几何阈值均不可避免。当有限时间状态空间坍缩可用时，可利用局部瓶颈几何结构进一步细化阈值。对于广义输入队列交换机，我们获得了具有紧致对数系数的有限时间峰值界。仿真展示了理论预测的两阶段包络、局部几何细化及方差敏感性改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding queue peak behavior in stochastic networks under limited resources, this study analyzes finite-time queue maxima in generalized switches using drift-minimizing policies like MaxWeight. It reveals that under uniform interior slack—a condition where arrival rates stay within a contraction of the capacity region—the peak growth follows a two-phase pattern: initially square-root scaling up to a geometry-dependent threshold, then logarithmic growth beyond it. The mechanism involves self-normalization, where queue fluctuations are scaled relative to stabilizing drift, eliminating geometry from the logarithmic coefficient while preserving it in the threshold. Experimental results confirm this two-phase envelope, demonstrate geometric refinements via local bottlenecks, and validate variance-sensitive improvements in peak bounds for input-queued switches.
为理解受限资源下随机网络中有限时间队列峰值行为，该研究分析了满足均匀内部余量条件的广义交换机模型，其中到达过程可具有依赖性和非平稳性。研究发现，如MaxWeight等漂移最小化调度策略表现出双阶段峰值包络：在几何依赖阈值前呈平方根增长，超过阈值后转为对数增长，且在期望值和高概率下均成立。该转变机制源于自归一化——队列波动被稳定漂移尺度归一化，从而消除对数系数中的几何影响，而几何结构仍保留在阈值中。理论下界证明了对数项与几何阈值的必要性，仿真结果验证了双阶段包络、局部几何细化及方差敏感性改进。
Search Beyond What Can Be Taught: Evolving the Knowledge Boundary in Agentic Visual Generation
Authors: Haozhe Wang, Weijia Feng, Jinpeng Yu, Che Liu, Ping Nie, Fangzhen Lin, Jiaming Liu, Ruihua Huang, Jimmy Lin, Wenhu Chen, Cong Wei
First: 2026-07-06T17:56:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:56:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual generators excel at rendering, but they confidently fabricate what they do not know. User requests are unbounded, evolving, and deeply long-tailed: new characters, trending entities, post-cutoff events, and more. This world-knowledge bottleneck is structural: generators are trained on fixed corpora, but the visual world is open-ended. We construct SearchGen-20K and SearchGen-Bench, with 20,839 prompts spanning twelve failure categories and twenty-two domains, paired with a pre-executed multimodal SearchGen-Corpus-1M to support offline, reproducible research. On SearchGen-Bench, frontier open generators score only 21 to 28 out of 100, a 40-point collapse invisible to existing benchmarks. The natural remedy is to employ search tools, enabling agentic visual generation. However, we find that naive search fails: it retrieves indiscriminately, injecting noise into prompts the generator already handles. We trace the root cause to a generator-specific, evolving knowledge boundary: the divide between what a generator can internalize through training and what must remain in external context. Although this boundary is hard to specify in advance, we show that it is discoverable through a teach-then-search co-training framework. Even a minimal version of this co-training recipe produces monotonic improvement, laying the foundation for recursive self-improvement in visual generation that can meet world-knowledge-grounded requests. We release the full dataset, co-training corpus, and search corpus as a replayable harness for tool-augmented, world-knowledge-grounded visual generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越可教授知识的边界：推动代理视觉生成的知识边界演进
视觉生成器擅长渲染，但对未知内容会自信地虚构。用户请求无界、动态且长尾效应显著：新角色、热门实体、截断后事件等。这一世界知识瓶颈具有结构性：生成器基于固定语料训练，而视觉世界是开放的。我们构建了SearchGen-20K和SearchGen-Bench，包含20,839个覆盖十二类失败场景与二十二个领域的提示，搭配预执行的多模态SearchGen-Corpus-1M，支持离线可复现研究。在SearchGen-Bench上，前沿开放生成器得分仅21至28分（满分100），比现有基准低40分。自然解决方案是引入搜索工具，实现代理视觉生成。但发现朴素搜索失效：它无差别检索，向生成器已处理的提示注入噪声。我们追溯根源至生成器特有的、动态演进的知识边界：即生成器通过训练可内化的知识与必须依赖外部上下文的知识之间的分界。尽管该边界难以预先明确，我们证明可通过“先教学后搜索”的协同训练框架发现。即使最简版本的协同训练方案也能实现单调提升，为视觉生成的递归自我改进奠定基础，使其能应对世界知识锚定的请求。我们发布完整数据集、协同训练语料与搜索语料，作为工具增强、世界知识锚定视觉生成的可复现框架。

Motivated by the structural limitation that visual generators fabricate unknown content despite user requests being unbounded and long-tailed, the paper introduces SearchGen-20K and SearchGen-Bench to evaluate agentic visual generation under real-world knowledge gaps. It proposes a teach-then-search co-training framework to bridge the generator’s internal knowledge boundary with external world knowledge, avoiding noise from naive search. Experimental results show that even a minimal version of this framework yields monotonic improvement, with frontier models scoring only 21–28/100 on the benchmark, highlighting the need for search-augmented, knowledge-grounded generation.
为解决视觉生成模型在面对开放世界知识时因训练数据固定而产生虚假内容的问题，该研究构建了SearchGen-20K和SearchGen-Bench数据集，涵盖十二类失败场景与二十二个领域。研究提出“先教后搜”的协同训练框架，通过先训练模型再结合外部搜索结果，识别并适应模型自身的知识边界。实验表明，直接搜索会引入噪声导致性能下降，而协同训练能持续提升模型表现，使其更可靠地处理现实世界知识驱动的视觉生成任务。
What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?
Authors: Rodrigo Casado Noguerales, Bernhard Schölkopf, Thomas Hofmann, Aran Raoufi
First: 2026-07-06T17:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:56:11+00:00
Comments: 66 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离散扩散模型学习什么？
离散扩散模型学习的是去噪器、得分比还是桥接插值预测器？在跳跃率层面，这些本质上是同一对象在不同坐标系下的表达，若在错误坐标系下解读神经网络，将改变所训练和采样的过程。我们从任意加噪过程（含边界项）的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）ELBO的严格推导出发，证明了“Oracle Distance”定理：负ELBO精确等于数据熵加上从理想反向过程到学习到过程的路径KL散度，而不仅是上界。其唯一最优解即为当前噪声状态下真实反向跳跃率的条件期望，其不可约成本即为前向过程$Z_t$对原始数据$Z_0$信息的破坏速率$-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$，因此每个加噪过程共享相同的最佳负ELBO：数据熵。对于具有令牌因子化噪声的序列，理想投影给出三个精确坐标：去噪器、空腔（桥接插值）和得分，它们之间存在闭式转换。该框架明确了文献中每种损失函数实际优化的分布律，恢复了MDM、UDM、SEDD和GIDD作为特例；解释了为何在掩码扩散中去噪器与空腔重合，而在均匀扩散中不重合；证明了去噪器参数化会导致均匀ELBO在初始化时发散，而桥接插值保持有限；并在初始化时精确校准ELBO实现。所有恒等式均在可精确求解的模型上经数值验证，无近似。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates what discrete diffusion models actually learn by analyzing them through the lens of continuous-time Markov chain ELBO, revealing that different training objectives—denoiser, score ratio, or bridge plug-in—are merely different coordinate representations of the same underlying optimization problem. The authors derive an exact 'Oracle Distance' theorem showing that the negative ELBO equals data entropy plus a path KL divergence from the true reverse process to the learned one, with its unique optimizer being the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate. For token-factorizing noise, they derive three exact coordinate systems (denoiser, cavity, score) with closed-form conversions, and show how existing models like MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD are special cases. They also explain why denoiser and cavity coincide in masked diffusion but not uniform diffusion, prove that denoiser parameterizations cause uniform ELBO divergence at initialization while bridge plug-in remains finite, and provide exact ELBO calibration at initialization—all verified numerically on an exactly solvable model.
该论文通过连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）变分推断视角，推导出包含边界项的ELBO公式，证明了Oracle Distance定理：负ELBO等于数据熵加上真实逆过程与学习过程之间的路径KL散度，其最优解为当前噪声状态下的真实逆跳跃率的条件期望。对于token因子化噪声，最优解可分解为三种精确形式——去噪器、空腔（桥接插值）和分数，且它们之间存在闭式转换。该框架阐明了现有模型如MDM、UDM、SEDD和GIDD均为特例，解释了为何在掩码扩散中去噪器与空腔一致而在均匀扩散中不一致，证明了去噪器参数化会导致均匀ELBO在初始化时发散而桥接插值保持有限，并在可精确求解的模型上数值验证了所有恒等式，无需近似。
TabPack: Efficient Hyperparameter Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning
Authors: Yury Gorishniy, Akim Kotelnikov, Ivan Rubachev, Artem Babenko
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:55:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:55:55+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Code: https://github.com/yandex-research/tabpack
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In deep learning for tabular data, efficient ensembles of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) have recently emerged as effective and practical architectures. Existing methods of this kind use the same hyperparameters for all underlying MLPs, which requires hyperparameter tuning for achieving the best performance. In this work, we introduce TabPack, an efficient MLP ensemble with strong out-of-the-box performance and reduced reliance on traditional tuning. In a single run, TabPack samples and trains many MLPs with different hyperparameters efficiently in parallel and selects ensemble members on the fly during training. Thus, TabPack only requires specifying ranges from which to sample MLP hyperparameter rather than exact hyperparameter values, which naturally demands less precision for good performance. In experiments on medium-to-large public datasets, TabPack with default settings performs on par with extensively tuned prior methods, thus substantially reducing effort and compute resources needed to achieve competitive results on tabular tasks. Notably, running the default TabPack configuration on a modern MacBook took less time than tuning some baselines on an industry-grade GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TabPack：面向表格数据深度学习的高效超参数集成
在表格数据的深度学习中，多层感知机（MLP）的高效集成架构近期已成为有效且实用的方案。现有方法通常为所有底层MLP使用相同的超参数，这要求进行超参数调优以获得最佳性能。本文提出TabPack，一种具有强大开箱即用性能且减少对传统调优依赖的高效MLP集成方法。TabPack在单次运行中可并行采样和训练多个具有不同超参数的MLP，并在训练过程中动态选择集成成员。因此，TabPack仅需指定超参数的采样范围而非精确值，从而自然降低对高精度的需求。在中等至大型公开数据集上的实验表明，TabPack默认设置的性能可媲美经过广泛调优的传统方法，从而大幅减少实现表格任务竞争力结果所需的努力与计算资源。值得注意的是，在现代MacBook上运行默认TabPack配置所用时间，甚至少于在工业级GPU上调优某些基线方法所需时间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional hyperparameter tuning for tabular deep learning ensembles, TabPack introduces a method that samples and trains multiple MLPs in parallel with varying hyperparameters during training, selecting ensemble members dynamically without requiring exact hyperparameter values. This approach reduces reliance on manual tuning while maintaining strong performance. Experiments on medium-to-large public datasets show that TabPack with default settings matches or exceeds the performance of extensively tuned baselines, significantly cutting down computational cost and tuning effort—demonstrated by its ability to complete training on a MacBook faster than tuning some baselines on high-end GPUs.
为解决传统超参数调优在表格数据深度学习集成中的低效问题，TabPack提出一种新方法：在训练过程中并行采样不同超参数训练多个MLP，并动态选择集成成员。该方法无需精确指定超参数，大幅降低调优依赖，同时保持高性能。实验表明，TabPack默认配置在中大型数据集上表现可媲美经过充分调优的基线方法，所需计算资源和时间显著减少——甚至在MacBook上完成训练的时间比在高端GPU上调优某些基线方法更短。
Starve to Perceive: Taming Lazy Perception in VLMs with Constrained Visual Bandwidth
Authors: Yuhuan Wu, Cong Wei, Fangzhen Lin, Wenhu Chen, Haozhe Wang
First: 2026-05-18T16:13:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:55:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) deployed as situated agents in high-resolution visual environments require active perception -- the ability to dynamically decide where to look through operations like zooming, cropping, and panning. However, current training paradigms produce models that mimic the surface form of such operations without functionally depending on their outputs, a phenomenon we term lazy perception. We trace this to a fundamental learning asymmetry: when coarse global views combined with language priors suffice for moderate accuracy, the model has no incentive to learn harder multi-step visual search. If a model can succeed without actively looking, it will never learn to look. This motivates Starve to Perceive, a training paradigm that constrains visual bandwidth -- restricting each observation to a tight token budget so that no single view suffices for task completion, making active perception the only viable strategy. Despite requiring no auxiliary losses, reward shaping, or architectural changes -- serving as a minimal, plug-in modification to standard post-training pipelines -- models trained under perceptual starvation achieve substantial gains of 5% average relative improvement across diverse benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：饥饿感知：通过受限视觉带宽驯化VLM中的懒惰感知
部署在高分辨率视觉环境中的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）需要主动感知——即通过缩放、裁剪和平移等操作动态决定观察位置的能力。然而，当前训练范式生成的模型仅模仿此类操作的表面形式，而未在功能上依赖其输出，我们称之为“懒惰感知”。我们将其归因于一种根本性的学习不对称性：当粗略的全局视图结合语言先验已足以达到中等精度时，模型没有动力学习更复杂的多步视觉搜索。如果模型无需主动观察即可成功，它就永远不会学会主动观察。这促使我们提出“饥饿感知”训练范式，通过限制视觉带宽——将每次观察限制在紧缩的token预算内，使得单个视图不足以完成任务，从而迫使模型必须采用主动感知作为唯一可行策略。尽管无需辅助损失、奖励塑造或架构修改——作为对标准后训练流程的最小插件式修改——在感知饥饿条件下训练的模型在多个基准测试中平均相对性能提升达5%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that current VLMs exhibit 'lazy perception'—relying on coarse views without dynamically seeking better visual evidence—the paper proposes 'Starve to Perceive,' a training method that constrains visual bandwidth by limiting each observation to a tight token budget, forcing models to actively search for relevant visual information. This approach, requiring no architectural changes or auxiliary losses, disrupts the learning asymmetry where models avoid effortful perception when surface-level views suffice. Experimental results show that models trained under this constraint achieve a 5% average relative improvement across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that perceptual starvation effectively promotes functional active perception.
为解决当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）存在‘懒惰感知’的问题——即依赖粗略全局视图而缺乏动态视觉注意力调整能力——本文提出‘饥饿感知’训练方法，通过限制每帧观察的视觉令牌预算，迫使模型必须主动搜索相关信息。该方法无需额外损失函数、奖励塑造或架构修改，仅作为标准微调流程的最小插件式改动即可生效。实验表明，在多个基准测试中，该方法训练出的模型平均相对性能提升达5%，且无需增加模型复杂度。
CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents
Authors: Yujiang Li, Zhenyu Hou, Yi Jing, Jie Tang, Yuxiao Dong
First: 2026-07-06T17:55:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:55:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).
中文标题/摘要
标题：CompactionRL：面向长时序代理的上下文压缩强化学习
长时序代理大语言模型日益受限于有限的上下文窗口，因扩展交互轨迹常在任务完成前超出最大上下文长度。上下文压缩通过总结先前交互状态并在压缩上下文中继续执行，提供自然解决方案，但将其融入强化学习仍属探索阶段。我们提出CompactionRL，一种用于训练长时序代理大语言模型的强化学习策略。该方法联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，采用词级损失归一化与跨轨迹广义优势估计。此设计使大语言模型代理能从压缩的长时序轨迹中学习。我们在开源模型上训练CompactionRL，在代理编码任务中观察到持续性能提升。CompactionRL使开源GLM-4.5-Air模型（106B-A30B）在SWE-bench Verified上达到66.8%的Pass@1，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到24.5%，分别提升7.0和3.1个百分点。基于GLM-4.7-Flash（30B-A3B），CompactionRL使Pass@1分别提升5.5和6.8个百分点，达到SWE-bench Verified 56.0%和Terminal-Bench 2.0 20.2%。CompactionRL已应用于RL训练流程，用于训练开源GLM-5.2模型（750B-A40B）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of finite context windows in long-horizon agentic LLMs, the paper introduces CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning framework that integrates context compaction to summarize interaction histories and enable continued rollout under compressed contexts. The method jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation using token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation, allowing agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. Experiments show consistent performance gains: CompactionRL boosts Pass@1 scores by 7.0 points (to 66.8%) on SWE-bench Verified and 3.1 points (to 24.5%) on Terminal-Bench 2.0 for GLM-4.5-Air, and by 5.5 and 6.8 points for GLM-4.7-Flash, reaching 56.0% and 20.2% respectively, with CompactionRL subsequently deployed in training GLM-5.2.
为解决长时序代理大语言模型受上下文窗口限制的问题，CompactionRL提出一种结合上下文压缩的强化学习框架，通过联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，采用词级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计。该方法使模型能从压缩的长时序轨迹中有效学习。实验表明：在GLM-4.5-Air（106B-A30B）上，Pass@1得分分别提升至SWE-bench Verified 66.8%（+7.0）和Terminal-Bench 2.0 24.5%（+3.1）；在GLM-4.7-Flash（30B-A3B）上，分别提升至56.0%（+5.5）和20.2%（+6.8）；该方法已应用于训练更大规模的GLM-5.2模型（750B-A40B）。
Cortex: A Bidirectionally Aligned Embodied Agent Framework for Long-horizon Manipulation
Authors: Jiaqi Peng, Xiqian Yu, Delin Feng, Yuqiang Yang, Wenzhe Cai, Jing Xiong, Ganlin Yang, Jinliang Zheng, Jiafei Cao, Xueyuan Wei, Jiangmiao Pang, Yuan Shen, Tai Wang
First: 2026-07-06T17:55:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:55:05+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://steinate.github.io/cortex.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise toward generalist manipulation policies, they struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their Markovian nature-relying solely on current observations. Hierarchical dual-system methods address this but suffer from a gap between high-level planning semantics and low-level execution kinematics. We introduce Cortex, a bidirectionally aligned embodied agent framework with a customized planning interface that conveys executable and tractable subtask plans from high-level VLM to low-level VLA. Specifically, we standardize manipulation subtasks into 32 canonical skill primitives and inject tractability principles, such as representative object attributes and improved trajectory reachability, into the data generation pipeline. This enables automatic annotation of over 4k hours of open-source video data and generation of 30 hours of simulation data. We further devise an event-balanced sampling strategy to construct training data for fine-tuning the framework to better handle planning ambiguity during subtask transitions, enhanced by carefully designed harness engineering from task contexts to skill constraints during inference. Both open-loop VLM and closed-loop system evaluations demonstrate Cortex's efficacy, e.g., it outperforms monolithic baselines by 3.1% on Libero-long and 4.1% on RoboTwin. Notably, Cortex's generalist VLM enables zero-shot completion of unseen real-world long-horizon tasks, such as multi-stage chemistry experiments, by simply combining with a fine-tuned VLA-a capability infeasible through VLA fine-tuning alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cortex：面向长时程操作的双向对齐具身智能体框架
尽管近期视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用操作策略方面展现出潜力，但其马尔可夫性质（仅依赖当前观测）使其难以应对长时程任务。分层双系统方法虽可缓解此问题，却存在高层规划语义与底层执行运动学之间的鸿沟。我们提出Cortex框架，其具备定制化规划接口，可将高层视觉语言模型（VLM）生成的可执行且可处理的子任务计划传递至底层VLA。具体而言，我们将操作子任务标准化为32个典型技能原语，并在数据生成流程中注入可处理性原则，如代表性物体属性与改进的轨迹可达性，从而实现对4000多小时开源视频数据的自动标注及30小时仿真数据的生成。我们进一步设计事件平衡采样策略，构建训练数据以优化框架在子任务转换时处理规划模糊性的能力，并通过精心设计的任务上下文到技能约束的推理工程加以增强。开放环路VLM与闭环系统评估均验证了Cortex的有效性，例如在Libero-long上优于单体基线3.1%，在RoboTwin上提升4.1%。值得注意的是，Cortex的通用VLM可结合微调后的VLA，实现零样本完成未见过的真实长时程任务（如多阶段化学实验），而仅靠VLA微调无法实现此能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Markovian Vision-Language-Action models in handling long-horizon manipulation tasks, Cortex introduces a bidirectionally aligned framework that bridges high-level planning semantics from vision-language models with low-level execution kinematics via a customized planning interface. It standardizes manipulation into 32 canonical skill primitives and injects tractability principles into data generation, enabling annotation of over 4,000 hours of video and synthesis of 30 hours of simulation data. An event-balanced sampling strategy and context-aware harness engineering enhance subtask transition robustness. Experimental results show Cortex outperforms monolithic baselines by 3.1% on Libero-long and 4.1% on RoboTwin, and enables zero-shot completion of unseen real-world tasks like multi-stage chemistry experiments through its generalist VLM combined with fine-tuned VLA.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型在长时程操作任务中的局限性，Cortex提出一种双向对齐的具身智能框架，通过定制化规划接口将高层视觉语言模型的语义规划与底层动作执行衔接。该框架将操作标准化为32个基础技能原语，并在数据生成中引入可执行性原则如物体属性与轨迹可达性，实现对4000小时开源视频的自动标注及30小时仿真数据生成。结合事件平衡采样策略与任务上下文驱动的约束工程，有效缓解子任务切换时的规划模糊问题。实验表明，Cortex在Libero-long和RoboTwin上分别优于单体基线3.1%和4.1%，并能通过通用视觉语言模型与微调动作模型的组合，零样本完成如多阶段化学实验等未见过的真实长时任务，这是仅靠动作模型微调无法实现的。
Causal Mechanism Reduction: Mechanism Replacement for Neural Network Pruning and Abstraction
Authors: Amir Asiaee
First: 2026-02-27T18:35:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:54:33+00:00
Comments: Causal abstraction and pruning have been combined in this version under the name of "reduction"
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Which internal mechanisms of a neural network can be replaced while preserving the computation it performs? Structured pruning asks for smaller deployable networks; causal abstraction asks for high-level models that commute with interventions. We introduce causal mechanism reduction (CMR), a framework that treats a trained network as a deterministic structural causal model and replaces selected internal variables by constants or affine functions of retained variables. These replacements compile exactly into smaller dense networks by bias and weight folding, and induce reduced causal models testable with interchange interventions. We derive a unified second-order replacement-risk objective whose special cases recover mean replacement, variance-based pruning (VBP), logit-distortion scoring, and affine neuron merging, together with a margin-based certificate linking logit distortion to interchange-intervention agreement. The framework also exposes a basic invariance requirement: functionally identical ReLU networks should induce the same reduction. Under exact positive-scaling reparameterizations, VBP's kept set collapses to chance-level overlap while the logit-distortion score is exactly invariant. Empirically, CMR variants are competitive with VBP under matched fine-tuning of DeiT-Tiny on ImageNet-100; the clearer separation appears in the invariance and interchange tests, where the logit-distortion score preserves kept sets and consistently improves distributional fidelity. CMR thus gives pruning, compilation, and causal-abstraction verification a common object to optimize and verify.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果机制约简：神经网络剪枝与抽象的机制替换
在保持神经网络计算功能不变的前提下，哪些内部机制可以被替换？结构化剪枝寻求更小的可部署网络；因果抽象寻求与干预兼容的高层模型。我们引入因果机制约简（CMR）框架，将训练好的网络视为确定性结构因果模型，并用常量或保留变量的仿射函数替换选定的内部变量。这些替换通过偏置与权重折叠精确编译为更小的稠密网络，并诱导可经交换干预检验的简化因果模型。我们推导出一个统一的二阶替换风险目标函数，其特例涵盖均值替换、基于方差的剪枝（VBP）、logit失真评分及仿射神经元合并，同时辅以基于边界的证书，将logit失真与交换干预一致性关联起来。该框架还揭示了一个基本不变性要求：功能等价的ReLU网络应诱导相同的约简。在精确正比例重参数化下，VBP保留集退化为随机水平重叠，而logit失真评分完全不变。实证上，CMR变体在ImageNet-100上对DeiT-Tiny进行匹配微调时与VBP表现相当；在不变性与交换测试中，logit失真评分保留集更清晰，且持续提升分布保真度。CMR因此为剪枝、编译与因果抽象验证提供了一个共同的优化与验证目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to prune neural networks while preserving their computational behavior and enabling causal abstraction, this paper introduces causal mechanism reduction (CMR), a framework that treats trained networks as structural causal models and replaces internal variables with constants or affine functions of retained variables. These replacements compile into smaller dense networks via weight and bias folding, and yield reduced causal models testable under interchange interventions. The method uses a unified second-order risk objective that generalizes several existing pruning techniques, including variance-based pruning and logit-distortion scoring, and includes a margin-based certificate linking logit distortion to intervention agreement. Experiments show CMR variants perform competitively with VBP on DeiT-Tiny on ImageNet-100, with notable improvements in invariance and distributional fidelity under interchange tests, where logit-distortion scores maintain consistent kept sets and better preserve functional equivalence.
为在保持功能的前提下剪枝神经网络并实现因果抽象，本文提出因果机制缩减（CMR）框架，将训练好的网络视为结构因果模型，用常量或保留变量的仿射函数替换内部变量。这些替换通过权重和偏置折叠编译为更小的稠密网络，并生成可经干预测试的简化因果模型。该框架统一了多种剪枝方法于一个二阶风险目标下，并引入基于边界的证书将对数损失与干预一致性关联。实验表明，CMR变体在DeiT-Tiny上经匹配微调可与方差剪枝（VBP）竞争，且在不变性与干预测试中表现更优，尤其对数损失能保持保留集并提升分布一致性。
MV-Forcing: Long Multi-View Video Generation via 4D-Grounded Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing
Authors: Gal Fiebelman, Hadar Averbuch-Elor, Sagie Benaim
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:54:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:54:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. Project webpage: https://galfiebelman.github.io/mv-forcing/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in video diffusion models have enabled either long single-view generation through temporal autoregression, or short multi-view synthesis through bidirectional attention. However, generating long, multi-view consistent videos of dynamic scenes remains unsolved. In this work, we present MV-Forcing, a framework that composes temporal and view-wise autoregression within a single diffusion model by introducing a 4D geometric bridge between sequentially generated views. Our key insight is that an autoregressive 3D reconstruction model naturally interfaces between autoregressively generated views. Given a completed source view, we reconstruct its 3D structure and render a geometric prior of the next target viewpoint, which the diffusion model refines into a high-quality video. To extend generation beyond the teacher's fixed temporal window, we introduce a joint denoising regime where both view slots are initialized from noise during training, enabling temporally unbounded generation. We distill the model via Distribution Matching Distillation with Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing, closing the train-inference exposure bias gap for both temporal and view-sequential autoregression. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MV-Forcing produces geometrically consistent multi-view videos of dynamic scenes at arbitrary lengths and viewpoint counts using a single few-step student model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MV-Forcing：通过4D几何锚定时空自强迫实现长时多视角视频生成
近期视频扩散模型的进展支持了通过时间自回归生成长单视角视频，或通过双向注意力生成短多视角合成。然而，动态场景中生成长时、多视角一致的视频仍属未解难题。本文提出MV-Forcing框架，通过在单个扩散模型中引入序列生成视角间的4D几何桥梁，将时间与视角自回归结合。核心思想是：自回归3D重建模型天然连接自回归生成的视角。给定已完成的源视角，我们重建其3D结构并渲染下一目标视角的几何先验，扩散模型将其精炼为高质量视频。为突破教师模型固定时间窗口限制，我们引入联合去噪机制，训练时两个视角槽均从噪声初始化，实现时间无界生成。通过时空自强迫的分布匹配蒸馏，我们弥合了训练与推理阶段在时间与视角序列自回归中的暴露偏差。在合成与真实数据上的大量实验表明，MV-Forcing仅用单个几步学生模型即可生成任意长度与视角数量的动态场景几何一致多视角视频。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating long, multi-view consistent videos of dynamic scenes, MV-Forcing introduces a 4D geometric bridge within a single diffusion model to unify temporal and view-wise autoregression. It leverages a 3D reconstruction model to infer geometric priors from completed source views, guiding the diffusion process to render high-quality target viewpoints. To enable unbounded temporal generation, the model employs joint denoising during training, initializing both view slots from noise. Enhanced via spatio-temporal self-forcing distillation, it mitigates exposure bias and achieves consistent, arbitrary-length multi-view video synthesis using a single few-step student model, validated on both synthetic and real-world datasets.
为解决动态场景中长时多视角一致视频生成的问题，MV-Forcing 在单个扩散模型内引入4D几何桥，统一时序与视角自回归。它利用3D重建模型从已完成的源视角推断几何先验，引导扩散过程生成高质量目标视角。为支持无界时序生成，训练时采用联合去噪机制，两个视角槽位均从噪声初始化。实验表明，该方法通过时空自强化蒸馏，使单个少量步骤的学生模型能生成任意长度和视角数量的几何一致多视角视频，在合成与真实数据上均优于现有方法。
Fitted Occupancy-Ratio Evaluation without Bellman Completeness
Authors: Lars van der Laan, Nathan Kallus
First: 2026-07-06T17:53:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:53:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Occupancy ratios correct distribution shift in offline reinforcement learning and are central to off-policy evaluation. Existing primal-dual and minimax methods typically estimate these ratios by enforcing occupancy-balance moments over a critic class. We propose fitted occupancy-ratio evaluation (FORE), a fitted fixed-point method that characterizes the discounted occupancy ratio through an adjoint Bellman recursion. At each iteration, FORE solves a single-level density-ratio objective on one-step-transition data, thereby projecting the adjoint Bellman image onto a log-ratio class in Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence. Unlike analyses of fitted Q-evaluation, which typically require value-function realizability together with Bellman completeness or projected-operator stability, our central approximation condition is just realizability of the discounted occupancy ratio itself. Under this condition, the population KL-projected recursion contracts in relative entropy toward the true ratio by virtue of the adjoint Bellman operator being a KL-contraction. For the empirical recursion, we establish finite-sample regret bounds that yield convergence in KL up to log-ratio approximation error and a statistical error governed by the complexity of the ratio hypothesis class. The fitted ratio supports direct value estimation by reward reweighting, occupancy-weighted fitted Q-evaluation, and doubly robust estimation that combines the fitted ratio with a fitted Q-function. Together, these results identify discounted occupancy-ratio realizability as a sufficient condition for offline policy evaluation without any completeness assumptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需贝尔曼完备性拟合占用率评估
占用率可校正离线强化学习中的分布偏移，是离策略评估的核心。现有对偶和最小最大方法通常通过在批评者类上强制占用平衡矩来估计这些比率。我们提出拟合占用率评估（FORE），这是一种拟合不动点方法，通过伴随贝尔曼递归刻画折扣占用率。在每次迭代中，FORE 在一步转移数据上求解单层密度比率目标，从而将伴随贝尔曼像投影到对数比率类中，以KL散度为度量。与拟合Q评估的分析不同，后者通常需要价值函数可实现性以及贝尔曼完备性或投影算子稳定性，我们的核心近似条件仅为折扣占用率本身的可实现性。在此条件下，由于伴随贝尔曼算子是KL收缩算子，总体KL投影递归在相对熵中收敛至真实比率。对于经验递归，我们建立了有限样本遗憾界，得到KL收敛至对数比率近似误差和由比率假设类复杂度决定的统计误差。拟合比率支持通过奖励重加权、占用加权拟合Q评估和双重稳健估计（结合拟合比率与拟合Q函数）直接进行价值估计。这些结果共同表明，折扣占用率可实现性是无需任何完备性假设的离线策略评估的充分条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for off-policy evaluation in offline reinforcement learning without Bellman completeness assumptions, the paper introduces FORE, a fitted fixed-point method that estimates discounted occupancy ratios via an adjoint Bellman recursion. At each iteration, FORE solves a density-ratio objective on one-step transition data, projecting the adjoint Bellman image onto a log-ratio class using KL divergence. The method relies only on realizability of the discounted occupancy ratio, not value-function realizability or operator stability. Empirical results show the recursion converges in KL divergence up to log-ratio approximation error and statistical error tied to the complexity of the ratio class, enabling direct value estimation through reward reweighting, occupancy-weighted Q-evaluation, and doubly robust methods.
为在无需贝尔曼完备性假设的前提下实现离线强化学习中的策略评估，本文提出FORE方法，通过伴随贝尔曼递归估计折扣占用比。每轮迭代中，FORE在一步转移数据上求解密度比目标，将伴随贝尔曼像投影到对数比假设类中，使用KL散度度量。该方法仅需折扣占用比本身可实现，无需价值函数可实现或算子稳定。实验表明，FORE在KL散度下收敛至真实比值，误差由对数比近似误差和假设类复杂度决定，支持通过奖励重加权、占用加权Q评估和双重稳健估计直接进行价值估计。
PixWorld: Unifying 3D Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Pixel Space
Authors: Sensen Gao, Zhaoqing Wang, Qihang Cao, Dongdong Yu, Changhu Wang, Jia-Wang Bian
First: 2026-07-06T17:51:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:51:00+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://sensengao.github.io/PixWorld/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D reconstruction and generation are commonly tackled by separate paradigms: pixel-based regression for reconstruction, and latent diffusion for generation. Recent works attempt to unify them in latent space, but with notable drawbacks: the diffusion objective is defined on latent features rather than the underlying 3D representation, and both branches suffer from information loss introduced by latent encoding, while requiring a pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) or Representation Autoencoder (RAE). In this paper, we reformulate these two tasks under a unified pixel-space diffusion paradigm and introduce PixWorld, a single model that jointly addresses 3D reconstruction and generation. By supervising diffusion directly on rendered images, PixWorld removes the above limitations and aligns optimization with 3D scene fidelity. Beyond photometric and perceptual supervision that operates at the 2D image level and lacks 3D geometric awareness, we further introduce a geometry perception loss that aligns rendered views with their ground truth in the geometry-aware feature space of a pretrained 3D foundation model, providing 3D structural supervision. PixWorld consistently outperforms prior latent-space generation methods and matches state-of-the-art reconstruction methods, demonstrating the superiority of a unified pixel-space approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PixWorld：在像素空间统一3D场景生成与重建
3D重建与生成通常由不同范式处理：基于像素的回归用于重建，潜在扩散用于生成。近期工作尝试在潜在空间统一二者，但存在明显缺陷：扩散目标定义在潜在特征而非底层3D表示上，且两个分支均因潜在编码引入信息损失，同时需依赖预训练的变分自编码器（VAE）或表示自编码器（RAE）。本文在统一的像素空间扩散范式下重新定义这两个任务，提出PixWorld，一个同时处理3D重建与生成的单一模型。通过直接在渲染图像上监督扩散，PixWorld消除了上述限制，并使优化与3D场景保真度对齐。除在2D图像层面进行光度与感知监督（缺乏3D几何意识）外，我们进一步引入几何感知损失，将渲染视角与预训练3D基础模型的几何感知特征空间中的真实值对齐，提供3D结构监督。PixWorld持续超越先前潜在空间生成方法，并匹配最先进重建方法，证明了统一像素空间方法的优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of separate 3D reconstruction and generation pipelines that rely on latent spaces and suffer from information loss and dependency on pretrained encoders, this paper introduces PixWorld, a unified pixel-space diffusion model that jointly handles both tasks. By directly optimizing diffusion on rendered images, PixWorld eliminates the need for latent encoders and aligns training with 3D scene fidelity. It further enhances performance by incorporating a geometry perception loss derived from a 3D foundation model, which provides structural supervision beyond standard 2D photometric metrics. Experimental results show PixWorld outperforms prior latent-space generation methods and matches state-of-the-art reconstruction models, validating the effectiveness of its pixel-space unification approach.
为解决传统3D重建与生成方法在潜在空间中因信息丢失和依赖预训练编码器而存在的不足，本文提出PixWorld，一个统一的像素空间扩散模型，同时处理重建与生成任务。通过直接在渲染图像上优化扩散过程，PixWorld消除了对潜在编码器的需求，并使训练目标与3D场景保真度对齐。此外，它引入几何感知损失，利用预训练3D基础模型在几何感知特征空间中对齐渲染视角与真实几何结构，弥补了传统2D监督的不足。实验表明，PixWorld超越了现有潜在空间生成方法，并达到当前最优重建性能，验证了像素空间统一方法的有效性。
TRIAGE: Role-Typed Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Hejian Sang, Xiaomin Li, Jiaxin Zhang, Xinchen Du, Sen Na, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
First: 2026-06-30T17:48:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:49:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic reinforcement learning requires assigning credit to environment-facing actions such as searches, clicks, edits, navigation commands, and object interactions. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework that adds a semantic role axis to outcome credit. A structured judge classifies each segment as decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression, and a fixed role-conditioned rule maps these labels to bounded segment-level process rewards. This keeps verifier outcomes as the source of optimization direction while correcting the two main blind spots of outcome-only credit. We further show that role-conditioned credit is the optimal segment-level correction expressible from role labels alone -- a projection of the per-segment advantage residual onto the role variable -- so that the fixed role constants reduce advantage estimation error whenever the judge is reliable, and we connect this to lower-variance policy gradients. Across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop, TRIAGE improves success rates over GRPO for two policy models and outperforms both a scalar judge-derived process reward and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. Ablations show that the gain comes from role typing rather than merely adding dense rewards: reliable detection of regression inside successful trajectories is the dominant contributor, while exploration credit provides a consistent secondary gain; on completed ALFWorld and WebShop rollouts, TRIAGE also reduces environment-facing turns by an additional $10.4\%$ and $14.8\%$ relative to GRPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIAGE：面向代理强化学习的角色类型信用分配
代理强化学习需要为面向环境的动作（如搜索、点击、编辑、导航指令和对象交互）分配信用。标准GRPO将最终验证器结果作为所有动作令牌的统一优势信号。该结果信号虽有用，但结构上不完整：它惩罚失败轨迹中的有益探索，同时强化成功轨迹中的冗余或退化动作。我们提出TRIAGE，一种基于角色类型的信用分配框架，为结果信用增加语义角色轴。一个结构化裁判将每个片段分类为决定性进展、有益探索、无进展基础设施或退化，并通过固定的角色条件规则将这些标签映射为有界的片段级过程奖励。这保留了验证器结果作为优化方向的来源，同时纠正了仅基于结果信用的两个主要盲点。我们进一步证明，角色条件信用是仅从角色标签可表达的最优片段级修正——即片段优势残差在角色变量上的投影——因此，当裁判可靠时，固定角色常数可降低优势估计误差，并与更低方差的策略梯度相关联。在ALFWorld、Search-QA和WebShop上，TRIAGE在两种策略模型上优于GRPO的成功率，并优于基于标量裁判推导的过程奖励和基于结果监督的共享主干价值基线。消融实验表明，收益来源于角色类型而非单纯增加密集奖励：成功轨迹内退化检测的可靠识别是主要贡献，探索信用提供持续的次要收益；在完成的ALFWorld和WebShop轨迹中，TRIAGE相比GRPO进一步减少了面向环境的交互轮次10.4%和14.8%。
Summary / 总结
TRIAGE addresses the limitation of standard GRPO, which assigns uniform credit to all actions based solely on final verifier outcomes, thereby penalizing useful exploration in failures and reinforcing redundant actions in successes. It introduces a role-typed credit assignment framework where a structured judge classifies action segments into four semantic roles—decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression—and maps them to bounded segment-level rewards conditioned on these roles. This preserves the verifier’s direction while correcting two key blind spots. Experimental results across ALFWorld, Search-QA, and WebShop show TRIAGE consistently outperforms GRPO and alternative baselines, with gains primarily driven by detecting regression within successful trajectories and secondary benefits from exploration credit; additionally, it reduces environment-facing turns by 10.4% and 14.8% on completed rollouts.
TRIAGE 解决了标准 GRPO 仅依据最终验证器结果对所有动作赋予统一信用的缺陷，该方法会惩罚失败轨迹中的有益探索，同时强化成功轨迹中的冗余动作。它引入了一种基于角色的信用分配框架，通过结构化裁判将动作片段分类为四种语义角色——决定性进展、有益探索、无进展基础设施或退化，并将其映射为基于角色的片段级奖励。该方法保留了验证器的优化方向，同时修正了仅凭结果信用的两大盲点。在 ALFWorld、Search-QA 和 WebShop 三个任务上，TRIAGE 在两个策略模型上均显著提升成功率，优于基于标量裁判的进程奖励和基于结果监督的共享主干价值基线，并在完成的轨迹中分别减少环境交互动作 10.4% 和 14.8%。消融实验表明，收益主要来自角色分类，尤其是成功轨迹中对退化动作的可靠检测。
GaP: A Graph-as-Policy Multi-Agent Self-Learning Harness For Variational Automation Tasks
Authors: Kaiyuan Chen, Shuangyu Xie, Letian Fu, Justin Yu, William Pacini, Sandeep Bajamahal, Hudson Kim, Jaimyn Drake, Daehwa Kim, Haoru Xue, Jonathan Francis, Christian Juette, Peter Schaldenbrand, Muhammet Yunus Seker, Ruwan Wickramarachchi, Uksang Yoo, Guanzhi Wang, Adithyavairavan Murali, Balakumar Sundaralingam, S. Shankar Sastry, Spencer Huang, Yuke Zhu, Linxi "Jim" Fan, Ken Goldberg
First: 2026-07-06T17:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:47:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
For robots to work reliably in commercial and industrial applications, can recent advances in agentic coding systems combine interpretable robot programming with the open-world adaptability of model-free policies? We focus on "Variational Automation" (VA), a class of tasks that have larger variations in object geometry and pose than fixed automation. Model-free policies often struggle to close the reliability gap for VA tasks, which must be executed persistently and reliably in commercial and industrial applications. Motivated by prior work on Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) and the Robot Operating System (ROS), we introduce Graph-as-Policy (GaP), a multi-agent coding harness that generates directed computation graphs with perception, planning, and control nodes from a Modular Open Robot Skill Library (MORSL). GaP then generates an internal simulation environment to rehearse task instances with different graphs in parallel to iteratively refine the graph structure and parameters to improve success rates and throughput. Evaluation with 8 new open VA task benchmarks, 4 in-simulation and 4 in real-world, suggests that GaP can achieve success rates that significantly outperform baselines. Details, code, and data can be found online: https://graph-robots.github.io/gap
中文标题/摘要

SPEARBench: A Benchmark for Naturalness Evaluation in Streaming Speech-to-Speech Language Models
Authors: Thomas Thebaud, Yuzhe Wang, Hao Zhang, Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar, Ashish Hallur, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velazquez
First: 2026-07-06T17:42:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:42:59+00:00
Comments: Corresponding Website: https://thomasthebaud.github.io/SPEAR-benchmark-website/#welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Streaming speech-to-speech language models aim to answer spoken queries directly with synthetic speech. However, standard speech and text benchmarks do not capture whether these systems behave naturally in conversations, where timing, turn-taking, prosody, interpersonal stance, language and dialect consistency, and relationship-aware appropriateness jointly shape perceived quality. We introduce SPEARBench, a benchmark for evaluating naturalness in speech-to-speech language models from question-answer interactions. SPEARBench constructs controlled dialogue prompts from the Seamless Interaction corpus, runs inference across multiple models, and evaluates generated answers using a multidimensional protocol that covers response latency, interruptions, speech quality, ASR robustness, language and dialect consistency, emotional naturalness, interpersonal stance, and explainable distributional baselines. The benchmark includes original human answers as a reference condition and reports results for several contemporary models. Results show that current models can achieve high signal-level quality and low ASR error while still differing from human conversational behavior in latency, overlap, dialect preservation, emotional adaptation, and interpersonal stance dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPEARBench：用于流式语音到语音语言模型自然度评估的基准
流式语音到语音语言模型旨在直接用合成语音回答口语查询。然而，标准的语音和文本基准无法衡量这些系统在对话中是否表现自然，因为对话中的自然度由响应延迟、轮流发言、语调、人际立场、语言与方言一致性以及关系感知的适当性共同塑造。我们引入SPEARBench，一个用于评估语音到语音语言模型自然度的基准，基于问答互动构建。SPEARBench从无缝交互语料库中构建受控对话提示，对多个模型进行推理，并使用多维评估协议评估生成答案，涵盖响应延迟、打断、语音质量、ASR鲁棒性、语言与方言一致性、情感自然度、人际立场及可解释的分布基线。该基准包含原始人类答案作为参考条件，并报告多个当前模型的结果。结果显示，当前模型虽能实现高信号质量与低ASR错误，但在响应延迟、重叠、方言保留、情感适应及人际立场动态方面仍与人类对话行为存在差异。
