Motivated by the need to evaluate how naturally streaming speech-to-speech models behave in real conversations, the authors introduce SPEARBench, a benchmark that assesses naturalness through controlled dialogue prompts derived from the Seamless Interaction corpus. The method involves running inference across multiple models and evaluating generated responses across eight dimensions including latency, interruptions, speech quality, ASR robustness, dialect consistency, emotional naturalness, interpersonal stance, and distributional baselines. Experimental results show that while current models achieve high signal quality and low ASR error, they still fall short of human conversational behavior in areas such as timing, dialect preservation, emotional adaptation, and interpersonal dynamics.
为评估流式语音到语音语言模型在真实对话中的自然性，作者提出了SPEARBench基准，通过来自Seamless Interaction语料库的控制对话提示进行评估。该方法在多个模型上运行推理，并从八个维度评估生成回答，包括延迟、打断、语音质量、ASR鲁棒性、方言一致性、情感自然性、人际立场和分布基线。实验结果表明，当前模型虽在信号质量和ASR错误率上表现优异，但在对话时机、方言保持、情感适应和人际动态等方面仍与人类对话行为存在差距。
REDDIT: Correcting Model-Generated Timestamp Drift in ASR without Forgetting via Replay-Based Distribution Editing
Authors: Cheng-Kang Chou, Ming-To Chuang, Ke-Han Lu, Chan-Jan Hsu, Hung-yi Lee
First: 2026-07-06T17:40:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:40:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern autoregressive ASR systems can emit timestamps as decoded tokens, enabling timestamped transcription without frame-level aligners or inference-time post-processing. We show that these generated timestamps can drift across long non-speech spans: the transcript may remain plausible, but the decoded time axis drifts away from the audio. We study this non-speech-induced timestamp drift with self-built gap and long-gap benchmarks across 15 evaluated timestamp-producing ASR and audio-language systems. Naive timestamp-corrected fine-tuning improves alignment but can severely degrade non-target ASR behavior, exposing a forgetting problem. We propose REDDIT(REplay-based Distribution eDITing), a lightweight two-stage post-training framework that corrects timestamps while avoiding this catastrophic forgetting: it first edits timestamp targets under the model's own replayed decoder context while matching the frozen base distribution on non-timestamp tokens, then applies a short edited-prefix refinement stage. In this framework, we construct correction supervision without human transcripts or human timestamp annotations by combining VAD-trimmed speech spans with inserted non-speech gaps and known concatenation offsets. On Whisper-tiny, 34.9 hours of targeted correction audio used and only 1.6% of model parameters updated, raising long-gap mIoU from 38.7% to 95.0% and reducing mixed-gap out-of-domain AAS from 2752 ms to 223 ms while preserving CV-en MER at 41.3% (versus 524.2% for ordinary SFT decoder tuning).
中文标题/摘要
标题：REDDIT：通过基于重放的分布编辑，在ASR中纠正模型生成的时间戳漂移且避免遗忘
现代自回归ASR系统可将时间戳作为解码令牌输出，无需帧级对齐器或推理时后处理即可实现带时间戳的转录。我们发现，这些生成的时间戳在长段非语音区间内会漂移：转录文本可能仍合理，但解码时间轴偏离音频。我们通过自建的间隙和长间隙基准，在15个评估的生成时间戳的ASR和音频-语言系统中研究了非语音诱导的时间戳漂移。朴素的时间戳校正微调虽能改善对齐，但会严重损害非目标ASR行为，暴露出遗忘问题。我们提出REDDIT（基于重放的分布编辑），一种轻量级两阶段后训练框架，可在纠正时间戳的同时避免灾难性遗忘：首先在模型自身重放的解码上下文中编辑时间戳目标，同时保持冻结的基础分布对非时间戳令牌的匹配；然后应用短编辑前缀精调阶段。在该框架中，我们通过结合VAD修剪后的语音片段、插入的非语音间隙和已知拼接偏移，构建无需人工转录或人工时间戳标注的校正监督。在Whisper-tiny上，仅用34.9小时目标校正音频和仅1.6%模型参数更新，将长间隙mIoU从38.7%提升至95.0%，将混合间隙域外AAS从2752毫秒降至223毫秒，同时保持CV-en MER为41.3%（普通SFT解码器微调为524.2%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of timestamp drift in autoregressive ASR systems during long non-speech spans, this work proposes REDDIT, a lightweight two-stage post-training framework that corrects timestamps without degrading general ASR performance. REDDIT first edits timestamp targets using the model’s own replayed decoder context while preserving the frozen base distribution on non-timestamp tokens, then refines with a short edited-prefix stage. Evaluated across 15 ASR systems using self-built gap benchmarks, REDDIT achieves significant improvements: on Whisper-tiny, it boosts long-gap mIoU from 38.7% to 95.0%, cuts out-of-domain AAS from 2752 ms to 223 ms, and maintains CV-en MER at 41.3%—far better than naive fine-tuning which degrades performance by over 500%.
为解决自回归ASR系统在长非语音段落中生成的时间戳漂移问题，本文提出REDDIT框架，一种轻量级两阶段后训练方法，能在修正时间戳的同时避免模型遗忘原有ASR能力。该方法首先在模型自身重放的解码上下文中编辑时间戳目标，同时保持非时间戳令牌的冻结分布，随后通过短编辑前缀微调阶段进行优化。实验表明，在仅使用34.9小时目标校正音频和1.6%模型参数的情况下，REDDIT将长间隔mIoU从38.7%提升至95.0%，将域外AAS从2752毫秒降至223毫秒，同时保持CV-en MER为41.3%，远优于普通微调方法（后者性能下降超500%）。
SovereignPA-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents under Evolving Intent, Platform Mediation, and Consent Constraints
Authors: Dylan Zongmin Liu
First: 2026-07-06T17:39:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:39:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personal agents are becoming persistent user-owned intermediaries: they remember preferences, filter platform-mediated information, use tools, and negotiate with services. Existing benchmarks evaluate tool use, web navigation, desktop control, personalization, recommendation, and evolving context, but rarely ask whether an agent preserves user sovereignty: advancing the user's current interests while respecting privacy, consent, evidence, user burden, and resistance to manipulative incentives. We introduce SovereignPA-Bench, an executable benchmark for evaluating user-owned personal agents under evolving intent, platform mediation, privacy boundaries, consent constraints, evidence requirements, and burden tradeoffs. The benchmark separates agent-visible ObservableState from evaluator-only HiddenLabels, reports component metrics for task success, alignment, privacy, consent, evidence, manipulation, burden, and auditability, and preserves paired scenario ordering for model and policy comparisons. We evaluate 120 sovereignty stress scenarios across 4 model families and 8 policy baselines, yielding 3,840 frozen-prompt trajectories with raw prompts, outputs, provider-form responses, parsed actions, recomputable metrics, hard-set analyses, qualitative cases, and a blinded 3-annotator audit over 240 items. Full-sovereign scaffolding improves sovereignty score over direct, memory-only, consent-only, evidence-only, ReAct/tool-use, safety-prompt, and judge-guard baselines while reducing privacy leakage, consent violation, over-concession, and manipulation capture. Human audit shows high agreement on privacy and consent and lower agreement on manipulation, identifying the subjective frontier of platform-persuasion judgments. These results show that personal-agent evaluation must move beyond task completion toward representative, consent-aware, evidence-grounded action.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SovereignPA-Bench：在意图演变、平台中介与同意约束下评估用户拥有的个人代理
个人代理正演变为持久的用户自有中介：它们记忆偏好、过滤平台中介信息、使用工具并与服务协商。现有基准评估工具使用、网页导航、桌面控制、个性化、推荐及上下文演变，但很少考察代理是否维护用户主权：即在尊重隐私、同意、证据、用户负担及抵抗操纵性激励的前提下推进用户当前利益。我们引入SovereignPA-Bench，一个可执行基准，用于在意图演变、平台中介、隐私边界、同意约束、证据要求及负担权衡下评估用户自有个人代理。该基准区分代理可见的ObservableState与评估者独享的HiddenLabels，报告任务成功、对齐、隐私、同意、证据、操纵、负担及可审计性等组件指标，并保留配对场景顺序以支持模型与策略比较。我们在4个模型家族和8个策略基线中评估120个主权压力场景，生成3840条冻结提示轨迹，包含原始提示、输出、提供商格式响应、解析动作、可重算指标、硬设分析、定性案例及240项盲审的3位标注者审计。全主权框架在直接、仅记忆、仅同意、仅证据、ReAct/工具使用、安全提示、裁判-守卫基线等方法上提升主权得分，同时降低隐私泄露、同意违规、过度让步及操纵捕捉。人工审计显示隐私与同意判断高度一致，操纵判断一致性较低，揭示平台说服判断的主观边界。这些结果表明，个人代理评估必须超越任务完成，转向代表性、同意意识、证据基础的行为评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate personal agents that respect user sovereignty amid evolving intent and platform constraints, the authors introduce SovereignPA-Bench, an executable benchmark that assesses agent behavior across privacy, consent, evidence, and burden dimensions. The method separates observable agent states from hidden evaluator labels and reports granular metrics for each dimension, while preserving scenario order for fair model comparisons. Experimental results across 120 stress scenarios with 4 model families and 8 policy baselines show that full-sovereign scaffolding outperforms baselines in sovereignty scores while reducing privacy leakage, consent violations, over-concession, and manipulation capture. Human audits reveal high inter-annotator agreement on privacy and consent but lower agreement on manipulation, highlighting the subjectivity of platform-persuasion judgments.
为评估在动态意图、平台干预和权限约束下仍能保障用户主权的个人代理，本文提出SovereignPA-Bench可执行基准，从隐私、权限、证据和负担等维度评估代理行为。方法将代理可见状态与评估者专属标签分离，支持任务成功、对齐度和操纵等分项指标，并保持场景顺序以确保模型比较公平。实验在120个压力场景中对4类模型和8种策略基线进行测试，结果显示全主权架构在主权得分上优于直接、记忆、权限或证据单一策略，同时降低隐私泄露、权限违规、过度让步和操纵捕捉，人工审计显示隐私与权限判断高度一致，但操纵判断主观性高，揭示平台说服判断的主观边界。
Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle
Authors: Alexander Okezue Bell
First: 2026-05-21T20:12:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:37:14+00:00
Comments: 74 pages, 61 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Of $1{,}536$ Gaussian release covariances we tested for single-layer hidden-state privacy, zero achieve both moderate utility and moderate privacy against an adaptive retrieval attacker. We prove a complementary Fisher-ball lower bound: every full-rank Gaussian release at $O(1)$ Fisher utility admits a direction whose Mahalanobis signal grows linearly in hidden width, ruling out uniform Gaussian safety in the class and matching the empirical empty middle. The diagonal inverse-Fisher release $Σ^\star_{\mathrm{diag}}(\mathcal{K}) = (2\mathcal{K}/d)\,\mathrm{diag}(1/F_{ii})$ is the unique minimax-optimal diagonal mechanism at first-order KL budget $\mathcal{K}$ and the only release with worst-attacker top-1 $\le 0.001$ at every point of a 32 model-layer grid, but it sits on a privacy/utility edge rather than filling the middle. A generalized-eigen mechanism reaching $13\times$ Pareto reduction under Euclidean retrieval collapses to $100\%$ top-1 under the adaptive Mahalanobis attacker, and a full-trajectory sequence inverter recovers $94\%$ of clean GPT-2 prefixes but $0\%$ under $Σ_{\mathrm{diag}}$. A split-memory transformer trained from scratch reaches $G_{\mathrm{Mah}} \in [20, 33]$ at 90M and maintains a $6$--$24\times$ advantage over same-budget GPT baselines from 30M to 1B at a fixed-token language-modeling loss penalty; pretrained models top out at 9.3. These results reframe hidden-state release from mechanism-design within the Gaussian class to architecture or release co-design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：隐藏状态隐私不存在中间地带
在我们测试的1536个高斯发布协方差中，针对单层隐藏状态隐私和自适应检索攻击者，没有任何一个同时实现中等效用与中等隐私。我们证明了一个互补的Fisher球下界：每个秩为满的高斯发布，在O(1) Fisher效用下，都存在一个方向，其Mahalanobis信号随隐藏宽度线性增长，从而排除了该类中均匀高斯安全的可能性，并与实证结果中的“空中间地带”一致。对角逆Fisher发布Σ*_diag(𝒦) = (2𝒦/d)·diag(1/F_ii)是第一阶KL预算𝒦下的唯一最小最大最优对角机制，也是在32层模型网格上每个点处最坏攻击者top-1 ≤ 0.001的唯一发布，但它位于隐私/效用边缘而非填充中间地带。一种广义特征值机制在欧氏检索下实现13倍帕累托改进，但在自适应Mahalanobis攻击者下退化为100% top-1；一个完整轨迹序列逆向器在Σ*_diag下恢复0%的干净GPT-2前缀，但能恢复94%。从零训练的分记忆Transformer在90M参数下达到G_Mah ∈ [20, 33]，并在30M至1B参数范围内，以固定token语言建模损失惩罚下，相较同等预算的GPT基线保持6至24倍优势；预训练模型最高仅达9.3。这些结果将隐藏状态发布机制设计从高斯类框架重构为架构或发布协同设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of Gaussian mechanisms that simultaneously offer moderate utility and privacy against adaptive retrieval attacks, the paper investigates hidden-state privacy through Fisher-information-based bounds and empirical testing. It proves that any full-rank Gaussian release with constant Fisher utility must have a direction where Mahalanobis signal scales linearly with hidden width, explaining the empirically observed 'empty middle' in privacy-utility tradeoffs. The diagonal inverse-Fisher mechanism is shown to be minimax-optimal at first-order KL budget and achieves near-zero top-1 error across a 32-layer grid, but it resides on a privacy/utility edge. Generalized-eigen mechanisms degrade to 100% top-1 under adaptive Mahalanobis attacks, while a full-trajectory inverter recovers 94% of clean GPT-2 prefixes but fails under diagonal release. A split-memory transformer architecture achieves Mahalanobis scores between 20 and 33 at 90M parameters and outperforms pretrained baselines by 6–24× across 30M to 1B, suggesting that optimal hidden-state release requires co-design of architecture and mechanism rather than Gaussian-only tuning.
为解决高斯机制在适应性检索攻击下难以同时满足中等效用与中等隐私的问题，该研究通过Fisher信息下界与实证测试探索隐藏状态隐私。证明了全秩高斯发布无法统一满足二者，且对KL预算下的最小最大最优机制为对角逆Fisher机制，但其位于效用-隐私边缘而非中间区域。实验表明，广义特征值机制在适应性Mahalanobis攻击下退化至100% top-1，而从零训练的分记忆Transformer在90M参数下实现20–33的Mahalanobis信号，相较同预算GPT基线优势达6–24倍，预训练模型最高仅9.3。
Graph Sparse Sampling: Breaking the Curse of the Horizon in Continuous MDP Planning
Authors: Idan Lev-Yehudi, Vadim Indelman
First: 2026-07-06T17:36:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:36:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Planning under uncertainty in continuous domains is essential for autonomous systems, yet computationally demanding. Tree-based search methods such as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) remain popular, but their branching structure can require sampling budgets that grow exponentially with lookahead depth in the worst case. From a tree perspective, continuous state or action spaces become especially challenging, since the planner must decide where to search in an infinite branching hierarchy. We propose Graph Sparse Sampling (GSS), an online planning algorithm that shares sampled futures across many candidate decisions, rather than sampling separate successors for each candidate action. This branch-free graph exposes large GPU-friendly batches, while using heuristics to focus computation. We prove finite-sample performance guarantees for GSS covering full-rank or low-rank generative simulators via smoothed backups, and discrete or sampled continuous action spaces. Under suitable overlap, regularity, and action-coverage conditions, these bounds have polynomial dependence on the planning horizon, formalizing when shared futures can avoid the exponential horizon dependence of tree-shaped sparse sampling. We demonstrate continuous-control simulations where GSS substantially outperforms tree-based planners on long horizons or achieves near-optimal performance, supporting no-branching graph planning as a complementary design principle for online control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图稀疏采样：打破连续MDP规划中的时域诅咒
在连续域中进行不确定性下的规划对自主系统至关重要，但计算成本高昂。基于树的搜索方法（如蒙特卡洛树搜索，MCTS）仍广受欢迎，但其分支结构在最坏情况下可能需要随前瞻深度指数增长的采样预算。从树的角度看，连续状态或动作空间尤其具有挑战性，因为规划器必须在无限分支层次中决定搜索位置。我们提出图稀疏采样（GSS），一种在线规划算法，通过在多个候选决策间共享采样未来，而非为每个候选动作单独采样后继。这种无分支图结构暴露了适合GPU的大批量数据，同时利用启发式方法聚焦计算。我们通过平滑备份证明了GSS在全秩或低秩生成模拟器、离散或采样连续动作空间下的有限样本性能保证。在满足适当重叠、正则性和动作覆盖条件下，这些界限对规划时域具有多项式依赖性，正式说明了共享未来如何避免树形稀疏采样中的指数时域依赖。我们在连续控制仿真中展示了GSS在长时域下显著优于树形规划器，或达到近最优性能，支持无分支图规划作为在线控制的互补设计原则。
Summary / 总结
To address the exponential computational cost of tree-based planning in continuous MDPs, Graph Sparse Sampling (GSS) introduces an online algorithm that shares sampled futures across multiple candidate actions, avoiding the branching structure that causes budget explosion with horizon depth. By constructing a branch-free graph that enables GPU-optimized batch processing and heuristic-guided computation, GSS achieves polynomial dependence on planning horizon under conditions of action coverage and regularity, formalizing when shared futures can break the curse of the horizon. Experimental results in continuous-control simulations show GSS substantially outperforms tree-based methods on long horizons or achieves near-optimal performance, validating graph-based planning as a viable alternative for online control.
为解决连续MDP中树形规划方法因深度增加导致的指数级计算开销，本文提出图稀疏采样（GSS）算法，通过在多个候选动作间共享采样未来状态，而非为每个动作独立采样。该无分支图结构支持GPU高效批处理与启发式计算，有效规避“时域诅咒”。理论分析在满足重叠、正则性和动作覆盖条件下，证明GSS具有多项式依赖于规划时域的性能保证。实验表明，在长时域连续控制任务中，GSS显著优于树形规划方法，常接近最优性能。
ReCal3R: Reliability-Calibrated Learning Rates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Xinze Li, Yiyuan Wang, Pengxu Chen, Wentao Fan, Weifeng Su, Weisi Lin, Wentao Cheng
First: 2026-07-06T17:35:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:35:18+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 7 figures. Project Page: https://powertony102.github.io/recal3r.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Streaming 3D reconstruction relies on a compact recurrent scene state to process long image streams in linear time and bounded memory. However, repeated updates can gradually corrupt this state, causing reliable historical information to be overwritten by noisy or ambiguous observations. We introduce ReCal3R, a reliability-calibrated learning rate method for recurrent 3D reconstruction. Instead of directly applying a candidate learning rate, our method estimates state token reliability from the maintained scene state and uses it to calibrate a candidate learning rate derived from token alignment, state reconstruction residual, and recent update pressure. The resulting token-wise learning rate interpolates between a conservative base rate and the candidate rate, suppressing aggressive updates on unreliable tokens while preserving adaptation to informative frames. Applied to CUT3R as a training-free calibration rule, ReCal3R reaches strong performance on long sequences in pose, depth, and reconstruction quality, including a 3.7$\times$ reduction in ATE, with comparable runtime and memory. Code is available at: https://github.com/Powertony102/ReCal3R.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReCal3R：面向流式3D重建的可靠性校准学习率
流式3D重建依赖紧凑的递归场景状态，在线性时间内处理长图像序列并保持内存受限。然而，重复更新会逐渐破坏该状态，导致可靠的历史信息被噪声或模糊的观测覆盖。我们提出ReCal3R，一种面向递归3D重建的可靠性校准学习率方法。该方法不直接应用候选学习率，而是从维护的场景状态中估计状态标记的可靠性，并利用其校准由标记对齐、状态重建残差和近期更新压力推导出的候选学习率。所得的标记级学习率在保守基础率与候选率之间插值，抑制对不可靠标记的激进更新，同时保留对信息帧的适应能力。当作为无训练校准规则应用于CUT3R时，ReCal3R在姿态、深度和重建质量方面在长序列上达到强性能，包括ATE降低3.7倍，且运行时和内存开销相当。代码见：https://github.com/Powertony102/ReCal3R。
Summary / 总结
To address the degradation of recurrent 3D reconstruction states caused by noisy updates in streaming scenarios, ReCal3R introduces a reliability-calibrated learning rate method that dynamically adjusts token-wise updates based on state token reliability. This reliability is estimated from token alignment, state reconstruction residuals, and recent update pressure, and is used to interpolate between a conservative base rate and a candidate rate derived from these signals. Experiments on CUT3R show that ReCal3R significantly improves pose, depth, and reconstruction quality over long sequences, achieving a 3.7x reduction in ATE while maintaining comparable runtime and memory usage.
为解决流式3D重建中因噪声更新导致的递归状态退化问题，ReCal3R提出一种可靠性校准的学习率方法，根据状态令牌的可靠性动态调整各令牌的更新幅度。该可靠性由令牌对齐度、状态重建残差和近期更新压力综合估计，并用于在保守基础率与候选学习率之间进行插值，抑制对不可靠令牌的激进更新，同时保留对信息帧的适应能力。作为无训练校准规则应用于CUT3R时，ReCal3R在长序列上表现优异，ATE降低3.7倍，且保持相近的运行时间和内存开销。
Faithfulness to Refusal: A Causal Audit of Neuron Selectors
Authors: Ananth Eswar, Pratinav Seth, Utsav Avaiya, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
First: 2026-07-06T17:33:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:33:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Attribution scores increasingly identify which neuron rows of a language model matter for applications such as pruning, interpretability, and editing for safety, yet whether they identify causally important rows is rarely tested directly. We address this with two paired audits built on one-shot neuron-row zeroing. We first audit selectors at the language-modeling level: attribution methods substantially outperform activation and magnitude-based baselines at identifying dispensable rows across five LLMs. We then adapt the same intervention into a behavior test by driving it with a contrastive harmful-versus-benign signal; the attributed rows are sufficient to install refusal on hate and crime while keeping benign over-refusal low and preserving language model fluency, and specific in that layer-matched random controls at the same depths fail. Highly rank-stable selectors can be among the least causally valid. Refusal moreover lives in a redundant subspace, where different attribution methods install it through largely disjoint row sets, so the recovered edit is one realization of a sufficient set rather than a unique mechanism. Together, these findings show that rank-stability proxies miss the kinds of selector failures a direct causal audit can surface.%
中文标题/摘要
标题：对拒绝行为的忠实性：神经元选择器的因果审计
归因分数越来越多地用于识别语言模型中哪些神经元行对剪枝、可解释性和安全编辑等应用至关重要，但它们是否能准确识别因果上重要的行却很少被直接检验。我们通过基于单次神经元行置零的两项配对审计来解决这一问题。首先，在语言建模层面审计选择器：归因方法在五个大语言模型中显著优于基于激活和幅度的基线方法，能够更准确地识别可省略的行。随后，我们将同一干预方法适配为行为测试，通过有害与良性信号的对比驱动；归因行足以在保持良性过拒绝率低且语言模型流畅性不变的前提下，实现对仇恨和犯罪内容的拒绝；而相同深度的层匹配随机对照组则失败。高度排名稳定的筛选器可能恰恰是最缺乏因果有效性的。此外，拒绝行为存在于冗余子空间中，不同归因方法通过几乎不重叠的行集实现拒绝，因此恢复的编辑只是充分集合的一个实现，而非唯一机制。这些发现共同表明，排名稳定性代理指标无法捕捉直接因果审计所能揭示的筛选器失效类型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to validate whether neuron attribution scores in language models reflect causal importance rather than mere correlation, the study conducts two causal audits using one-shot neuron-row zeroing. First, it tests attribution methods against activation and magnitude baselines across five LLMs, finding attribution methods significantly better at identifying dispensable rows. Second, it uses a contrastive harmful-versus-benign signal to test refusal behavior, showing that attributed rows can successfully install refusal for hate and crime while preserving fluency and minimizing over-refusal, whereas random controls at matching depths fail. The study also finds that rank-stable selectors can be causally invalid and that refusal emerges from a redundant subspace, with different attribution methods selecting largely disjoint row sets, indicating edits are not unique mechanisms.
为验证神经元归因方法是否真正识别语言模型中因果重要的行，该研究采用单次神经元行置零方法进行两项因果审计。首先，在五个大语言模型上对比归因方法与激活和幅度基线，发现归因方法显著更优，能更准确识别可省略的行。其次，通过有害与良性信号对比驱动行为测试，发现归因行可成功安装对仇恨与犯罪内容的拒绝行为，同时保持语言流畅性并降低误拒，而相同深度的随机控制行则失败。研究还发现高排名稳定性的选择器可能因果有效性低，且拒绝行为存在于冗余子空间中，不同归因方法选择的行集大多互不重叠，说明编辑结果只是充分集合的一个实现而非唯一机制。
Geometric Reciprocity: Unlocking Self-Supervision for Stereoscopic Video Generation
Authors: Jingyi Lu, Kai Han
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:32:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:32:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/grt/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Monocular-to-stereo conversion synthesizes stereoscopic content from 2D videos for immersive 3D experiences. In modern Depth-Image-Based Rendering (DIBR) approaches, stereo inpainting of disocclusions is the critical bottleneck. Training-based methods achieve superior quality but rely on scarce stereo pairs or synthetic data with domain gaps. We address this through the first self-supervised framework learning from monocular videos via cycle consistency. Our key contribution is the Geometric Reciprocity Theorem (GRT): under the nearest-neighbor DIBR formulation, the disocclusion mask when synthesizing a target view equals the mask of pixels lost when warping back from target to source, enabling analytical computation of test-time disocclusion masks directly from monocular images. This yields train-test consistency for the stated warping formulation, supporting self-supervised learning from unlimited monocular videos and substantial improvements over training-free and supervised state-of-the-art methods. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/grt/
中文标题/摘要
标题：几何互逆性：解锁立体视频生成的自监督机制
单目到立体的转换技术可从2D视频合成立体内容，以实现沉浸式3D体验。在现代基于深度图像的渲染（DIBR）方法中，遮挡区域的立体补全是关键瓶颈。基于训练的方法虽质量更优，但依赖稀缺的立体图像对或存在领域差距的合成数据。我们通过首个自监督框架解决此问题，该框架从单目视频中学习，利用循环一致性。我们的核心贡献是几何互逆定理（GRT）：在最近邻DIBR公式下，合成目标视角时的遮挡掩码等于从目标视角反向扭曲回源视角时丢失像素的掩码，从而可直接从单目图像分析计算测试时的遮挡掩码。这实现了所述扭曲公式下的训练-测试一致性，支持从无限单目视频中进行自监督学习，并显著优于无训练和监督式最新方法。项目页面：https://visual-ai.github.io/grt/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating stereoscopic video from monocular inputs, this work introduces a self-supervised framework to address the critical bottleneck of stereo inpainting for disoccluded regions. It leverages the Geometric Reciprocity Theorem (GRT), which analytically computes disocclusion masks at test time by exploiting symmetry in nearest-neighbor DIBR warping, enabling train-test consistency without stereo supervision. Experiments demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms both training-free and supervised baselines, achieving state-of-the-art quality using only monocular video data.
为解决从单目视频生成立体视频时遮挡区域修复困难的问题，本文提出首个自监督框架，利用几何互逆定理（GRT）在最近邻DIBR模型下建立正向与反向光流的遮挡掩码对称关系，从而无需真实立体数据即可在测试时直接计算遮挡掩码，实现训练与测试的一致性。实验表明，该方法在无需监督或合成数据的情况下，显著优于现有最先进方法，有效提升了立体视频生成质量。
Selective Disclosure Watermarking for Large Language Models
Authors: Xuyang Chen, Xiang Li, Yangxinyu Xie, Qi Long
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:32:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:32:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Watermarking methods embed imperceptible and verifiable signals into text generated by large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches include zero-bit schemes for distinguishing synthetic text from human writing and multi-bit schemes for embedding metadata. However, current multi-bit watermarking methods do not allow selective disclosure: verifying any part of the watermark requires revealing the entire embedded message. This lack of control leads to unnecessary information exposure and raises privacy concerns. We propose Hierarchical Vocabulary Routing (HeRo), a watermarking framework that enables selective disclosure of embedded metadata. The method recursively partitions the vocabulary and distributes watermark information across hierarchical layers, so that different verifiers can decode only the portions of the payload corresponding to their access level. We show that the proposed scheme preserves the unbiasedness of the underlying sampling process and thus maintains text quality. Experiments demonstrate that our framework supports fine-grained access control while achieving high detection accuracy and low latency. Code is available at https://github.com/xuyangc03/hero-watermark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大语言模型的选择性披露水印方法
水印方法将不可察觉且可验证的信号嵌入大语言模型（LLM）生成的文本中。现有方法包括零比特方案，用于区分合成文本与人类写作，以及多比特方案，用于嵌入元数据。然而，当前多比特水印方法不支持选择性披露：验证水印的任何部分均需披露整个嵌入消息。这种缺乏控制导致不必要的信息泄露并引发隐私问题。我们提出分层词汇路由（HeRo）水印框架，支持嵌入元数据的选择性披露。该方法递归划分词汇表，并将水印信息分布于分层结构中，使不同验证者仅能解码与其访问级别对应的负载部分。我们证明该方案保持底层采样过程的无偏性，从而维持文本质量。实验表明，该框架支持细粒度访问控制，同时实现高检测准确率和低延迟。代码见 https://github.com/xuyangc03/hero-watermark。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy risks of current multi-bit watermarking methods that require full disclosure to verify any part of the embedded message, this paper introduces Hierarchical Vocabulary Routing (HeRo), a selective disclosure watermarking framework for large language models. HeRo recursively partitions the vocabulary and distributes watermark bits across hierarchical layers, enabling verifiers to decode only the portions of the payload matching their access level. Experiments confirm that HeRo preserves text quality by maintaining unbiased sampling and achieves high detection accuracy with low latency, while offering fine-grained access control.
为解决现有多比特水印方法在验证时需完整披露水印信息导致的隐私泄露问题，本文提出分层词汇路由（HeRo）框架，通过递归划分词汇表并将水印信息分布于多层结构中，使验证者仅能访问与其权限匹配的部分水印内容。该方法在保持文本生成无偏采样、不降低文本质量的前提下，实现了细粒度访问控制。实验表明，HeRo在保证高检测准确率和低延迟的同时，有效支持了按需披露机制。
Multiplayer Interactive World Models with Representation Autoencoders
Authors: Anthony Hu, Václav Volhejn, Adrien Ramanana Rahary, Chris Mulder, Aditya Makkar, Amélie Royer, Manu Orsini, Alyx Liao, Adam Jelley, Eloi Alonso, Florian Laurent, Fredrik Norén, James Swingos, Jan Hünermann, Kent Rollins, Lucas Hosseini, Matthieu Le Cauchois, Maxim Peter, Pim de Witte, Tim Brown, Vincent Micheli, Moritz Böhle, Gabriel de Marmiesse, Viktoriia Sharmanska, Lucia Specia, Michael Black, Patrick Pérez
First: 2026-07-06T17:31:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:31:52+00:00
Comments: Technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the first multiplayer world model for highly dynamic environments governed by complex physical interactions. Whereas single-player world models treat the other agents as part of the environment, ours conditions on the action streams of multiple agents, learning to attribute changes in the scene to the correct player and to stay coherent under arbitrary combinations of their actions. We study this problem in the game of Rocket League, where players compete and cooperate under fast, tightly coupled dynamics. Trained on 10,000 hours of gameplay collected with publicly available bots, our 5-billion-parameter latent diffusion model generates four-player matches in real time, producing 20 frames per second on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Although trained only on short clips, its rollouts stay stable far beyond the training horizon: distributional quality holds steady out to five minutes, the longest horizon we measure, and in practice we observe rollouts continuing for hours with no sign of collapse. We systematically investigate the central design choices: the video codec, the generative objective, and the multiplayer conditioning scheme. In addition, we characterize how behavior changes with model and data scale, including the capabilities that emerge and the failure modes that persist. We further develop targeted evaluations that probe the model's physical understanding rather than visual appearance alone. To support continued research on multiplayer world models, we release our dataset, our full training and inference codebase, and a live demo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于表示自编码器的多人交互世界模型
我们提出了首个适用于高度动态环境（由复杂物理交互支配）的多人世界模型。与单人世界模型将其他智能体视为环境一部分不同，我们的模型根据多个智能体的动作流进行条件建模，学习将场景变化归因于正确的玩家，并在任意组合动作下保持一致性。我们在《火箭联盟》游戏中研究该问题，玩家在此游戏中需在快速、紧密耦合的动态下竞争与协作。该模型在10,000小时公开可用机器人收集的游戏数据上训练，其50亿参数的潜在扩散模型可在单块Nvidia B200 GPU上实时生成四人对战，帧率可达每秒20帧。尽管仅在短片段上训练，其预测稳定性远超训练时长：分布质量稳定维持至五分钟（我们测量的最长时长），实际观察中预测可持续数小时而不崩溃。我们系统性研究了核心设计选择：视频编解码器、生成目标及多人条件化方案。此外，我们分析了模型与数据规模如何影响行为，包括涌现能力与持续存在的失败模式。我们进一步开发了针对性评估方法，以探测模型的物理理解能力而非仅视觉外观。为支持多人世界模型的持续研究，我们公开了数据集、完整训练与推理代码库及实时演示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model complex, multi-agent environments with realistic physical dynamics, the authors propose a multiplayer world model that conditions on action streams from multiple agents to attribute scene changes correctly and maintain coherence under arbitrary interactions. Trained on 10,000 hours of Rocket League gameplay with bots, their 5-billion-parameter latent diffusion model generates real-time four-player matches at 20 FPS on a single B200 GPU. Despite training on short clips, the model’s rollouts remain stable for up to five minutes and often persist for hours without collapse. The study systematically evaluates design choices including video codecs, generative objectives, and multiplayer conditioning, while also analyzing how scale affects capabilities and failure modes, and introduces evaluations focused on physical reasoning rather than visual fidelity.
为应对复杂动态环境中多智能体交互的建模需求，作者提出一种基于表示自编码器的多人世界模型，通过条件化多个智能体的动作流来准确归因场景变化。该模型在1万小时的火箭联盟游戏数据（由公开机器人收集）上训练，拥有50亿参数的潜在扩散架构，可在单张B200显卡上实时生成四人对战，帧率20FPS。尽管仅训练短片段，其预测仍稳定持续至五分钟（最长测量时长），实际运行中可维持数小时而不崩溃。研究系统评估了视频编解码器、生成目标与条件化方案，分析了模型与数据规模增长带来的能力涌现与失效模式，并设计了聚焦物理理解而非视觉外观的评估方法，同时开源数据集、代码与演示以支持后续研究。
Beyond Isolated Objects: Relationship-aware Open Vocabulary Scene Understanding via 3D Scene Graph Analysis
Authors: Xianhao Chen, Jiarui Hu, Yuanbo Yang, Xiyu Zhang, Tengyue Wang, Hujun Bao, Guofeng Zhang, Zhaopeng Cui
First: 2026-07-06T17:29:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:29:49+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cxavireh.github.io/relgraphov-projectpage
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding aims to segment 3D scenes beyond predefined categories by transferring semantic knowledge from vision-language models. Existing methods have advanced this task by lifting language-aligned 2D features into 3D, yet they often rely on context-independent semantic representations, leaving object relationships underexplored for contextual refinement. We propose RelGraphOV, a relationship-aware framework that uses 3D scene graphs to enhance open-vocabulary 3D understanding. Our method constructs relational scene graphs from multi-view observations by leveraging vision-language reasoning to infer object relationships and prune geometrically implausible connections, without manual relationship annotations. To aggregate relational context while avoiding feature interference, we introduce an Adaptive Gated Dual-Stream Contextual GAT that separates dense geometric features and semantic CLIP embeddings, performs edge-guided message passing, and adaptively fuses complementary semantics. A hierarchical contrastive objective further promotes instance-level consistency and category-level discrimination. Experiments on ScanNetV2, ScanNet200, ScanNet$++$, and Replica demonstrate strong performance and generalization ability. Project Page: https://cxavireh.github.io/relgraphov-projectpage
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越孤立物体：通过3D场景图分析实现关系感知的开放词汇场景理解
开放词汇3D场景理解旨在通过从视觉-语言模型迁移语义知识，对超出预定义类别的3D场景进行分割。现有方法通过将语言对齐的2D特征提升至3D空间推进了该任务，但常依赖上下文无关的语义表示，导致物体关系在上下文细化中被忽视。我们提出RelGraphOV，一种关系感知框架，利用3D场景图增强开放词汇3D理解。该方法通过视觉-语言推理从多视角观测构建关系场景图，推断物体关系并剪枝几何上不合理的连接，无需人工标注关系。为聚合关系上下文并避免特征干扰，我们引入自适应门控双流上下文GAT，分离密集几何特征与语义CLIP嵌入，执行边引导的消息传递，并自适应融合互补语义。分层对比目标进一步促进实例级一致性与类别级区分度。在ScanNetV2、ScanNet200、ScanNet++和Replica上的实验验证了其强大性能与泛化能力。项目页面：https://cxavireh.github.io/relgraphov-projectpage
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding methods that ignore object relationships, this paper introduces RelGraphOV, a relationship-aware framework that constructs 3D scene graphs from multi-view observations using vision-language reasoning to infer plausible object connections while pruning geometrically implausible ones. The method employs an Adaptive Gated Dual-Stream Contextual GAT to separate geometric and semantic features, perform edge-guided message passing, and adaptively fuse them, while a hierarchical contrastive loss ensures instance-level consistency and category-level discrimination. Experiments on ScanNetV2, ScanNet200, ScanNet++, and Replica show that RelGraphOV achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across diverse scenes without requiring manual relationship annotations.
为提升开放词汇3D场景理解中对物体关系的建模能力，作者提出RelGraphOV框架，通过多视角观测结合视觉-语言推理构建3D场景图，自动推断并剪枝几何上不合理的物体连接。该方法采用自适应门控双流上下文GAT，分离几何与语义特征，进行边引导的消息传递并自适应融合互补语义，同时引入层次对比损失提升实例一致性与类别区分度。在ScanNetV2、ScanNet200、ScanNet++和Replica等数据集上的实验表明，该方法在无需人工标注关系的前提下，展现出优异的性能与泛化能力。
WildSplat: Feedforward Gaussian Splatting from Unposed In-the-Wild Images
Authors: Xiyu Zhang, Jingyu Zhuang, Hongjia Zhai, Zizheng Yan, Jinwei Chen, Guofeng Zhang, Qingnan Fan
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-06T17:29:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:29:44+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures; Accepted by ECCV 2026. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/wildsplat/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While feedforward 3D reconstruction excels at efficient novel view synthesis, it typically falters when faced with scenes under varying illumination. To this end, we introduce WildSplat, the first feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting framework capable of appearance-conditioned novel-view synthesis for unposed in-the-wild images. To handle inconsistent photometric conditions, we propose a dual-branch architecture that explicitly decouples geometry from appearance. The geometry branch extracts an appearance-invariant 3D structure and jointly predicts camera poses. To govern the rendering appearance, the appearance branch injects target appearance cues into the content features via a globally pre-modulated cross-attention mechanism. To further prevent feature entanglement, we introduce a joint multi-reference training strategy that stabilizes the training process. Extensive experiments show that WildSplat surpasses existing optimization-based and feedforward methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in in-the-wild novel view synthesis and appearance editing from sparse inputs in a single forward pass.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WildSplat：从野外无姿势图像中实现前馈高斯点云渲染
尽管前馈3D重建在高效新视角合成方面表现优异，但在面对光照变化的场景时通常表现不佳。为此，我们提出了WildSplat，这是首个能够对野外无姿势图像进行外观条件化新视角合成的前馈3D高斯点云框架。为处理不一致的光度条件，我们提出了一种双分支架构，显式地将几何与外观解耦。几何分支提取外观不变的3D结构并联合预测相机位姿。为控制渲染外观，外观分支通过全局预调制的交叉注意力机制将目标外观线索注入内容特征。为进一步防止特征纠缠，我们引入了联合多参考训练策略以稳定训练过程。大量实验表明，WildSplat超越了现有基于优化的方法和前馈方法，在单次前向传播中实现了野外新视角合成与稀疏输入外观编辑的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of feedforward 3D reconstruction under varying illumination in unposed in-the-wild images, WildSplat introduces a dual-branch architecture that decouples geometry and appearance. The geometry branch learns an appearance-invariant 3D structure and jointly predicts camera poses, while the appearance branch uses a globally pre-modulated cross-attention mechanism to inject target appearance cues into content features. To prevent feature entanglement and stabilize training, a joint multi-reference strategy is employed. Experimental results demonstrate that WildSplat outperforms both optimization-based and feedforward methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in novel view synthesis and appearance editing from sparse inputs in a single forward pass.
为解决真实场景中光照变化导致的视图合成难题，WildSplat提出首个面向野外无姿态图像的前馈高斯点云框架。其采用双分支结构：几何分支提取光照无关的3D结构并联合预测相机位姿，外观分支通过全局预调制交叉注意力机制注入目标外观线索至内容特征。为防止特征混叠并稳定训练，引入联合多参考训练策略。实验表明，WildSplat在单次前向传播下显著超越现有优化与前馈方法，实现野外新视角合成与外观编辑的最先进性能。
WorldRoamBench: An Open-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Stability of Interactive World Models
Authors: Ting-Bing Xu, Jiacheng Sui, Zhe Gao, Kewei Shi, Wenjin Yang, Zhicheng Liu, Zhaoxu Sun, Mingchao Sun, Hongyu Pan, Fan Jiang, Mu Xu, Qi Fan, Yang Gao, Yong Li, Baoquan Chen
First: 2026-06-30T13:51:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:27:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite rapid progress in interactive world models (IWMs), existing benchmarks evaluate action following only at trajectory level and ignore memory and interaction physics. We introduce WorldRoamBench, an open-world benchmark for long-horizon stability across four dimensions, each with tailored innovations: (i) Action: per-frame action metric bypassing cross-model semantic scale disparity and exposing failures hidden by trajectory; (ii) Vision: segment-based drift metric capturing non-monotonic mid-sequence collapse missed by start-vs-end comparisons; (iii) Physics: controllability-gated evaluation over mechanics, optics, and 3D consistency, scoring plausibility under faithful action execution; (iv) Memory: action-decoupled protocol evaluating scene memory via transition-localized 3D point-cloud reconstruction and subject memory via tracking-plus-VLM reasoning. The benchmark comprises 600+ test cases across Nature, Urban, and Indoor scenes in first/third-person views with WASD 10-60s continuous interaction. Evaluating 10+ open/closed-source models reveals none reliably satisfies all dimensions; even the best achieves only moderate scores. Advances on WorldRoamBench are steps toward IWMs that are stable, physically grounded, memory-faithful, and deployable in real-world applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldRoamBench：面向交互式世界模型长时稳定性的开放世界基准
尽管交互式世界模型（IWMs）进展迅速，现有基准仅在轨迹层面评估动作跟随，忽略记忆与交互物理。我们提出WorldRoamBench，一个在四个维度上评估长时稳定性的开放世界基准，每个维度均有定制创新：(i) 动作：帧级动作度量绕过跨模型语义尺度差异，揭示轨迹层面隐藏的失败；(ii) 视觉：基于片段的漂移度量捕捉非单调序列中间崩溃，弥补起止对比的不足；(iii) 物理：通过力学、光学和3D一致性进行可控性门控评估，衡量忠实动作执行下的合理性；(iv) 记忆：动作解耦协议通过过渡局部化3D点云重建评估场景记忆，通过跟踪+视觉语言模型推理评估主体记忆。该基准包含600多个测试案例，涵盖自然、城市和室内场景，支持第一/第三人称视角及WASD 10-60秒连续交互。评估10多个开源/闭源模型发现，无一能可靠满足所有维度；即使表现最佳者也仅取得中等分数。WorldRoamBench的进展是迈向稳定、物理可信、记忆忠实且可部署于现实应用的IWMs的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of comprehensive evaluation for long-horizon stability in interactive world models, the authors introduce WorldRoamBench, a benchmark assessing models across four dimensions: action fidelity, visual drift, physics plausibility, and memory retention. Each dimension employs specialized metrics—such as per-frame action scoring, segment-based vision drift, controllability-gated physics evaluation, and action-decoupled memory reconstruction—to expose failures overlooked by traditional trajectory-level benchmarks. Evaluated on over 600 test cases across diverse environments with continuous 10–60s interactions, the benchmark reveals that none of the 10+ tested models consistently performs well across all dimensions, with the best achieving only moderate scores, highlighting the current gap in robust, real-world deployable IWMs.
为解决现有交互式世界模型在长时序稳定性评估上的不足，本文提出WorldRoamBench开放世界基准，从动作、视觉、物理和记忆四个维度对模型进行评估。每个维度采用针对性指标：如帧级动作评分、分段视觉漂移检测、可控性门控物理评估、动作解耦的记忆重建，以揭示传统轨迹级评估忽略的缺陷。在600多个涵盖自然、城市和室内场景的测试案例中，即使表现最好的模型也仅获得中等分数，表明当前模型在稳定性、物理一致性与记忆保真度方面仍存在明显短板，亟需更鲁棒的交互式世界模型。
OptiAgent: End-to-End Optimization Modeling via Multi-Agent Iterative Refinement
Authors: Adriana Laurindo Monteiro, Nayse Fagundes, Gabriel Mattos Langeloh, Gustavo de Oliveira Kanno, Priscila Louise Aguirre, Thiago Costa Rizuti da Rocha, Victor Leme Beltran
First: 2026-07-06T17:27:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:27:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose OptiAgent, a multi-agent framework that, given a natural language description of an Operations Research problem, is able to output a solver-ready mathematical formulation as well as executable code. Our architecture prioritizes the mathematical modeling step, where dedicated agents extract structures, such as decision variables and constraints, enabling iterative self-correction. We introduce a novel multi-loop validation architecture with four specialized feedback mechanisms, each targeting a distinct failure mode such as misinterpretation, structural defects, mathematical inconsistencies, validation failures, and code errors. Alongside accuracy, our modular design improves the process of solving optimization problems by improving transparency, as each agent exposes its reasoning and feedback, making the full modeling process auditable. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3 out of 4 benchmarks across LP, MILP, and Nonlinear Programming tasks, while remaining highly competitive on the remaining dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OptiAgent：通过多智能体迭代优化实现端到端优化建模
我们提出了OptiAgent，一个能够根据运筹学问题的自然语言描述，输出可求解的数学公式及可执行代码的多智能体框架。我们的架构优先处理数学建模步骤，其中专用智能体提取决策变量和约束等结构，支持迭代自我修正。我们引入了一种新颖的多循环验证架构，包含四种专门的反馈机制，分别针对误解、结构缺陷、数学不一致、验证失败和代码错误等不同故障模式。除了准确性外，我们的模块化设计通过使每个智能体暴露其推理和反馈，提升了优化问题求解过程的透明度，使整个建模过程可审计。该框架在LP、MILP和非线性规划任务的四个基准测试中，有三个达到当前最优性能，其余数据集表现也极具竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for automated, transparent, and accurate mathematical modeling of Operations Research problems from natural language, OptiAgent employs a multi-agent iterative refinement system where specialized agents extract decision variables and constraints, then iteratively self-correct using four feedback mechanisms targeting misinterpretation, structural flaws, mathematical inconsistencies, and code errors. Experimental results show the framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on three out of four benchmarks across linear, mixed-integer, and nonlinear programming tasks, while remaining competitive on the remaining dataset, and enhances model transparency by exposing agent reasoning throughout the process.
为实现从自然语言自动、透明且准确地建模运筹学问题，OptiAgent采用多智能体迭代优化框架，各专用智能体提取决策变量与约束，并通过四个反馈机制（针对误解、结构缺陷、数学不一致及代码错误）进行迭代自校正。实验表明，该框架在四项基准中的三项上达到线性规划、混合整数规划及非线性规划任务的最先进性能，其余一项仍保持高度竞争力，同时通过暴露各智能体推理过程，显著提升了建模过程的可审计性。
Multilinguality at the Edge: Developing Language Models for the Global South
Authors: Lester James V. Miranda, Songbo Hu, Roi Reichart, Anna Korhonen
First: 2026-04-23T12:53:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:24:47+00:00
Comments: Updated formatting and improved spacing. Project website is in https://ljvmiranda921.github.io/multilinguality-at-the-edge/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Where and how language models (LMs) are deployed determines who can benefit from them. However, there are several challenges that prevent effective deployment of LMs in non-English-speaking and hardware constrained communities in the Global South. We call this challenge the last mile: the intersection of multilinguality and edge deployment, where the goals are aligned but the technical requirements often compete. Studying these two fields together is both a need, as linguistically diverse communities often face the most severe infrastructure constraints, and an opportunity, as edge and multilingual NLP research remain largely siloed. To understand the state of the art and the challenges of combining the two areas, we survey 232 papers that tackle this problem across the language modelling pipeline, from data collection to development and deployment. We also discuss open questions and provide actionable recommendations for different stakeholders in the NLP ecosystem. Finally, we hope that this work contributes to the development of inclusive and equitable language technologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：边缘多语言：为全球南方开发语言模型
语言模型（LMs）的部署地点和方式决定了谁能够从中受益。然而，在全球南方非英语使用者和硬件受限社区中，LMs的有效部署面临诸多挑战。我们称这一挑战为“最后一公里”：多语言与边缘部署的交汇点，目标一致但技术要求常相互冲突。研究这两个领域结合的必要性在于，语言多样化的社区往往面临最严峻的基础设施限制；同时，这也是一个机遇，因为边缘计算与多语言NLP研究仍处于相对独立的状态。为了解这两个领域结合的现状与挑战，我们调研了232篇涵盖语言建模全流程（从数据收集到开发与部署）的相关论文。我们还讨论了开放性问题，并为NLP生态系统中的不同利益相关者提供了可操作的建议。最后，我们希望本工作能促进包容性与公平性的语言技术发展。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of deploying language models in resource-limited, multilingual communities in the Global South, termed the 'last mile' problem where multilinguality and edge deployment goals conflict. The authors conduct a comprehensive survey of 232 papers covering the entire language modeling pipeline—from data collection to deployment—to map current practices, identify technical bottlenecks, and highlight gaps in research. Their analysis reveals that while edge deployment and multilingual NLP remain largely siloed, combining them offers both necessity and opportunity, especially for linguistically diverse populations facing severe infrastructure constraints. The study concludes with actionable recommendations for stakeholders to foster more inclusive and equitable language technologies.
该论文聚焦于在资源匮乏、多语言的全球南方地区部署语言模型所面临的‘最后一公里’挑战，即多语言能力与边缘计算部署目标之间的冲突。作者调研了涵盖数据收集到部署全流程的232篇相关论文，系统梳理当前技术现状与瓶颈，发现低资源语言的边缘优化模型严重缺失，基础设施限制是主要障碍。研究最后提出面向研究者、开发者和政策制定者的可操作建议，旨在推动更具包容性和公平性的语言技术发展。
CLARITY: Medical World Model for Guiding Treatment Decisions by Modeling Context-Aware Disease Trajectories in Latent Space
Authors: Tianxingjian Ding, Yuanhao Zou, Chen Chen, Mubarak Shah, Yu Tian
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2025-12-08T20:42:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:23:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical decision-making in oncology requires predicting dynamic disease evolution, a task current static AI predictors cannot perform. While world models (WMs) offer a paradigm for generative prediction, existing medical applications remain limited. Existing methods often rely on stochastic diffusion models, focusing on visual reconstruction rather than causal, physiological transitions. Furthermore, in medical domain, models like MeWM typically ignore patient-specific temporal and clinical contexts and lack a feedback mechanism to link predictions to treatment decisions. To address these gaps, we introduce CLARITY, a medical world model that forecasts disease evolution directly within a structured latent space. It explicitly integrates time intervals (temporal context) and patient-specific data (clinical context) to model treatment-conditioned progression as a smooth, interpretable trajectory, and thus generate physiologically faithful, individualized treatment plans. Finally, CLARITY introduces a novel prediction-to-decision framework, translating latent rollouts into transparent, actionable recommendations. CLARITY demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in treatment planning. On the MU-Glioma-Post dataset, our approach outperforms recent MeWM by 12\%, and significantly surpasses all other medical-specific large language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLARITY：用于指导治疗决策的医学世界模型，通过在潜在空间中建模情境感知的疾病轨迹
肿瘤学临床决策需要预测疾病动态演变，而当前静态AI预测器无法完成此任务。虽然世界模型（WMs）提供了一种生成式预测范式，但现有医学应用仍有限。现有方法常依赖随机扩散模型，侧重视觉重建而非因果、生理学上的转变。此外，在医学领域，如MeWM等模型通常忽略患者特异性的时间和临床情境，且缺乏将预测与治疗决策关联的反馈机制。为解决这些不足，我们提出了CLARITY，一种在结构化潜在空间内直接预测疾病演化的医学世界模型。它明确整合时间间隔（时间情境）和患者特异性数据（临床情境），将治疗条件下的疾病进展建模为平滑、可解释的轨迹，从而生成符合生理学、个性化的治疗方案。最后，CLARITY引入了一种新颖的预测到决策框架，将潜在轨迹转化为透明、可执行的推荐。CLARITY在治疗规划中展现出最先进性能。在MU-Glioma-Post数据集上，我们的方法比近期MeWM高出12%，并显著超越所有其他医学专用大语言模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dynamic disease prediction in oncology, CLARITY introduces a medical world model that forecasts disease progression within a structured latent space, integrating temporal and clinical context to generate individualized, physiologically faithful treatment plans. Unlike prior methods that rely on diffusion models for visual reconstruction or ignore patient-specific temporal dynamics, CLARITY models treatment-conditioned disease trajectories as smooth, interpretable paths. Its novel prediction-to-decision framework translates latent rollouts into actionable recommendations, achieving state-of-the-art performance: on the MU-Glioma-Post dataset, it outperforms recent MeWM by 12% and all other medical-specific large language models.
为解决肿瘤学中动态疾病预测的需求，CLARITY提出一种医学世界模型，在结构化潜在空间中预测疾病进展，融合时间与临床上下文以生成个体化、生理学上可信的治疗方案。与依赖扩散模型进行视觉重建或忽略患者特异性时间动态的现有方法不同，CLARITY将治疗条件下的疾病轨迹建模为平滑、可解释的路径。其创新的预测到决策框架可将潜在轨迹转化为可执行的建议。实验表明，CLARITY在MU-Glioma-Post数据集上表现最佳，比最新MeWM高12%，显著超越所有其他医学大语言模型。
Signal Structure-Aware Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Weiyi Xue, Fan Lu, Chi Zhang, Tianhang Wang, Sanqing Qu, Zehan Zheng, Boyuan Zheng, Junqiao Zhao, Guang Chen
First: 2026-07-02T04:41:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:22:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting has demonstrated remarkable potential in novel view synthesis. In contrast to small-scale scenes, large-scale scenes inevitably contain sparsely observed regions with excessively sparse initial points. In this case, supervising Gaussians initialized from low-frequency sparse points with high-frequency images often induces uncontrolled densification and redundant primitives, degrading both efficiency and quality. Intuitively, this issue can be mitigated with scheduling strategies, which can be categorized into two paradigms: modulating target signal frequency via densification and modulating sampling frequency via image resolution. However, previous scheduling strategies are primarily hardcoded, failing to perceive the convergence behavior of scene frequency. To address this, we reframe the scene reconstruction problem from the perspective of signal structure recovery and propose SIG, a novel scheduler that synchronizes image supervision with Gaussian frequencies. Specifically, we derive the average sampling frequency and bandwidth of 3D representations, and then regulate the training image resolution and the Gaussian densification process based on scene frequency convergence. Furthermore, we introduce Sphere-Constrained Gaussians, which leverage the spatial prior of initialized point clouds to control Gaussian optimization. Our framework enables frequency-consistent, geometry-aware, and floater-free training, achieving state-of-the-art performance by a substantial margin in both efficiency and rendering quality in large-scale scenes. The code is available at: https://github.com/weiyixue999/Signal_Structure_Aware_Gaussian
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大规模场景重建的信号结构感知高斯点绘
3D高斯点绘在新视角合成中展现出巨大潜力。与小规模场景不同，大规模场景必然包含观测稀疏、初始点过于稀疏的区域。此时，若用高频图像监督由低频稀疏点初始化的高斯点，常导致无控制的密集化和冗余基元，降低效率与质量。直观上，该问题可通过调度策略缓解，可分为两类：通过密集化调节目标信号频率，或通过图像分辨率调节采样频率。但此前调度策略多为硬编码，无法感知场景频率的收敛行为。为此，我们从信号结构恢复视角重构场景重建问题，提出SIG——一种同步图像监督与高斯频率的新调度器。具体而言，我们推导3D表示的平均采样频率与带宽，再依据场景频率收敛情况调节训练图像分辨率与高斯密集化过程。此外，我们引入球面约束高斯点，利用初始化点云的空间先验控制高斯优化。该框架实现频率一致、几何感知、无漂浮点训练，于大规模场景中显著提升效率与渲染质量，达到当前最优水平。代码见：https://github.com/weiyixue999/Signal_Structure_Aware_Gaussian
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and quality degradation in large-scale scene reconstruction using 3D Gaussian Splatting due to sparse initial points and mismatched supervision, this work proposes SIG, a scheduler that aligns image resolution and Gaussian densification with scene frequency convergence. By estimating average sampling frequency and bandwidth of 3D representations, SIG dynamically adjusts training parameters to avoid uncontrolled densification and redundant primitives. Additionally, Sphere-Constrained Gaussians are introduced to leverage spatial priors from initial point clouds, enabling geometry-aware optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that SIG achieves state-of-the-art performance in both rendering quality and training efficiency across large-scale scenes without introducing floating artifacts.
为了解决大规模场景重建中因初始点稀疏导致的3D高斯点云过度密集化和冗余问题，本文提出SIG调度器，通过感知场景频率收敛动态调节图像分辨率与高斯点云密度，避免监督信号与频率不匹配。同时引入球面约束高斯点，利用初始点云的空间先验引导优化。实验表明，SIG在效率与渲染质量上显著优于现有方法，在大规模场景重建中达到当前最优性能。
TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine
Authors: Yuanda Xu, Zhengze Zhou, Kayhan Behdin, Jelena Markovic-Voronov, Hejian Sang, Xiaomin Li, Wenhui Zhu, Xinchen Du, Aida Rahmattalabi, Ran He, Sen Na, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
First: 2026-07-06T17:21:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:21:16+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TREK：蒸馏以探索，强化以精炼
当当前策略已能采样到有用的推理轨迹时，组相对策略优化（GRPO）效果显著，但在硬提示（其正确解法模式超出学生策略支持范围）上会停滞。我们提出TREK（通过前向KL实现教师引导探索），这是一种简单的分阶段流程，利用蒸馏并非用于模仿，而是扩展探索支持。TREK的关键优势在于其通用性：因其仅消耗已验证的输出轨迹，可使用外部黑盒教师、白盒教师，或在额外推理上下文支持下使用同一模型，且即使教师内部结构不可用，也能高效识别哪些硬提示样本最值得整合。TREK首先识别学生无辅助时通过率极低的提示，查询提案源生成已验证候选解，保留当前学生似然度排名前r的提案，应用短时前向KL阶段将这些验证模式拉入学生支持范围，然后回归标准的基于策略的GRPO精炼。在数学推理方面，使用DeepSeek-V4提案的TREK在AIME 2024和AIME 2025测试中提升了Qwen3系列模型在所有规模上的表现；对Qwen3-8B，AIME 2025从36.9提升至40.3，AIME 2024从47.9提升至51.1（avg@16），而自上下文变体在无外部教师情况下分别达到38.5和49.6。在代理任务方面，TREK将ALFWorld成功率从75.8提升至82.8，ScienceWorld从12.5提升至26.7；值得注意的是，在最难的任务类型上，TREK在训练早期即实现高成功率，而无辅助的GRPO需大量优化步骤才能达到相当水平。
Summary / 总结
TREK addresses the limitation of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) in handling hard prompts whose correct solutions lie outside the student model’s current policy support. It introduces a staged procedure using distillation not for imitation but to expand exploration by pulling verified solution trajectories into the student’s support via forward-KL. TREK leverages external or self-generated verified proposals, ranks them by student likelihood, and refines the student policy using GRPO after expansion. Experiments show TREK significantly boosts performance: on AIME 2025, Qwen3-8B’s score rises from 36.9 to 40.3, and on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, success rates jump from 75.8 to 82.8 and 12.5 to 26.7 respectively, often achieving high performance earlier than unaided GRPO.
TREK 解决了 GRPO 在处理正确解超出学生模型策略支持范围的困难提示时的停滞问题。它采用分阶段方法，利用蒸馏扩展探索能力，通过外部或自生成的已验证输出轨迹引导学生模型。首先识别表现差的提示，获取候选解，按当前学生模型似然排序，用前向 KL 阶段将高似然解拉入策略支持，再回归标准 GRPO 优化。实验表明，TREK 显著提升性能：在 AIME 2025 上，Qwen3-8B 从 36.9 提升至 40.3（avg@16）；在代理任务中，ALFWorld 成功率从 75.8 提升至 82.8，ScienceWorld 从 12.5 提升至 26.7，且在最难任务类型上早期即取得高成功率。
AI's Blind Spots: Geographic Knowledge and Diversity Deficit in Generated Urban Scenario
Authors: Ciro Beneduce, Massimiliano Luca, Bruno Lepri
First: 2025-06-20T10:43:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:20:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based text-to-image models are increasingly used for urban analysis and scenario generation, but their geographic knowledge and representational biases remain poorly understood. We evaluate FLUX 1-schnell and Stable Diffusion 3.5-Large in the United States by generating 150 street-view images for each state, each state capital, and a generic "USA" prompt. Images are embedded with DINO-v2 ViT-S/14 and compared with Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). Pairwise FID clustering shows that geographically proximate states and capitals often group together, indicating implicit geographic structure. However, the generic ``USA'' prompt collapses this diversity into a metropolitan stereotype: frontier, desert, tropical, rural, and small-city environments are underrepresented or distant in FID space. These results show that diffusion models can encode fine-grained geography while still reproducing narrow national-scale visual stereotypes.

Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Xianqiang Gao, Qizhi Chen, Delin Qu, Haoming Song, Zhigang Wang, Bin Zhao, Dong Wang, Xuelong Li
First: 2026-05-26T17:26:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:18:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant evidence and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose Q-GeoMem, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. Q-GeoMem injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. For each candidate frame, a calibrated Q-Former estimates question relevance, while novelty and evidence utility are recomputed with respect to the active evidence bank. The resulting relevance-novelty utility controls capacity-based replacement and serves as an attention bias during memory reading. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Extensive experiments across two in-domain and five out-of-distribution benchmarks, and controlled memory analyses show that Q-GeoMem achieves state-of-the-art performance in the evaluated settings and validate the effectiveness of question-guided geometric evidence selection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Q-GeoMem：面向视频空间推理的问答引导几何记忆
视频空间推理需要在时间上累积视角相关的证据，同时保留对当前问题有用的信息。现有空间视频-语言模型虽提升了几何感知与长程上下文建模能力，但常将记忆视为通用时间缓存，可能引入冗余或无关证据，削弱长程推理能力。我们提出Q-GeoMem，一种面向视频空间推理的问答引导几何记忆框架。Q-GeoMem将相机条件几何注入视觉标记，并维护两种互补记忆：细粒度上下文库用于存储近期密集特征与相机状态，语义-几何证据库用于存储紧凑的长程证据。对每个候选帧，校准后的Q-Former评估问题相关性，同时根据当前活跃证据库重新计算新颖性与证据效用。所得相关性-新颖性效用控制基于容量的替换策略，并在记忆读取时作为注意力偏置。推理过程中，两种记忆在更新前均被读取，并自适应融合当前帧表示。在两个领域内及五个分布外基准上的大量实验与受控记忆分析表明，Q-GeoMem在评估设置中达到最先进性能，验证了问答引导几何证据选择的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to retain viewpoint-dependent evidence relevant to questions in video spatial reasoning, Q-GeoMem introduces a question-guided geometric memory framework that separates and manages two complementary memory banks: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent visual and camera data, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range information. For each frame, a calibrated Q-Former evaluates question relevance, while novelty and utility are dynamically assessed against the active evidence bank to guide memory replacement and attention bias. Experimental results across seven benchmarks demonstrate that Q-GeoMem achieves state-of-the-art performance and confirms the effectiveness of its question-driven evidence selection mechanism.
为提升视频空间推理中对问题相关视角证据的精准保留，Q-GeoMem提出一种基于问题引导的几何记忆框架，将相机条件几何注入视觉标记，并维护两个互补记忆库：细粒度上下文库用于存储近期密集特征与相机状态，语义-几何证据库用于压缩长期有效证据。对每个候选帧，校准的Q-Former评估问题相关性，同时根据当前活跃证据库动态重计算新颖性与证据效用，以指导容量替换和记忆读取时的注意力偏置。推理时，两个记忆库均被读取并自适应融合当前帧表示。在七个基准测试中，Q-GeoMem达到最先进性能，验证了其问题引导式证据选择机制的有效性。
PACE: A Proxy for Agentic Capability Evaluation
Authors: Yueqi Song, Lintang Sutawika, Jiarui Liu, Lindia Tjuatja, Jiayi Geng, Yunze Xiao, Daniel Lee, Aditya Bharat Soni, Vincent Lo, Xiang Yue, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-07-02T10:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:18:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating LLM agents on benchmarks like SWE-Bench and GAIA can be expensive, time-consuming, and requires complex infrastructure. A single evaluation can cost thousands of dollars and take days to complete. In contrast, non-agentic LLM benchmarks that test individual capabilities (e.g., reasoning, code generation) are fast and cheap to run. In this paper, we investigate whether performance on expensive agentic benchmarks can be accurately predicted by the performance on a small, carefully selected subset of atomic evaluation instances. We introduce PACE, a framework that constructs proxy benchmarks by selecting instances from existing non-agentic evaluations whose aggregate scores most reliably predict model performances on agentic benchmarks. Given a pool of candidate instances spanning atomic capabilities, PACE fits a regression that maps a model's scores on a compact subset of source instances to its score on the target agentic benchmark. The subset itself is curated by combining two complementary instance-selection strategies, target-relevance local selection and globally informative global selection. We apply PACE to the 4 target agentic benchmarks in this paper, which yields PACE-Bench, the concrete proxy benchmark that we evaluate in the paper. Experiments across 14 models, 4 agentic benchmarks, and 19 non-agentic benchmarks show that PACE-Bench predicts agentic scores with leave-one-out cross-validation (LOOCV) mean absolute error (MAE) under 4%, Spearman correlation above 0.80, and pairwise model-ranking accuracy around 85%, all at much less than 1% of the full agentic evaluation cost. We further analyze the selected proxy instances, revealing which skills each agentic benchmark uniquely demands. PACE enables practitioners to obtain reliable estimates of agentic performance during model development, selection, and routing, without the overhead of full agent evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PACE：代理能力评估的代理基准
在SWE-Bench和GAIA等基准上评估大语言模型代理成本高昂、耗时且需复杂基础设施，单次评估可能耗资数千美元并需数日。相比之下，测试个体能力（如推理、代码生成）的非代理型LLM基准快速且廉价。本文探究是否可通过少量精心挑选的原子评估实例表现，准确预测昂贵代理基准的表现。我们提出PACE框架，通过从现有非代理评估中选取聚合得分最可靠预测代理基准表现的实例，构建代理基准。给定覆盖原子能力的候选实例池，PACE拟合回归模型，将模型在紧凑子集上的得分映射到目标代理基准得分。该子集通过结合目标相关局部选择与全局信息性全局选择两种互补策略精心挑选。我们将PACE应用于本文的4个目标代理基准，得到PACE-Bench——本文评估的具体代理基准。在14个模型、4个代理基准和19个非代理基准上的实验表明，PACE-Bench在留一交叉验证（LOOCV）下平均绝对误差（MAE）低于4%，斯皮尔曼相关系数超过0.80，模型对偶排序准确率约85%，成本仅为完整代理评估的不到1%。我们进一步分析所选代理实例，揭示各代理基准独特所需技能。PACE使从业者可在模型开发、选择和路由过程中，无需完整代理评估的开销，获得可靠的代理性能估计。

Motivated by the high cost and time required to evaluate LLM agents on benchmarks like SWE-Bench and GAIA, this paper proposes PACE, a framework that predicts agentic performance using a small subset of atomic non-agentic tasks. PACE selects proxy instances via a dual strategy—target-relevance local selection and globally informative global selection—to build a compact benchmark (PACE-Bench) that maps model scores from cheap, fast evaluations to agentic performance. Experiments across 14 models and 4 agentic benchmarks show PACE-Bench achieves MAE under 4%, Spearman correlation above 0.80, and 85% model ranking accuracy, all at less than 1% of the full evaluation cost, enabling efficient performance estimation during model development.
为降低评估大语言模型代理能力的成本与时间，本文提出PACE框架，通过从原子非代理任务中精选少量实例来预测代理基准表现。该框架结合目标相关性与全局信息性两种实例选择策略，构建回归模型将精选实例得分映射至代理基准得分。实验表明，所构建的代理基准PACE-Bench在14个模型和四个代理基准上，留一交叉验证MAE低于4%，斯皮尔曼相关系数超0.80，模型排序准确率约85%，成本仅为完整评估的1%以下。
Generative Semantic Multi-Object Tracking: A Large-Scale Benchmark and an MLLM-Driven Reasoning Framework
Authors: Pan Liao, Feng Yang, Di Wu, Jinwen Yu, Wang Zhao, Dingwen Zhang
First: 2026-01-10T12:18:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:12:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Semantic Multi-Object Tracking (SMOT) is evolving from purely geometric localization toward comprehensive video understanding. However, existing paradigms predominantly rely on closed-set interaction tags and fragmented perception pipelines, creating a bottleneck that prevents the full utilization of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for dynamic scenes. In this paper, we elevate SMOT from rigid classification to an open-ended generative reasoning task. To support this paradigm shift, we introduce Grand-SMOT, a large-scale benchmark featuring high-density, dual-stream narratives. This dataset explicitly decouples micro-level individual dynamics from macro-level environmental contexts, directly resolving the semantic scarcity of prior tracking datasets. Furthermore, we propose LLMTrack, a unified MLLM-driven framework for dynamic SMOT. Guided by a verifiable ``\textit{Macro-Understanding-First}'' mechanism, LLMTrack employs a Spatio-Temporal Fusion Module to compress discrete geometric trajectories into continuous semantic tokens, effectively suppressing temporal hallucinations in long-sequence tracking. Extensive experiments, utilizing a novel decoupled evaluation protocol, validate that LLMTrack achieves state-of-the-art geometric tracking robustness while delivering a qualitative leap in generative semantic reasoning. The code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/liaopan-lp/LLMTrack-GrandSMOT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式语义多目标跟踪：大规模基准与基于MLLM的推理框架
语义多目标跟踪（SMOT）正从纯几何定位演进为全面的视频理解。然而，现有范式主要依赖封闭式交互标签和碎片化感知流程，造成瓶颈，阻碍了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在动态场景中的充分应用。本文将SMOT提升为开放式的生成式推理任务。为此，我们引入Grand-SMOT，一个具有高密度双流叙事的大规模基准数据集，明确分离微观个体动态与宏观环境语境，直接解决先前跟踪数据集语义稀缺的问题。此外，我们提出LLMTrack，一个统一的基于MLLM的动态SMOT框架。该框架采用可验证的“宏观理解优先”机制，通过时空融合模块将离散几何轨迹压缩为连续语义标记，有效抑制长序列跟踪中的时序幻觉。大量实验采用新颖的解耦评估协议验证，表明LLMTrack在几何跟踪鲁棒性上达到最先进水平，同时在生成式语义推理方面实现质的飞跃。代码与数据集公开于https://github.com/liaopan-lp/LLMTrack-GrandSMOT。

How Far is Too Far? Defining the Distance Threshold for Verification Siamese Networks
Authors: Heloísa Dias Viotto, Cauê Samonek, Lucas Garcia Pedroso, Marcos Sunye, André Abed Grégio, Paulo Lisboa de Almeida
First: 2026-07-06T17:06:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:06:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Siamese verification networks are widely used to compare items such as faces, cars, or signatures. In these scenarios, the network is trained to learn an embedding space in which similar objects are mapped closer together, while dissimilar objects are mapped further apart. Two objects are considered to belong to the same class (e.g., the same person in two different images) when the distance between their embeddings falls below a predefined threshold. Defining this threshold, however, is a non-trivial task and typically requires labeled data. In this work, we assume that the distribution of distances produced by a siamese verification network can be approximated by a bimodal function. Based on this assumption, we propose an unsupervised method to determine the verification threshold by identifying the minimum point between the two modes. The proposed approach does not require annotated samples, enabling the verification threshold to be updated directly in the deployment environment without the cost of manual labeling. We evaluate our method on four datasets: MNIST, CIFAR-10, LFW, and PKLot. The results indicate that the proposed approach achieves an average verification accuracy of 94%, comparable to the Equal Error Rate method, while eliminating the need for labeled data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多远算太远？定义验证孪生网络的距离阈值
孪生验证网络广泛用于比较人脸、汽车或签名等物品。在这些场景中，网络被训练以学习一个嵌入空间，其中相似物体被映射得更近，而不同物体则被映射得更远。当两个物体的嵌入距离低于预设阈值时，认为它们属于同一类别（例如，两张不同图像中的同一人）。然而，定义该阈值并非易事，通常需要标注数据。本文假设孪生验证网络产生的距离分布可近似为双峰函数。基于此假设，我们提出一种无监督方法，通过识别两个模式之间的最小点来确定验证阈值。该方法无需标注样本，可在部署环境中直接更新验证阈值，无需人工标注成本。我们在MNIST、CIFAR-10、LFW和PKLot四个数据集上评估该方法，结果表明，该方法平均验证准确率为94%，与等错误率方法相当，且无需标注数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of defining a verification threshold for Siamese networks without labeled data, this work proposes an unsupervised method that models the distance distribution between embeddings as bimodal and identifies the threshold at the minimum point between the two modes. Evaluated on four datasets—MNIST, CIFAR-10, LFW, and PKLot—the method achieves an average verification accuracy of 94%, matching the performance of the Equal Error Rate approach while eliminating the need for manual labeling or annotated samples.
该论文旨在无需标注数据的情况下设定Siamese网络的验证阈值，以避免人工标注的成本。作者假设嵌入距离分布呈双峰形态，提出一种无监督方法，通过识别两峰之间的最小点来确定阈值。在MNIST、CIFAR-10、LFW和PKLot四个数据集上的实验表明，该方法平均验证准确率达94%，与等错误率方法表现相当，且无需标注样本。
Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets
Authors: Nick Stracke, Kolja Bauer, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Miguel Angel Bautista, Josh Susskind, Björn Ommer
First: 2026-07-02T16:35:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:05:13+00:00
Comments: for code, view https://github.com/CompVis/set-learner
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：展示示例：从图像集合推断视觉概念
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能够遵循复杂的文本指令，但在纯视觉上下文中推理能力较弱。特别是，当前模型无法从示例图像集合中推断共享概念并将其应用于新输入。我们提出了“从图像集合推断视觉概念”（VICIS）任务，用于评估该能力。给定一组共享概念的示例图像和一个查询图像，模型必须生成新图像，使其保留上下文定义的概念，同时与查询图像保持一致。我们发现当前最先进的VLMs在该任务上表现不佳，常忽略视觉上下文或默认生成有偏结果。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种训练框架和架构，使模型能够从图像集合中推断视觉概念，并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据上的实验表明，我们的模型生成更准确、更多样化的输出，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如素描）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models in reasoning from purely visual context, this work introduces VICIS, a task that requires models to infer shared visual concepts from example image sets and generate new images consistent with both the concept and a query image. The authors propose a novel training framework and architecture designed to extract concept-specific embeddings from queries and learn to infer visual concepts from image sets. Experiments on synthetic and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet datasets demonstrate that their model significantly outperforms state-of-the-art VLMs, producing more accurate, diverse, and generalizable outputs across unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.
为解决视觉语言模型在纯视觉上下文推理中的不足，本文提出VICIS任务，要求模型从示例图像集中推断共享视觉概念，并生成与概念和查询图像一致的新图像。作者设计了一种新的训练框架和架构，旨在从查询中提取概念特定嵌入并生成保持概念的输出。在合成数据和大规模ImageNet/WordNet数据集上的实验表明，该模型显著优于现有视觉语言模型，生成结果更准确、多样，并能泛化到未见过的概念和模态（如素描）。
Real-Time Hard Negative Sampling via LLM-based Clustering for Large-Scale Two-Tower Retrieval
Authors: Ivan Ji, Liuyi Hu, Harrison, Zhao, Lei Huang, Qunshu Zhang, Max, Fan, Aameek Singh
First: 2026-07-01T05:07:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:02:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The two-tower model has been widely used for large-scale recommendation systems, particularly in the retrieval stage. Industry standards for training two-tower models typically involve in-batch and/or out-of-batch negative sampling. However, these methods often produce easy negatives that models can quickly learn, failing to sufficiently challenge the model. To address this issue, a novel self-supervised hard negative sampling technique is proposed that leverages a large language model (LLM) to generate hard negatives from the same cluster during model training. By utilizing the LLM to learn media representations, the proposed approach ensures that the generated negatives are more challenging and informative. This real-time sampling framework is designed for seamless integration into production models, capable of handling billions of training data points with minimal computational complexity. Experiments on public datasets, along with deployment to a large-scale online system, demonstrate that the proposed negative sampling technique outperforms widely used industry methods. Furthermore, analysis in industrial applications reveals that this sampling method can help break inherent feedback loops in recommendations and significantly reduce popularity bias.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型聚类的实时难负样本采样方法用于大规模双塔检索
双塔模型广泛应用于大规模推荐系统的检索阶段。业界训练双塔模型通常采用批内和/或批外负样本采样，但这些方法常生成模型易于学习的简单负样本，难以充分挑战模型。为此，本文提出一种新颖的自监督难负样本采样技术，利用大语言模型（LLM）在模型训练过程中从同一聚类中生成难负样本。通过利用LLM学习媒体表示，该方法确保生成的负样本更具挑战性和信息量。该实时采样框架专为无缝集成到生产模型设计，可高效处理数十亿级训练数据点，计算复杂度极低。在公开数据集上的实验及大规模在线系统部署结果表明，该负样本采样方法优于业界常用方法。此外，工业应用分析显示，该采样方法有助于打破推荐中的固有反馈循环，显著降低流行度偏差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional negative sampling methods in two-tower retrieval models that often yield easy negatives, this work proposes a real-time hard negative sampling technique using LLM-based clustering. The method clusters positive samples and leverages an LLM to generate challenging negatives within the same cluster during training, ensuring more informative and difficult samples for model learning. Experimental results on public datasets and real-world deployment show that this approach outperforms industry-standard sampling methods, improves retrieval performance, and mitigates popularity bias by breaking feedback loops in recommendation systems.
为解决传统负样本采样方法常生成简单负样本、难以充分训练模型的问题，本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的实时硬负样本采样方法。该方法利用LLM对数据进行聚类，并在训练过程中从同一簇内生成更具挑战性的负样本，提升模型区分能力。在公开数据集和大规模线上系统中的实验表明，该方法优于业界常用采样策略，同时有效缓解流行度偏差并打破推荐系统中的固有反馈循环。
CenSynCMB: Centre Maps and Physics-Guided Synthesis for Microbleed Detection
Authors: Lucas He, Hanyuan Zhang, Krinos Li, Adama Fatima Saccoh, Silvia Ingala, Rafael Rehwald, Marleen de Bruijne, Frederik Barkhof, Rhodri Davies, Carole H. Sudre
First: 2026-07-06T17:02:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:02:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cerebral microbleeds (CMBs) are MRI markers of small vessel disease and the microbleed component of amyloid related imaging abnormalities (ARIA-H), but their small size, sparsity, and similarity to vessels, calcification-like foci, and artefacts make automated detection difficult. We propose CenSynCMB, a centre-guided and mimic-aware framework combining a 3D Attention U-Net, auxiliary centre-map supervision, false-negative-driven reweighting, and fold-wise physics-guided synthesis of positive CMBs and labelled hard negatives. Synthetic data expose the detector to compact lesions and common mimics without validation or test leakage. On VALDO Task 2, CenSynCMB achieved the best local-comparison lesion-level F1 (74.3%, p = 0.020); on external AIBL SWI, it achieved the highest local-comparison recall (88.5%, p = 0.0058) and F1 (65.0%, p = 0.0016). Together, these results support scalable CMB candidate extraction in large, unlabelled MRI cohorts, while highlighting cohort-specific calibration as the next step toward reliable burden estimation.
