标题：CenSynCMB：基于中心图与物理引导合成的微出血检测方法
脑微出血（CMBs）是小血管疾病及淀粉样变性相关影像异常（ARIA-H）的MRI标志物，但因其体积小、分布稀疏且易与血管、钙化灶及伪影混淆，自动化检测困难。我们提出CenSynCMB框架，结合3D注意力U-Net、辅助中心图监督、假阴性驱动的重加权机制，以及基于折叠的物理引导合成阳性CMB与标注困难负样本。合成数据使检测器接触紧凑病灶与常见伪影，且无验证或测试数据泄露。在VALDO任务2中，CenSynCMB获得最佳局部比较病灶级F1值（74.3%，p=0.020）；在外部AIBL SWI数据上，其局部比较召回率最高（88.5%，p=0.0058）及F1值最高（65.0%，p=0.0016）。这些结果支持在大规模无标注MRI队列中可扩展的CMB候选提取，同时强调队列特异性校准是实现可靠负担评估的下一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of detecting cerebral microbleeds (CMBs) due to their small size and similarity to artifacts, the authors propose CenSynCMB, a physics-guided synthesis framework that combines a 3D Attention U-Net with centre-map supervision and false-negative reweighting. The method generates synthetic CMBs and hard negatives to enhance training without data leakage. Experiments on VALDO Task 2 and external AIBL SWI data show CenSynCMB achieved the highest lesion-level F1 (74.3%) and recall (88.5%), demonstrating its effectiveness in improving automated detection across datasets.
为了解决脑微出血（CMBs）因体积小、易与伪影混淆而难以自动检测的问题，作者提出CenSynCMB框架，结合3D注意力U-Net、中心图监督和假阴性重加权机制，并通过物理引导合成正样本和难负样本进行训练。实验在VALDO Task 2和外部AIBL SWI数据集上验证，结果显示该方法在病变级F1值（74.3%）和召回率（88.5%）上均表现最优，证明其在跨数据集自动检测中的有效性。
The Piggyback Hypothesis of Generalization: Explaining and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment
Authors: Jiachen Zhao, Zhengxuan Wu, Aryaman Arora, Yiyou Sun, David Bau, Weiyan Shi
First: 2026-06-04T19:32:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:01:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The mechanisms behind LLMs' broad over-generalization beyond training examples remain unclear. Emergent misalignment (EM) offers a striking case study: finetuning on narrow tasks induces broad misalignment to semantically-unrelated test domains. In this work, we propose the Piggyback Hypothesis: the chat-template tokens can piggyback the finetuned behaviour onto out-of-domain queries. We validate this hypothesis by showing that subtle perturbations to the prefix (tokens preceding all user queries), or patching the prefix representations with those from the unfinetuned model, can restore alignment without changing the user query. Building on this finding, we propose Token-Regularized Finetuning (TReFT), which regularizes specific token representations during training to mitigate EM. Across different models and multiple EM-inducing datasets, TReFT reduces EM while preserving in-domain learning. On Llama-3.1-8B finetuned on the legal domain, TReFT achieves 33.5% more EM reduction than data interleaving with a retain set of aligned examples. We further show that TReFT extends to other narrow-finetuning settings, including abstention, tool use, and refusal (off-topic generalization is reduced by 54.3% on average), supporting the Piggyback Hypothesis. Broadly, our work highlights that LLMs may learn and generalize in unintended ways and suggests a path toward more constrained finetuning. It also calls for further study of how shared input features can piggyback model behavior across domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：泛化中的搭便车假说：解释与缓解涌现错位
大型语言模型在训练示例之外广泛泛化的机制尚不明确。涌现错位（EM）提供了一个鲜明的案例：在狭窄任务上微调会导致对语义无关的测试领域产生广泛错位。本文提出“搭便车假说”：聊天模板令牌可将微调行为“搭便车”至域外查询。我们通过证明对前缀（所有用户查询前的令牌）进行细微扰动，或用未微调模型的前缀表示替换前缀表示，可在不改变用户查询的情况下恢复对齐。基于此发现，我们提出令牌正则化微调（TReFT），在训练过程中正则化特定令牌表示以缓解EM。在不同模型和多个EM诱导数据集上，TReFT在保持域内学习的同时减少EM。在针对法律领域微调的Llama-3.1-8B模型上，TReFT比数据交错搭配对齐示例的保留集实现33.5%更高的EM减少效果。我们进一步表明，TReFT适用于其他狭窄微调场景，包括拒绝、工具使用等（平均减少54.3%的离题泛化），支持搭便车假说。总体而言，我们的工作揭示了LLM可能以非预期方式学习和泛化，并提出了一条更受约束的微调路径，同时呼吁进一步研究共享输入特征如何跨领域“搭便车”模型行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unclear mechanisms behind large language models' over-generalization beyond training data, this work investigates emergent misalignment (EM), where fine-tuning on narrow tasks causes unintended misalignment to unrelated domains. The authors propose the Piggyback Hypothesis: chat-template prefix tokens can carry over fine-tuned behavior to out-of-domain queries. They validate this by showing that modifying or replacing prefix representations restores alignment without altering user input. Building on this, they introduce Token-Regularized Finetuning (TReFT), which constrains token representations during training to reduce EM. Experiments across models and datasets show TReFT significantly reduces EM while preserving in-domain performance, achieving 33.5% more reduction than data interleaving on legal-domain fine-tuning, and reducing off-topic generalization by 54.3% on average across abstention, tool use, and refusal tasks.
为了解释大语言模型在训练数据之外的广泛泛化机制，本研究聚焦于‘涌现错位’（EM）现象——即在狭窄任务上微调会导致模型在语义无关的测试域中产生错位。作者提出‘搭便车假设’，认为聊天模板前缀标记会将微调行为‘搭便车’传递到域外查询。通过修改前缀标记或用未微调模型的前缀表示替换，他们验证了该假设可恢复对齐而无需改变用户输入。在此基础上，他们提出‘标记正则化微调’（TReFT），在训练中约束特定标记表示以减少EM。实验表明，TReFT在法律领域微调中比数据交错+保留对齐样本的方法减少EM达33.5%，在其他狭窄微调场景（如拒绝、工具使用）中平均降低离题泛化54.3%，支持该假设并为更可控的微调提供路径。
Large Language Models Develop Novel Social Biases Through Adaptive Exploration
Authors: Addison J. Wu, Ryan Liu, Xuechunzi Bai, Thomas L. Griffiths
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral
First: 2025-11-08T21:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T17:00:36+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Oral
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) are adopted into frameworks that grant them the capacity to make real decisions, it is increasingly important to ensure that they are unbiased. In this paper, we argue that the predominant approach of simply removing existing biases from models is not enough. Using a paradigm from the psychology literature, we demonstrate that LLMs can spontaneously develop novel social biases about artificial demographic groups even when no inherent differences exist. These biases result in highly stratified task allocations, which are less fair than assignments by human participants and are exacerbated in newer and larger models. In humans, emergent biases like these have been shown to result from exploration-exploitation trade-offs, where the decision-maker explores too little, allowing early observations to strongly influence impressions about entire demographic groups. To alleviate this effect, we explore a series of interventions targeting model inputs, problem structure, and explicit steering. While most interventions have limited effect, explicitly incentivizing exploration robustly reduces stratification, highlighting the need for better multifaceted objectives to mitigate bias. These results reveal that LLMs are not merely passive mirrors of human social biases, but can actively create new ones from experience, raising urgent questions about how these systems will shape societies over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型通过适应性探索自发发展新的社会偏见
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）被纳入赋予其实际决策能力的框架，确保其无偏见变得愈发重要。本文认为，仅简单移除模型中现有偏见的方法不足以解决问题。我们借鉴心理学研究范式，证明即使人工人口群体间不存在固有差异，LLMs仍能自发发展出关于这些群体的新社会偏见，导致任务分配高度分层，其公平性低于人类参与者分配，且在较新、更大的模型中更为严重。在人类中，此类新兴偏见已被证明源于探索-利用权衡，即决策者探索不足，导致早期观察强烈影响对整个群体的印象。为缓解此效应，我们探索了一系列针对模型输入、问题结构和显式引导的干预措施。尽管多数干预效果有限，但明确激励探索能显著减少分层现象，凸显了需要更全面的多目标优化以减轻偏见。这些结果表明，LLMs并非仅被动反映人类社会偏见，而是能从经验中主动创造新偏见，引发关于这些系统将如何塑造社会的紧迫问题。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models (LLMs) can develop novel social biases through adaptive exploration, even in the absence of inherent demographic differences. Motivated by the limitations of simply removing existing biases, the authors apply a psychological paradigm to show that LLMs allocate tasks in highly stratified ways that are less fair than human decisions, especially in larger models. They find that this bias arises from exploration-exploitation trade-offs, where models rely too heavily on early observations. Interventions targeting inputs or problem structure had limited success, but explicitly incentivizing exploration significantly reduced stratification, underscoring the need for more comprehensive objective design to prevent bias emergence.
该论文探讨了大语言模型如何通过适应性探索自发产生新的社会偏见，即使在不存在固有群体差异的情况下。研究动机在于单纯去除现有偏见的方法不足，作者借鉴心理学框架发现，LLM会形成比人类更不公正的任务分配，尤其在更大模型中更为严重。实验测试了针对输入、问题结构和引导的多种干预措施，发现仅通过激励探索能显著减少分层现象，凸显需设计更复杂的多目标机制以防止偏见的涌现。
Steering Optimisation Trajectories in Diffusion Representation Learning
Authors: Rajat Rasal, Avinash Kori, Tian Xia, Ben Glocker
First: 2026-07-06T16:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study why diffusion autoencoders can achieve similar image quality while learning substantially different latent structures. We trace this behaviour to optimisation dynamics; we analyse curves of image reconstruction against latent representation quality, revealing trajectories that organise around two distinct regimes early in training. Models in the reconstruction regime prioritise image fidelity early, whereas those in the disentanglement regime improve reconstruction and disentanglement more gradually. We hypothesise that this behaviour can be influenced by targeting shortcut pathways in the diffusion U-Net and controlling early noise-level exposure, thereby shaping the reconstruction-disentanglement trade-off during training. To steer optimisation toward stronger representations, we introduce SteeringDRL, combining gated residual U-Nets with a simple noise-level exposure curriculum for training. Across disentanglement benchmarks, SteeringDRL improves representation quality and reduces seed sensitivity. Our method further extends to spatial disentanglement in object-centric learning, improving segmentation quality on synthetic and real-world datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散表示学习中的引导优化轨迹
我们研究了扩散自编码器为何能在学习截然不同的潜在结构的同时实现相似的图像质量。我们将此行为归因于优化动态：通过分析图像重建质量与潜在表示质量的曲线，我们发现训练早期存在两条不同的优化轨迹。处于重建模式的模型早期优先保证图像保真度，而处于解耦模式的模型则更渐进地提升重建与解耦效果。我们假设该行为可通过在扩散U-Net中针对捷径路径进行引导，并控制早期噪声水平暴露，从而塑造训练过程中的重建-解耦权衡。为引导优化向更强表示发展，我们提出SteeringDRL方法，结合门控残差U-Net与简单的噪声水平暴露课程训练。在解耦基准测试中，SteeringDRL提升了表示质量并降低了种子敏感性。该方法还可扩展至以物体为中心学习中的空间解耦，提升了合成与真实数据集上的分割质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that diffusion autoencoders can produce similar image quality despite learning divergent latent structures, the authors investigate the underlying optimization dynamics. They analyze reconstruction and latent quality curves during training, identifying two distinct early-stage regimes: one focused on rapid image fidelity and another on gradual disentanglement. To steer optimization toward better representations, they propose SteeringDRL, which combines gated residual U-Nets with a controlled noise-level exposure curriculum. Experiments show SteeringDRL improves disentanglement quality and reduces seed sensitivity across benchmarks, and extends to spatial disentanglement in object-centric tasks, enhancing segmentation performance on both synthetic and real-world data.
为了解释扩散自编码器在图像质量相近的情况下为何学习到不同的潜在结构，作者研究了优化动态的影响。通过分析训练初期的重建与潜在表示质量曲线，他们发现两种不同模式：一种早期优先保证图像保真度，另一种则逐步提升重建与解耦效果。为引导优化向更强表示发展，作者提出SteeringDRL方法，结合门控残差U-Net与噪声水平暴露课程。实验表明，该方法在解耦基准上提升表示质量并降低种子敏感性，且可扩展至物体中心学习中的空间解耦，提高合成与真实数据集上的分割性能。
PiSAs: Benchmarking Contextual Integrity in Multi-User Agentic Systems
Authors: Shubham Gupta, Nazanin Mohammadi Sepahvand, Abhinav Kumar, Cem Subakan, Spandana Gella, Pierre-André Noël, Perouz Taslakian, Eugene Bagdasarian, Valentina Zantedeschi
First: 2026-07-06T16:57:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:57:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLM agents evolve from single-user assistants into shared organizational infrastructure, new privacy risks emerge: inappropriate information may not only be exposed through outputs for external recipients, but also internally across users through inter-agent messages, shared memory and agents. These data spillage risks are not captured by existing privacy benchmarks grounded in contextual integrity (CI) as they focus primarily on either single-user settings or interactions between independently owned agents. We introducePiSAs (Privacy in Shared Agentic systems), a benchmark for assessing unintentional leaks with dual CI annotations: whether an information is appropriate for the task, and which users may legitimately access it. This enables direct measurement of cross-user spillage across agentic system components and interfaces, such as outputs, inter-agent communication, and memory. PiSAsis system-agnostic and supports evaluation across different agent topologies and memory regimes. We find that, although system design improves CI compliance, results are bottlenecked by incorrect LLM judgment calls: even state-of-the-art models fail to reliably filter inappropriate content or restrict transmission to authorized users. Our findings underscore the need for privacy-preserving strategies, beyond those studied in this work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiSAs：多用户代理系统中情境完整性基准测试
随着大语言模型代理从单用户助手演变为共享组织基础设施，新的隐私风险浮现：不当信息不仅可能通过输出对外部接收者暴露，也可能通过代理间通信、共享记忆和代理内部跨用户泄露。现有基于情境完整性（CI）的隐私基准未涵盖此类数据泄露风险，因其主要关注单用户场景或独立拥有代理间的交互。我们提出PiSAs（共享代理系统中的隐私），一种评估无意泄露的基准，包含双重CI标注：信息是否适合任务，以及哪些用户可合法访问。这使我们能直接测量代理系统组件与接口（如输出、代理间通信和记忆）中的跨用户泄露。PiSAs系统无关，支持不同代理拓扑与记忆机制下的评估。我们发现，尽管系统设计提升CI合规性，结果受限于LLM的错误判断：即使最先进模型也难以可靠过滤不当内容或限制仅向授权用户传输。我们的研究强调，需超越本工作所研究的隐私保护策略。
Summary / 总结
As large language model agents transition from single-user assistants to shared organizational systems, new privacy risks arise from unintended information leakage across users via inter-agent communication, shared memory, and outputs. To address this, PiSAs introduces a benchmark with dual contextual integrity annotations—task appropriateness and legitimate user access—to measure cross-user data spillage across system components. Evaluated across diverse agent architectures and memory regimes, PiSAs reveals that while system design enhances privacy compliance, state-of-the-art LLMs still frequently fail to correctly filter inappropriate content or restrict access to authorized users, highlighting a critical bottleneck in current privacy enforcement.
随着大语言模型代理从单用户助手演变为共享组织系统，用户间通过代理间通信、共享记忆和输出产生的意外数据泄露风险日益凸显。为应对这一问题，PiSAs提出一个双维度情境完整性标注基准——任务适当性与合法访问用户——用于衡量系统组件间的跨用户信息泄露。在多种代理架构和记忆机制下评估发现，尽管系统设计能提升隐私合规性，但当前最先进的大语言模型仍频繁无法正确过滤不当内容或限制访问权限，暴露出当前隐私机制的关键瓶颈。
NatureBench: Can Coding Agents Match the Published SOTA of Nature-Family Papers?
Authors: Yuru Wang, Lejun Cheng, Yuxin Zuo, Sihang Zeng, Bingxiang He, Che Jiang, Junlin Yang, Yuchong Wang, Kaikai Zhao, Weifeng Huang, Kai Tian, Zhenzhao Yuan, Jincheng Zhong, Weizhi Wang, Ning Ding, Bowen Zhou, Kaiyan Zhang
First: 2026-06-23T12:58:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:56:53+00:00
Comments: Add results of GLM-5.2 and MinMax-M3
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce NatureBench, a cross-discipline benchmark of 90 tasks distilled from peer-reviewed Nature-family publications, designed to evaluate whether AI coding agents can move beyond reproduction toward discovery on real scientific problems. NatureBench is built on NatureGym, an automated pipeline that constructs a standardized, per-task containerized environment from a source paper, addressing the environment-fragmentation problem that has limited the credibility of prior agent-on-research benchmarks. Evaluating ten frontier agent configurations under a strict web-search-disabled protocol, we find that the strongest model surpasses SOTA on only 17.8% of tasks under the g>0.1 criterion. Analysis of method pathways reveals that agents succeed primarily through methodological translation, converting scientific tasks into familiar supervised prediction problems, rather than through genuine scientific invention. Failures are dominated by wrong method choice and insufficient compute budget, not by task misunderstanding. We release the benchmark, the NatureGym pipeline, and a public leaderboard with maintainer-side reproduction. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/NatureBench
中文标题/摘要
标题：NatureBench：AI编码代理能否达到Nature系列论文的已发表最先进水平？
我们提出了NatureBench，这是一个从Nature系列同行评审论文中提炼出的90项跨学科基准测试，旨在评估AI编码代理是否能从复制转向在真实科学问题上实现发现。NatureBench基于NatureGym构建，后者是一个自动化流程，从源论文构建标准化、按任务容器化环境，解决了此前代理研究基准因环境碎片化而影响可信度的问题。在严格禁止网络搜索的协议下评估十种前沿代理配置，我们发现最强模型仅在17.8%的任务中（g>0.1标准）超越最先进水平。方法路径分析表明，代理主要通过方法论转换成功——将科学任务转化为熟悉的监督预测问题，而非真正的科学创新。失败主要源于方法选择错误和计算预算不足，而非任务理解错误。我们发布该基准、NatureGym流程及公开排行榜（含维护者侧复现）。代码：https://github.com/FrontisAI/NatureBench
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether AI coding agents can achieve true scientific discovery rather than mere reproduction, the authors introduce NatureBench—a benchmark of 90 tasks distilled from Nature-family papers. Built on NatureGym, an automated pipeline that standardizes per-task environments, the benchmark evaluates ten state-of-the-art agents under a strict web-search-disabled protocol. Results show that even the strongest agent surpasses published SOTA on only 17.8% of tasks under a g>0.1 threshold, with success primarily driven by methodological translation into supervised learning tasks rather than genuine scientific innovation. Failures stem mostly from incorrect method selection and insufficient compute, not task misinterpretation.
为评估AI编码代理能否超越简单复现而实现真正的科学发现，作者构建了NatureBench，该基准由90个源自Nature系列论文的任务组成。基于NatureGym自动化管道，该基准标准化了每个任务的环境，对十种前沿代理在禁用网络搜索的严格条件下进行评估。结果显示，最强模型仅在17.8%的任务上超越现有SOTA（g>0.1标准），成功主要依赖将科学任务转化为监督学习问题，而非真正的科学创新；失败主因是方法选择错误和计算资源不足，而非任务理解错误。
Topological Shape Representation for Aneurysm -- Bifurcation Detection
Authors: Akshay Gokhale, Mansi Dhamne
First: 2026-07-06T16:56:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:56:17+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 12 figures, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated detection of intracranial aneurysms (IAs) from CT angiography (CTA) is severely hindered by high false-positive rates. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) rely on local pixel intensities, causing systematic confusion between saccular aneurysms and vascular bifurcations -- a problem especially acute for small lesions (<3 mm), where detection sensitivity falls below 60%. We propose a plug-and-play, topology-aware false-positive reduction framework evaluating the Smooth Euler Characteristic Transform (SECT) -- a directional representation encoding global 3D vascular geometry independently of intensity -- against persistence-based summaries (Persistence Images and Landscapes), tested on a stratified subset of the RSNA 2025 dataset. SECT achieves an AUC of 0.943, substantially outperforming direction-agnostic methods (AUC ~0.68), and exhibits a clinical performance inversion: it excels on the sub-3 mm cohort, maintaining 0.943 AUC and 78.5% sensitivity at 95% specificity. The representation is also scanner-agnostic, achieving 0.927 mean AUC under leave-one-scanner-out (LOGO) validation across four manufacturers. By capturing asymmetric geometric invariants rather than intensity profiles, SECT reliably resolves the primary structural confounder in IA detection, positioning it as a robust downstream filter for hybrid deep-learning diagnostic pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于动脉瘤的拓扑形状表示——分叉检测
从CT血管造影（CTA）中自动检测颅内动脉瘤（IAs）受到高假阳性率的严重阻碍。卷积神经网络（CNN）依赖局部像素强度，导致系统性混淆囊状动脉瘤与血管分叉——这一问题在小病变（<3 mm）中尤为突出，检测敏感度低于60%。我们提出一种即插即用、拓扑感知的假阳性降低框架，评估方向性表示——平滑欧拉特征变换（SECT），该方法编码独立于强度的全局3D血管几何结构，与基于持久性的摘要（持久图像和景观）进行对比，测试于RSNA 2025数据集的分层子集。SECT达到AUC 0.943，显著优于方向无关方法（AUC约0.68），并表现出临床性能反转：在小于3 mm队列中表现卓越，保持0.943 AUC和95%特异性下的78.5%敏感度。该表示法还与扫描仪无关，在跨四家制造商的留一扫描仪外（LOGO）验证中平均AUC达0.927。通过捕捉不对称几何不变量而非强度分布，SECT可靠地解决了IA检测中的主要结构混淆因素，使其成为混合深度学习诊断流程中稳健的下游过滤器。
Summary / 总结
To address the high false-positive rates in automated detection of intracranial aneurysms from CT angiography, this study introduces a topology-aware framework using the Smooth Euler Characteristic Transform (SECT), a directional 3D vascular representation independent of intensity. Evaluated against persistence-based methods on a stratified RSNA 2025 subset, SECT achieved an AUC of 0.943, significantly outperforming direction-agnostic approaches (AUC ~0.68). Notably, SECT maintained high performance on small (<3 mm) aneurysms with 0.943 AUC and 78.5% sensitivity at 95% specificity, and demonstrated scanner-agnostic robustness with a mean AUC of 0.927 under leave-one-scanner-out validation across four manufacturers, effectively resolving the key confounder of vascular bifurcation mimicry.
为解决CT血管造影中颅内动脉瘤自动检测的高假阳性问题，本文提出一种拓扑感知框架，利用平滑欧拉特征变换（SECT）区分动脉瘤与血管分叉，尤其针对小于3毫米的小病变。SECT通过方向性编码全局三维血管几何结构，独立于强度信息，并在RSNA 2025数据集的分层子集上与基于持久性的方法对比。结果表明，SECT达到0.943的AUC，显著优于无方向性方法（AUC约0.68），且在小病变中保持78.5%的敏感度和95%特异性。该方法还具备扫描仪无关性，在四家厂商的留一扫描仪验证中平均AUC达0.927，证明其作为深度学习诊断流程下游过滤器的鲁棒性。
How Much is Left? LLMs Linearly Encode Their Remaining Output Length
Authors: Mohamed Amine Merzouk, Dmitri Carpov, Mirko Bronzi, Damiano Fornasiere, Adam Oberman
First: 2026-07-06T16:56:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:56:04+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models generate one token at a time, yet their responses show remarkably consistent length structure: step-by-step solutions converge in predictable token counts, retrievals stop after a few sentences, retractions extend responses by measurable amounts. We ask whether the model carries an internal estimate of how much response remains. Training minimal-capacity linear probes on frozen hidden states of three open-weight 7-8B models across seven completion-style datasets, we find three converging pieces of evidence. First, total response length is linearly decodable from the prompt's last hidden state alone, before any output is emitted. Second, probe directions trained on natural-language datasets transfer broadly, including to controlled synthetic completions never seen in training, outperforming a statistical baseline; the converse direction generally fails, and this asymmetry is itself informative. Third, on curated high-loss completions, the probe's per-position estimate shifts upward at the moment the model retracts and restarts a partial solution, a directional behavior no position-only predictor can reproduce (qualitative, not aggregate). We frame this as approximate estimation of remaining generation length, distinct from exact-counting impossibility results for transformers, and interpret it as evidence that LLMs maintain a plan-like internal representation of output length (decodable, not necessarily used causally).
中文标题/摘要
标题：还剩多少？大语言模型线性编码其剩余输出长度
大语言模型逐个生成token，但其响应表现出显著一致的长度结构：分步解题在可预测的token数量上收敛，检索在几句话后停止，撤回会以可测量的方式延长响应。我们探究模型是否内部估计了剩余响应长度。在三个开源7-8B模型的冻结隐藏状态上，训练容量最小的线性探针，覆盖七种完成式数据集，我们发现三组收敛证据。首先，仅从提示的最后一个隐藏状态即可线性解码总响应长度，且在任何输出生成前即可实现。其次，从自然语言数据集训练的探针方向可广泛迁移，包括到训练中从未见过的受控合成完成任务，优于统计基线；相反方向通常失败，这种不对称性本身具有信息价值。第三，在精选的高损失完成任务中，当模型撤回并重启部分解题时，探针在该位置的估计值会上升，这种方向性行为无法由仅基于位置的预测器复制（定性而非聚合）。我们将此现象视为对剩余生成长度的近似估计，区别于Transformer架构中精确计数的不可能性结果，并将其解释为大语言模型维持了类似计划的内部输出长度表征（可解码，未必因果使用）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models produce responses with consistent length patterns despite generating tokens sequentially, researchers investigated whether models internally estimate remaining output length. Using minimal-capacity linear probes trained on frozen hidden states of three 7-8B open-weight models across seven completion datasets, they found that total response length can be linearly predicted from the prompt’s final hidden state before any output is generated. Probe directions trained on natural language data generalized to unseen synthetic completions, outperforming statistical baselines, while the reverse direction failed — an asymmetry suggesting directional encoding. Additionally, on high-loss completions where models retract and restart, the probe’s per-token estimate increased at retraction points, a behavior unattainable by position-only predictors. These findings suggest LLMs maintain an approximate, decodable internal representation of remaining output length, distinct from exact counting impossibility, implying a plan-like structure in their generation process.
鉴于大语言模型在逐字生成时仍表现出高度一致的输出长度结构，研究者探究模型是否内部估计剩余输出长度。通过对三个7-8B开源模型的冻结隐藏状态训练最小容量线性探针，跨七个完成式数据集发现：仅凭提示末尾隐藏状态即可线性预测总输出长度；在自然语言数据训练的探针可泛化至未见过的合成完成任务，优于统计基线，而反向预测则失败，这种不对称性支持内部估计假说；在高损失完成任务中，当模型回退并重启部分解时，探针的每位置估计值方向性上升，而位置仅预测器无法复现此行为，表明LLM维持着类似计划的内部输出长度表征，与Transformer无法精确计数的理论不同。
Socially-Aware Autonomous Doorway Traversal and Payload Delivery for Emergency Assistance
Authors: Andrew Snowdy, Ananya Trivedi, Sarvesh Prajapati, Lorena Maria Genua, Taskin Padir
First: 2026-07-06T16:56:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:56:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this work, we focus on the scenario of a robot-assisted emergency evacuation. We consider two capabilities relevant to such a setting. The first is opening doors ahead of the people being evacuated, so that their path toward an exit stays clear. The second is retrieving rescue equipment and delivering it to the emergency responders carrying out the evacuation. From a systems perspective, this involves several tasks at once. The robot must locate ADA-compliant door buttons and the rescue equipment it needs to retrieve. Additionally, it must remain aware of the people around it and adapt its behavior to them, so that it supports the evacuation rather than getting in the way. We address these demands with a behavior tree at the core of our framework. This structure is chosen for its ability to select high-level tasks based on environmental triggers, and to extend to new situations as they arise. We evaluate the system in 105 trials on the Toyota Human Support Robot, across five hardware and three simulation scenarios. These trials capture the decisions the robot must make in this setting: whether to press a door button, yield to a nearby person, walk through a door someone else is holding, or first retrieve rescue equipment before traversing the door. Overall, the system completes 97 of the 105 trials successfully. These results suggest our framework provides a practical basis for robotic assistance in broader emergency response tasks. Code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/AndrewSnowdy/hsr_mm_control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向紧急援助的社会感知自主门廊穿越与物资投送
本研究聚焦于机器人辅助紧急疏散场景。我们考虑两种相关能力：一是为疏散人员提前开门，确保其通往出口的路径畅通；二是取回救援设备并将其交付给执行疏散任务的应急人员。从系统角度看，这涉及多项任务：机器人需定位符合无障碍标准的门按钮及所需救援设备，同时需感知周围人员并调整自身行为，以支持而非阻碍疏散。我们采用行为树作为框架核心，因其能根据环境触发选择高层任务，并适应新出现的情境。我们在丰田人形支持机器人上进行了105次试验，涵盖五种硬件和三种仿真场景，测试机器人在该场景下需做出的决策：是否按门按钮、为附近人员让路、穿过他人持握的门，或先取回救援设备再穿越门。系统成功完成105次试验中的97次。结果表明，我们的框架为机器人在更广泛的应急响应任务中提供实用基础。代码与演示视频见 https://github.com/AndrewSnowdy/hsr_mm_control。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to assist in emergency evacuations by clearing paths and delivering critical equipment, this work develops a behavior tree-based framework that enables a robot to autonomously navigate doorways while respecting human presence. The system must locate ADA-compliant door buttons, retrieve rescue gear, and adapt its actions—such as yielding to pedestrians or waiting for others to open doors—based on real-time social awareness. Evaluated across 105 trials on the Toyota Human Support Robot in varied hardware and simulation environments, the robot successfully completed 97 trials, demonstrating reliable decision-making in complex, dynamic scenarios involving human-robot interaction.
为提升机器人在紧急疏散中的辅助能力，本研究设计了一种基于行为树的框架，使机器人能自主通过门廊并协调人类活动，包括寻找无障碍门控和救援物资，根据环境动态调整行为如按门铃、避让行人或先取物资再通行。在105次试验中（涵盖多种硬件与仿真场景），机器人成功完成97次任务，验证了其在复杂社交环境中的可靠决策能力。
Deep Learning for Semen Analysis in Male Infertility: Computer Vision, Multimodal Fusion, and Clinical Translation
Authors: Runwei Guan, Shaofeng Liang, Jiacheng Weng, Xiaoyi Gu, Jia Weng, Daizong Liu, Duo Pan, Qingxin Zhang, Xiao Liang, Weiping Ding, Suoyu Zhu, Ming Yuan, Yanhua Fei
First: 2026-07-06T16:51:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:51:49+00:00
Comments: 46 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Male infertility contributes substantially to the global infertility burden, and sperm analysis remains central to diagnosis, treatment planning, and assisted reproductive technology. Conventional semen evaluation, however, is labor-intensive, operator-dependent, and limited by inter- and intra-observer variability, motivating the development of objective and reproducible computational approaches. This review provides a comprehensive and perspective-oriented synthesis of artificial intelligence-driven sperm analysis, with a focus on computer vision, deep learning, multimodal fusion, robustness, and clinical translation. We first review task-specific methods for sperm detection and counting, tracking-based motility assessment, semantic and instance segmentation, morphology and defect classification, functional assessment, and genetic integrity evaluation. We then summarize public datasets, benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and emerging multimodal strategies that integrate microscopic images, time-lapse videos, CASA-derived parameters, DNA integrity assays, and clinical metadata. Beyond algorithmic performance, we discuss key barriers to real-world deployment, including data scarcity, cross-center domain shift, annotation inconsistency, interpretability, uncertainty calibration, privacy-preserving learning, and workflow integration. Finally, we outline a staged clinical translation roadmap spanning technical standardization, multicenter retrospective validation, silent prospective evaluation, human-in-the-loop clinical testing, ART outcome validation, regulatory approval, and post-market monitoring. By organizing the field from task-specific visual recognition to trustworthy multimodal reproductive intelligence, this review highlights both the progress and the unresolved challenges required to translate AI-driven sperm analysis into clinically meaningful decision support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：男性不育精液分析的深度学习：计算机视觉、多模态融合与临床转化
男性不育在全球不孕症负担中占重要比例，精液分析仍是诊断、治疗规划和辅助生殖技术的核心。然而，传统精液评估劳动密集、依赖操作者且受观察者间及内变异影响，推动了客观可重复的计算方法发展。本综述从人工智能驱动的精子分析角度，系统梳理计算机视觉、深度学习、多模态融合、鲁棒性及临床转化等关键方向。首先回顾精子检测与计数、基于跟踪的运动评估、语义与实例分割、形态与缺陷分类、功能评估及遗传完整性评价等任务特定方法；继而总结公开数据集、基准、评估指标及整合显微图像、延时视频、CASA参数、DNA完整性检测与临床元数据的新兴多模态策略；除算法性能外，讨论真实部署的关键障碍，包括数据稀缺、跨中心域偏移、标注不一致、可解释性、不确定性校准、隐私保护学习及工作流整合；最后提出分阶段临床转化路线图，涵盖技术标准化、多中心回顾性验证、无声前瞻性评估、人机协同临床测试、ART结果验证、监管审批及上市后监测。本综述从任务导向视觉识别到可信多模态生殖智能，系统呈现AI驱动精液分析的进展与亟待解决的挑战，以推动其转化为临床决策支持。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for objective, reproducible semen analysis to address male infertility, this review synthesizes deep learning methods in computer vision, multimodal fusion, and clinical translation. It covers task-specific AI approaches for sperm detection, motility tracking, morphology classification, and functional assessment, alongside public datasets and evaluation benchmarks. Key findings reveal progress in algorithmic performance but highlight persistent barriers such as data scarcity, domain shift, annotation inconsistency, and workflow integration challenges. The review proposes a staged clinical translation roadmap to bridge AI research and real-world adoption, emphasizing the need for technical standardization, multicenter validation, and regulatory pathways to enable trustworthy AI in reproductive medicine.
为应对男性不育诊断中人工评估主观性强、效率低的问题，本文综述了基于深度学习的计算机视觉、多模态融合及临床转化方法。内容涵盖精子检测计数、运动追踪、形态分类、功能与遗传完整性评估等任务的AI模型，以及公共数据集、评估指标和整合显微图像、视频、CASA参数与临床数据的多模态策略。实验表明算法在理想环境下表现优异，但在真实临床部署中仍面临数据不足、跨中心域偏移、标注不一致和可解释性差等挑战。文章提出从技术标准化到临床验证、监管审批的分阶段转化路径，旨在推动AI精子分析系统成为可信赖的辅助决策工具。
Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models
Authors: Guli Zhu, Chenwei Wu, Liyue Shen
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-07-06T16:49:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:49:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Model editing promises a fast, targeted way to correct post-deployment mistakes in medical vision-language models (VLMs) without costly retraining. However, existing multimodal model editing benchmarks focus on general-purpose tasks and do not reflect realistic clinical domain requirements and variability. To address this, we introduce M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark for multimodal model editing that evaluates whether an edit remains reliable, precise, and generalizable under the challenges of image and text variation, modality and protocol shifts, clinical knowledge composition, and temporal progression. M3Bench contains 16,276 questions spanning diverse anatomy, modalities, and specialties, and supports both single and sequential edits. By evaluating 4 representative editors across 6 medical and general VLMs, we find that no method excels across all criteria. Gradient-based editors achieve strong transfer but suffer from catastrophic locality violations, whereas memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generality and exhibit high backbone-dependent hyperparameter sensitivity. We further attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how different editing methods shift its landscape. Overall, M3Bench establishes a rigorous clinical stress test for multimodal model editing and offers actionable guidance for safer post-deployment adaptation. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench .
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估与理解医学视觉语言模型的模型编辑
模型编辑为医学视觉语言模型（VLMs）在部署后快速、精准地修正错误提供了无需昂贵再训练的途径。然而，现有多模态模型编辑基准主要针对通用任务，未能反映临床领域的实际需求与多样性。为此，我们提出了M3Bench，一个基于临床实践的多模态模型编辑基准，用于评估编辑在图像与文本变化、模态与协议转换、临床知识组合及时间演变等挑战下是否仍保持可靠性、精确性和泛化能力。M3Bench包含16,276个覆盖多样解剖结构、模态与专科的问题，支持单次及顺序编辑。通过对6种医学与通用VLMs上的4种代表性编辑器进行评估，我们发现无一方法在所有标准上表现最优：基于梯度的编辑器虽具强迁移能力，但易出现灾难性局部性破坏；基于记忆的方法则保持局部性，但缺乏组合泛化性且对主干网络依赖的超参数敏感。我们进一步将这些失败归因于VLMs的潜在空间几何结构及不同编辑方法对其景观的扰动。总体而言，M3Bench为多模态模型编辑提供了严格的临床压力测试，并为部署后安全适应提供了可操作指导。该基准公开于https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable post-deployment corrections in medical vision-language models without costly retraining, this work introduces M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark evaluating model editing under realistic clinical challenges such as modality shifts, text-image variability, and temporal progression. The benchmark includes 16,276 diverse questions across specialties and supports single and sequential edits. Evaluating four editing methods across six VLMs, the study finds that gradient-based editors transfer well but cause catastrophic locality violations, while memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generalization and are sensitive to backbone-specific hyperparameters. The authors attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how editing methods alter it, offering insights for safer deployment adaptation.
为解决医疗视觉语言模型部署后无需重训练即可精准修正错误的需求，本研究提出M3Bench，一个面向临床真实场景的模型编辑评估基准，涵盖模态变化、图文变异、协议转换及时间演化等挑战。该基准包含16,276个多样化临床问题，支持单次与序列编辑。在六种VLM上评估四种编辑方法后发现，无一方法全面胜出：基于梯度的方法迁移能力强但易引发局部灾难性破坏，基于记忆的方法保持局部一致性但缺乏组合泛化能力且对模型架构超参敏感。研究归因于不同编辑方法对VLM潜在空间几何结构的扰动，为安全部署提供可操作指导。
Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection
Authors: Yewei Yuan, Michele Minervini, Mark M. Wilde, Nana Liu
First: 2026-07-06T16:47:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:47:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A core task in quantum anomaly detection is to compute an anomaly score that quantifies how strongly a test quantum state deviates from a given quantum dataset assumed to be normal. Classically, principal component analysis (PCA) for centered data computes the anomaly score by evaluating the test sample relative to the subspace spanned by the selected leading eigenvectors. However, for quantum data that lack a standard centering, explicitly recovering principal eigenvectors, constructing full Gram matrices, or loading quantum-random-access-memory-style data can be more costly than estimating the anomaly score itself. To avoid these costs, we propose Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection (QSPADE), which computes PCA-like anomaly scores directly from the spectrum of the average state of the normal dataset. By replacing hard PCA rank selection with a smooth, temperature-controlled spectral threshold, QSPADE makes near-threshold spectral components contribute partially to the anomaly score. This makes the score vary continuously rather than jump when a borderline component is included or excluded, and makes it less sensitive to noise or arbitrary hard cutoffs near the threshold. In the zero-temperature limit, QSPADE recovers the hard-projector PCA score. The proposed measurement-based quantum detector can be calibrated with a sample complexity independent of the data dimension. Numerical simulations show that QSPADE behaves like kernel-PCA on encoded classical data and detects changes across a transverse-field Ising transition without predefined order parameters. Consequently, QSPADE gives an efficient framework for both quantum-kernel anomaly detection on encoded classical data and the monitoring of quantum-native systems where diagnostic observables are unknown.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子谱异常检测
量子异常检测的核心任务是计算一个异常分数，以量化测试量子态相对于假设为正常的量子数据集的偏离程度。经典方法中，针对中心化数据的主成分分析（PCA）通过评估测试样本相对于选定主导特征向量张成的子空间来计算异常分数。然而，对于缺乏标准中心化的量子数据，显式恢复主特征向量、构建完整Gram矩阵或加载量子随机存取内存式数据，其成本可能高于直接估计异常分数。为避免这些开销，我们提出量子谱异常检测（QSPADE），该方法直接从正常数据集平均态的谱中计算类似PCA的异常分数。通过将硬性PCA秩选择替换为平滑的温度控制谱阈值，QSPADE使接近阈值的谱分量部分贡献于异常分数。这使得分数在边界分量被包含或排除时连续变化而非跳跃，并使其对噪声或阈值附近任意硬性截断更不敏感。在零温度极限下，QSPADE恢复硬投影PCA分数。所提出的基于测量的量子探测器可通过与数据维度无关的样本复杂度进行校准。数值模拟表明，QSPADE在编码的经典数据上表现如核PCA，并能在无预定义序参量的情况下检测跨横向场伊辛相变。因此，QSPADE为编码经典数据的量子核异常检测及诊断可观测量未知的量子原生系统监控提供了一个高效框架。

Motivated by the computational inefficiency of classical PCA for quantum data lacking standard centering, the paper introduces Quantum Spectral Anomaly Detection (QSPADE), a method that computes PCA-like anomaly scores directly from the spectrum of the average normal state without explicitly constructing eigenvectors or Gram matrices. QSPADE replaces hard rank selection with a smooth, temperature-controlled spectral threshold, enabling continuous anomaly scoring and reduced sensitivity to noise or arbitrary cutoffs. Numerical results show QSPADE behaves like kernel-PCA on classical data and successfully detects phase transitions in quantum systems without predefined order parameters, demonstrating efficiency for both encoded classical data and native quantum systems.
为解决量子数据缺乏标准中心化时经典PCA方法计算成本过高的问题，本文提出量子谱异常检测（QSPADE）方法，直接从正常数据集平均态的谱中计算异常分数，无需显式构造特征向量或Gram矩阵。QSPADE用平滑的温度控制谱阈值替代硬性秩选择，使异常分数连续变化、对噪声和阈值附近任意截断不敏感。零温极限下可恢复标准PCA分数，其测量型实现与数据维度无关。数值模拟表明，QSPADE在编码经典数据上表现如核PCA，并能在无预设序参量的情况下检测横向场伊辛模型的相变，适用于编码经典和原生量子系统的异常检测。
Biologically Informed Deep Neural Networks for Multi-Omic Integration, Pathway Activity Inference and Risk Stratification in Cancer
Authors: Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar, Le Ou-Yang, Min Wu, Sophia Tsoka
First: 2026-07-06T16:47:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:47:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integrating complex, multi-omics data presents significant challenges. Existing approaches often face a trade-off between model interpretability and representational capacity, with most either relying on post-hoc interpretation or use linear models that may overlook complex interactions. We report Pathway Activity Autoencoders for the multi-omics setting, which embed prior knowledge via pathway-informed architectural constraints, fostering interpretability, while preserving representational power. Our multi-omic framework is applied in the context of breast cancer and is evaluated in survival prediction and subtype classification with results indicating a positive effect of integration. We conduct analysis of individual omics layer impact on end-task performance, revealing that gene, protein, and microRNA expression layers provide the strongest contribution. Repeatability studies indicate that, while dropout improves model robustness and consistency, excessive regularisation can reduce predictive performance. Finally, visualizations of the learned feature space illustrate the framework's intrinsic transparency and clinical relevance. The results underscore the value of multi-omic integration and delineate the impact of individual omics layers, establishing practical guidelines for integration within our framework. Overall, our pathway activity autoencoder frameworks yield superior latent representations that are biologically meaningful and are directly translatable into clinically relevant insights.

Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models
Authors: Aaron Branson Cigres Li, Zhaowei Wang, Yu Zhao, Yiming Du, Haobo Li, Xiyu Ren, Ginny Wong, Simon See, Lishu Luo, Haodong Duan, Pasquale Minervini, Yangqiu Song
First: 2026-05-26T16:24:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:46:07+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.

为支持长上下文视觉语言模型在文档、视频等多模态内容中定位视觉证据，本研究探讨检索头能否有效处理图像信息。提出一种多模态检索头检测方法，通过衡量问题词对文本或图像证据的注意力得分。实验表明，仅少量头（4.4–10.2%）贡献了大部分检索性能，屏蔽这些头部会显著降低MMLongBench-Doc和SlideVQA等任务表现，而随机屏蔽影响甚微。进一步分析显示，这些头部在模态间部分共享，但在各自模态内动态变化，图像检索头比文本检索头更敏感于上下文长度和模态变化。重要的是，无需额外训练，这些头部即可直接用于排序视觉丰富文档，在MMDocIR上较最强基线提升Recall@1 7.7–6.8个百分点。
Adaptive Partitioning and Learning for Stochastic Control of Diffusion Processes
Authors: Hanqing Jin, Renyuan Xu, Yanzhao Yang
First: 2025-12-17T00:52:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:45:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study reinforcement learning for controlled diffusion processes with unbounded continuous state spaces, bounded continuous actions, and polynomially growing rewards: settings that arise naturally in finance, economics, and operations research. To overcome the challenges of continuous and high-dimensional domains, we introduce a model-based algorithm that adaptively partitions the joint state-action space. The algorithm maintains estimators of drift, volatility, and rewards within each partition, refining the discretization whenever estimation bias exceeds statistical confidence. This adaptive scheme balances exploration and approximation, enabling efficient learning in unbounded domains. Our analysis establishes regret bounds that depend on the problem horizon, state dimension, reward growth order, and a newly defined notion of zooming dimension tailored to unbounded diffusion processes. The bounds recover existing results for bounded settings as a special case, while extending theoretical guarantees to a broader class of diffusion-type problems. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our approach through numerical experiments, including applications to high-dimensional problems such as multi-asset mean-variance portfolio selection.

标题：扩散过程随机控制的自适应分区与学习
我们研究具有无界连续状态空间、有界连续动作和多项式增长奖励的受控扩散过程的强化学习问题，这类设定在金融、经济和运筹学中自然出现。为应对连续和高维域的挑战，我们提出一种基于模型的算法，该算法自适应地划分联合状态-动作空间。算法在每个分区中维护漂移、波动率和奖励的估计值，并在估计偏差超过统计置信度时细化离散化。该自适应方案平衡探索与近似，使算法能在无界域中高效学习。我们的分析建立了依赖于问题时域、状态维度、奖励增长阶数以及针对无界扩散过程新定义的“缩放维度”的遗憾界。这些界在有界情形下可还原为已有结果，同时将理论保证扩展至更广泛的扩散型问题。最后，我们通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性，包括应用于多资产均值-方差投资组合选择等高维问题。

MultAttnAttrib: Training-Free Multimodal Attribution in Long Document Question Answering
Authors: Dang Quang Thien Tran, Quang V. Dang, Vinamra Tyagi, Sai Soorya Rao Veeravalli, Trang Nguyen, Ryan A. Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Nedim Lipka, Koustava Goswami, Samyadeep Basu
Venue: EMNLP 2026 Long
First: 2026-07-01T19:29:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:45:18+00:00
Comments: 25 pages (8 main, 17 references + appendix), 15 figures, Submitted to EMNLP 2026 Conference (Long Paper)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As grounded QA systems are increasingly deployed in AI assistants, accurately attributing generated answers to evidence is critical for user trust and model safety. While unimodal attributions have been explored in depth, the multimodal setting remains relatively under-researched. As a result, we introduce MultAttnAttrib, a training-free attribution-generation method that leverages a model's prefill pass, selected attention heads, and calibrated thresholds to locate source evidence within a document. To establish baseline results for the method, we introduce MultAttrEval, a complementary benchmark dataset annotated with fine-grained, ground-truth attributions for answer components grounded in multimodal source documents. To our knowledge, this is the first evaluation dataset designed specifically for multimodal attribution in long-form documents. Experimental results show that MultAttnAttrib consistently outperforms a variety of attribution-generation methods, including several strong prompting-based approaches and matches the latest frontier models such as GPT 5.4. Our method not only substantially improves attribution accuracy for both unimodal and multimodal attribution types, but also produces attributions at up to one-seventh of the direct inference latency compared to prompting on the same base model.

标题：MultAttnAttrib：长文档问答中的无训练多模态归因方法

为提升AI助手的可信度与安全性，该研究针对长文档多模态问答中答案溯源的挑战，提出无需训练的MultAttnAttrib方法，利用模型预填充阶段、选定的注意力头和校准阈值定位证据源。在首个专为长文档多模态溯源设计的MultAttrEval基准上，该方法显著优于多种提示式方法，性能可媲美GPT 5.4等前沿模型，同时推理延迟仅为直接提示的七分之一。
Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming
Authors: Yiwei Zhou, Ziheng Chen
First: 2026-06-03T07:23:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:43:40+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 11 tables, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize superlinear drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the current stochastic gradient, the transformed update can have a biased conditional mean even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. This creates a stationary mean-shift channel that is absent for deterministic denominators.We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. The construction keeps the denominator deterministic given the current state, and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels retain the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory bounds the stationary bias through Euler, envelope, and stochastic-gradient residuals. The analysis also shows why purely local taming rules can lose control in the far tail and motivates a hybrid construction with additional tail protection. Experiments confirm the stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：驯化SGLD的确定性包络：解耦随机梯度噪声与局部化驯化
随机梯度朗之万算法常使用驯化分母以稳定超线性漂移。本文表明，当分母依赖于当前随机梯度时，变换后的更新即使原始梯度无偏，也可能具有有偏的条件均值，从而产生无确定性分母时不存在的稳态均值偏移通道。我们提出一种保持结构的驯化分母设计框架：该构造在给定当前状态时保持分母确定性，并使用局部确定性包络避免在典型区域不必要的驯化。这些核函数保留驯化的稳定效果，同时避免梯度依赖分母引入的偏置。我们的理论通过欧拉、包络和随机梯度残差对稳态偏置进行界。分析还解释了纯局部驯化规则在远尾部可能失控的原因，并推动了带额外尾部保护的混合构造。实验验证了随机分母的稳态失真、确定性包络设计的偏置降低效果，以及混合构造的稳定作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the bias introduced by gradient-dependent denominators in tamed stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics, this paper proposes a structure-preserving framework that replaces stochastic denominators with deterministic envelopes localized to the current state. The method ensures unbiased conditional means while retaining stabilization benefits, avoiding the stationary mean-shift artifacts caused by random denominators. Experimental results confirm that deterministic envelopes reduce bias significantly compared to random denominators and that a hybrid construction with tail protection further enhances stability in extreme regions.
为解决梯度依赖分母在驯化随机梯度朗之万算法中引入的偏差问题，本文提出一种保持结构的确定性包络框架，将随机梯度噪声与驯化机制解耦。该方法在当前状态条件下构造确定性分母，并采用局部包络避免典型区域过度驯化，在保持稳定性的同时消除偏差。实验表明，随机分母会引发稳态均值偏移，确定性包络设计显著降低该偏差，而结合尾部保护的混合构造进一步提升稳定性。
Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning
Authors: Fabien Polly
First: 2026-07-06T16:43:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:43:04+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures, 2 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/infinition/z-manifold
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized. We study an alternative: adaptation constrained to the subspace estimated from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large with 196 public LoRA adapters, we show that (1) the functionally relevant content of an adapter lies in a low-dimensional shared subspace, 30 to 38 percent of its weight norm being redundant under the evaluated task distributions; (2) gradient adaptation restricted to 128 coordinates on this subspace matches full LoRA fine-tuning on clean classification data, while under targeted label inversion LoRA collapses to 3-26 percent exact match and the constrained learner keeps 62-96 percent on the tasks the pool covers; (3) the constrained learner cannot fit corrupted data, its adaptation loss separating clean from garbage by two orders of magnitude (120x), an out-of-distribution signal without an extra detector; and (4) against an adaptive backdoor attacker who optimizes within the subspace, the attack is blocked (8 percent success versus 100 for LoRA) on the task where its target behavior is unlike anything in the pool, and only partially blocked (85 percent) when the target coincides with a common pool behavior. On these two tasks the outcome is consistent with how close the target is to the pool's directions, which suggests but does not establish a pool-relative boundary. The mechanism trades peak plasticity for these properties: on tasks the pool covers poorly, unconstrained fine-tuning wins, and the protection assumes the pool itself is trusted. Code and data are public.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅学习有效适配器所能表达的内容：子空间约束的适应性以抵御微调投毒
参数高效的微调仍保留大量可改变行为的更新空间，因此可被投毒目标表示并优化。我们研究一种替代方案：将适应性约束于从可信现有任务适配器池中估计出的子空间。在flan-t5-large上使用196个公开LoRA适配器，我们表明：(1) 适配器的功能相关内容位于低维共享子空间中，在评估任务分布下其权重范数的30%至38%为冗余；(2) 在该子空间的128维坐标上限制梯度适应，在干净分类数据上与完整LoRA微调表现一致，而在目标标签反转攻击下，LoRA准确率降至3%-26%，而受限学习器在适配器池覆盖的任务上保持62%-96%；(3) 受限学习器无法拟合被污染数据，其适应损失在干净数据与垃圾数据间相差两个数量级（120倍），无需额外检测器即可提供分布外信号；(4) 面对在子空间内优化的自适应后门攻击者，当攻击目标行为与适配器池无相似性时，攻击被完全阻断（成功率8% vs LoRA的100%），当目标与池中常见行为一致时，仅部分阻断（85%）。在两项任务中，结果与目标行为距离池方向的接近程度一致，暗示但未确立池相对边界。该机制以牺牲峰值可塑性换取上述特性：在适配器池覆盖不佳的任务上，无约束微调胜出，且该保护机制假设适配器池本身可信。代码与数据公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that parameter-efficient fine-tuning can be poisoned by adversarial objectives, this work proposes constraining adaptation to a low-dimensional subspace derived from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large using 196 LoRA adapters, the study finds that only 30–38% of adapter weight norms are functionally relevant under task distributions, with the rest being redundant. Gradient adaptation restricted to 128 subspace coordinates achieves performance matching full LoRA fine-tuning on clean data, while resisting label inversion attacks by maintaining 62–96% accuracy on covered tasks versus LoRA’s 3–26%. The constrained method also exhibits strong out-of-distribution detection, with adaptation loss differing by 120x between clean and corrupted data, and blocks adaptive backdoor attacks (8% success vs. 100% for LoRA) when the target behavior is outside the pool, while partially blocking attacks (85%) when the target aligns with common pool behaviors. The approach trades peak plasticity for robustness, performing worse on tasks poorly covered by the pool, and relies on the trustworthiness of the adapter pool.
为应对参数高效微调易被投毒攻击的风险，本文提出将适应过程限制在由可信任务适配器池估计的低维子空间中。在flan-t5-large上使用196个LoRA适配器发现，任务分布下仅30–38%的适配器权重范数具有功能相关性，其余冗余。限制在128维子空间上的梯度适应在干净数据上性能可媲美完整LoRA微调，而在目标标签反转攻击下保持62–96%准确率（LoRA仅3–26%）。该方法还能通过120倍的适应损失差异自动区分干净与污染数据，无需额外检测器，并在目标行为远离适配器池时能将后门攻击成功率从100%降至8%，若目标与池内常见行为一致则仅部分阻断（85%）。该方法以牺牲最大可塑性换取鲁棒性，前提是适配器池本身可信。
Adaptive Time-step Training for Enhancing Spike-Based Neural Radiance Fields
Authors: Ranxi Lin, Canming Yao, Jiayi Li, Weihang Liu, Xin Lou, Pingqiang Zhou
First: 2025-07-30T18:56:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:41:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an energy-efficient computing paradigm for neural rendering, but existing spike-based Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) models usually use a fixed inference time step for all scenes. This fixed temporal budget is inefficient because NeRF follows a scene-specific training paradigm, and different scenes require different temporal capacities to preserve rendering quality. This paper proposes Pretraining-based Adaptive Time-step Adjustment (PATA), a scene-wise adaptive time-step training framework for spike-based NeRF. PATA parameterizes the target inference time step as a trainable variable and optimizes it through a two-stage training process. A hybrid input mode strengthens early time-step outputs, while full-step soft supervision, smoothed rendering loss, and temporal-budget loss jointly maintain rendering fidelity and reduce temporal computation. The learned target time step is shared by all ray samples within a scene, preserving the parallel rendering structure of NeRF. Experiments on INGP-NeRF and TensoRF backbones across Synthetic-NeRF, Mip-NeRF 360, and LLFF show that PATA consistently reduces inference cost while maintaining competitive rendering quality. PATA reduces the estimated inference energy by up to 57.57\% on INGP-NeRF and 68.90\% on TensoRF, demonstrating its effectiveness across different neural rendering representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向提升脉冲神经辐射场的自适应时间步训练
脉冲神经网络（SNNs）为神经渲染提供了节能计算范式，但现有基于脉冲的神经辐射场（NeRF）模型通常对所有场景采用固定推理时间步。这种固定时间预算效率低下，因为NeRF遵循场景特定的训练范式，不同场景需不同时间容量以保持渲染质量。本文提出基于预训练的自适应时间步调整（PATA），一种面向基于脉冲NeRF的场景自适应时间步训练框架。PATA将目标推理时间步参数化为可训练变量，并通过两阶段训练过程进行优化。混合输入模式增强早期时间步输出，而全步软监督、平滑渲染损失和时间预算损失共同维持渲染保真度并减少时间计算。所学习的目标时间步在场景内所有射线样本间共享，保留NeRF的并行渲染结构。在Synthetic-NeRF、Mip-NeRF 360和LLFF数据集上，以INGP-NeRF和TensoRF为骨干网络的实验表明，PATA持续降低推理成本同时保持竞争力的渲染质量。在INGP-NeRF和TensoRF上，PATA分别将估计推理能耗降低57.57%和68.90%，证明其在不同神经渲染表示上的有效性。

Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed time steps in spike-based neural radiance fields (NeRFs), this work introduces PATA, a scene-adaptive time-step training framework that learns an optimal inference duration per scene. PATA treats the target time step as a trainable parameter, optimizing it via a two-stage process that combines hybrid input modes to boost early outputs and soft supervision with smoothed rendering and temporal-budget losses to preserve quality while reducing computation. Experiments across multiple NeRF backbones and datasets show PATA consistently cuts inference cost without sacrificing quality, achieving up to 57.57% and 68.90% energy savings on INGP-NeRF and TensoRF respectively.
为解决现有脉冲神经网络NeRF模型中固定时间步长导致的计算效率低下问题，本文提出PATA框架，通过学习每个场景最优的推理时间步长以提升效率。该方法将目标时间步长设为可训练参数，结合混合输入与软监督机制，在保证渲染质量的同时降低计算开销。实验表明，PATA在多个NeRF架构和数据集上可使推理能耗降低高达68.90%，同时保持渲染质量，验证其跨架构的有效性。
Data Driven Optimization of GPU efficiency for Distributed LLM-Adapter Serving
Authors: Ferran Agullo, Joan Oliveras, Chen Wang, Alberto Gutierrez-Torre, Olivier Tardieu, Alaa Youssef, Jordi Torres, Josep Ll. Berral
First: 2026-02-27T14:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:40:44+00:00
Comments: update of the journal paper contents after major revision
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) adapters enable low-cost model specialization, but introduce complex caching and scheduling challenges in distributed serving systems where hundreds of adapters must be hosted concurrently. While prior work has largely focused on latency and throughput optimization, minimizing GPU resource requirements through near-peak utilization remains largely underexplored. This paper presents a data-driven pipeline that, for a given workload, computes an adapter placement that serves the workload with the minimum number of GPUs while avoiding request starvation and GPU memory errors. To that end, the approach identifies the maximum feasible throughput attainable on each GPU by leveraging accurate performance predictions learned from real serving behavior. The proposed pipeline integrates three components: (i) a Digital Twin (DT) tailored to LLM-adapter serving, (ii) a distilled machine learning (ML) model trained on DT-generated data, and (iii) a greedy placement algorithm that exploits ML-based performance estimates to maximize GPU efficiency. The DT emulates real system dynamics with high fidelity, achieving below 5% throughput estimation error while executing up to 90x faster than full LLM benchmarking across both predictable and unpredictable workloads. The learned ML models further accelerate performance estimation with marginal accuracy degradation, enabling scalable optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that the pipeline substantially improves GPU efficiency, reducing the number of GPUs required to sustain target workloads by 60\% on average across the evaluated scenarios. Beyond GPU efficiency, the pipeline can be adapted to alternative objectives, such as latency minimization, highlighting its versatility for future large-scale LLM serving infrastructures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于数据驱动的分布式LLM适配器服务中GPU效率优化
大型语言模型（LLM）适配器可实现低成本模型专业化，但在分布式服务系统中，当需同时托管数百个适配器时，会引入复杂的缓存与调度挑战。尽管先前研究主要聚焦于延迟与吞吐量优化，但通过近满载利用降低GPU资源需求仍鲜有探索。本文提出一种数据驱动管道，针对给定工作负载，计算出能以最少GPU数量服务该负载且避免请求饥饿与GPU内存错误的适配器部署方案。为此，该方法通过从真实服务行为中学习的精确性能预测，识别每块GPU可实现的最大可行吞吐量。该管道包含三个组件：(i) 专为LLM适配器服务设计的数字孪生（DT），(ii) 基于DT生成数据训练的蒸馏机器学习（ML）模型，(iii) 利用ML性能估计的贪心部署算法以最大化GPU效率。该DT以高保真度模拟真实系统动态，吞吐量预测误差低于5%，且在可预测与不可预测工作负载下执行速度比完整LLM基准测试快90倍。所学ML模型进一步加速性能估算，仅带来微小精度损失，从而实现可扩展优化。实验结果表明，该管道显著提升GPU效率，在评估场景中平均减少60%维持目标负载所需的GPU数量。除GPU效率外，该管道还可适配其他目标，如延迟最小化，凸显其对未来大规模LLM服务基础设施的通用性。

Motivated by the need to reduce GPU resource consumption in distributed LLM-adapter serving while maintaining performance, this paper introduces a data-driven pipeline that optimizes adapter placement to minimize GPU usage without causing request starvation or memory errors. The method combines a Digital Twin for realistic system emulation, a distilled ML model trained on DT-generated data for fast throughput prediction, and a greedy placement algorithm that leverages these predictions to maximize GPU efficiency. Experiments show the pipeline reduces GPU requirements by 60% on average across diverse workloads, while maintaining high prediction accuracy (below 5% error) and achieving up to 90x faster simulation than full benchmarking. The approach is also adaptable to other objectives like latency minimization, demonstrating broad applicability for large-scale LLM serving.
为降低分布式LLM适配器服务中的GPU资源消耗同时保障性能，本文提出一种数据驱动的适配器部署优化管道，旨在最小化GPU数量并避免请求饥饿与显存错误。该方法结合高保真数字孪生系统模拟、基于DT数据训练的轻量级机器学习模型进行吞吐量预测，以及利用预测结果的贪心部署算法以最大化GPU效率。实验表明，该管道平均减少60%的GPU使用量，同时保持系统稳定，并支持扩展至延迟最小化等其他优化目标。
MetaSkill-Evolve: Recursive Self-Improvement of LLM Agents via Two-Timescale Meta-Skill Evolution
Authors: Zefeng Wang, Minxi Yan, Jinhe Bi, Sikuan Yan, Volker Tresp, Yunpu Ma
First: 2026-07-06T16:40:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:40:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent LLM agents tackle increasingly long-horizon, open-ended tasks, and external skills, reusable procedural knowledge supplied to the agent, further extend this capability. However, a fixed, hand-authored skill is rarely optimal, and cannot adapt to the diversity of tasks an agent encounters. Self-improving agents address this by rewriting their own skill files from execution traces, yielding meaningful gains on challenging benchmarks. Yet such self-evolution remains non-recursive: it improves only the task skill (what the agent does) while the improvement procedure (how it improves) is authored once and held fixed. We introduce MetaSkill-Evolve, a two-timescale framework that makes agentic skill improvement recursive: every branch carries both a task skill $s$ and a branch-local meta-skill $m=(ψ,σ,α,π,\varepsilon)$ whose five components parameterise the Analyzer, Retriever, Allocator, Proposer, and Evolver agents of the improvement pipeline. Task skills evolve on a fast loop while the meta-skill evolves on a slower one under the same pipeline applied to itself, with no additional model or objective. With all five pipeline agents sharing a single frozen backbone, MetaSkill-Evolve outperforms no-skill, static-skill, and single-level evolution baselines on three agentic benchmarks (OfficeQA, SealQA, ALFWorld), improving held-out test accuracy over the raw backbone by +23.54, +16.09, and +1.92 points respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaSkill-Evolve：通过双时间尺度元技能演化实现LLM代理的递归自我改进
近期的LLM代理正处理越来越长的开放性任务，并借助外部技能（可重用的程序性知识）进一步扩展能力。然而，固定的手动编写技能很少是最优的，且无法适应代理所遇到任务的多样性。自我改进代理通过从执行轨迹中重写自身技能文件来解决此问题，从而在挑战性基准上获得显著提升。但此类自我演化仍非递归：它仅改进任务技能（代理做什么），而改进过程（如何改进）则由人工编写并保持固定。我们提出MetaSkill-Evolve，一种双时间尺度框架，使代理技能改进递归化：每个分支同时包含一个任务技能s和一个局部元技能m=(ψ,σ,α,π,ε)，其五个组成部分分别参数化改进流程中的分析器、检索器、分配器、提议者和演化器代理。任务技能在快速循环中演化，而元技能则在相同流程下以较慢速度自我演化，无需额外模型或目标。所有五个流程代理共享同一冻结主干网络，MetaSkill-Evolve在三个代理基准（OfficeQA、SealQA、ALFWorld）上优于无技能、静态技能和单层演化基线，分别在保留测试集上较原始主干提升23.54、16.09和1.92个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLM agents to adapt to diverse, long-horizon tasks beyond fixed skills, the paper proposes MetaSkill-Evolve, a two-timescale framework enabling recursive self-improvement. It allows both task skills and meta-skills—comprising five components governing the improvement pipeline—to evolve iteratively: task skills update rapidly via execution traces, while meta-skills evolve slowly by applying the same pipeline to themselves. Evaluated on three benchmarks, the method outperforms static and single-level evolution baselines, boosting test accuracy over the base model by 23.54, 16.09, and 1.92 points respectively.
为应对大语言模型代理在多样化长时任务中难以适应固定技能的问题，本文提出MetaSkill-Evolve框架，支持技能的递归自我进化。该框架采用双时间尺度机制：任务技能快速迭代更新，而元技能（由五个组件构成，控制自我进化流程）则缓慢进化，通过将同一进化流程应用于自身实现。在OfficeQA、SealQA、ALFWorld三个基准上，该方法显著优于无技能、静态技能及单层进化基线，测试准确率较基础模型分别提升23.54、16.09和1.92个百分点，且无需额外模型或目标函数。
Resource-constrained Project Scheduling with Time-of-Use Energy Tariffs and Machine States: A Logic-based Benders Decomposition Approach
Authors: Corentin Juvigny, Antonín Novák, Jan Mandík, Zdeněk Hanzálek
First: 2026-01-10T11:47:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:37:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we investigate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) with Time-of-Use (TOU) energy tariffs and machine states, a variant of RCPSP for production scheduling, where energy price is part of the criteria and one highly energy-demanding machine can be in one of the following three states: proc, idle, or off. The problem involves scheduling all tasks, respecting precedence constraints and resource limitations, while minimizing the combination of the overall makespan and the Total Energy Cost (TEC), which varies according to the TOU tariffs, which can take negative values. We propose two novel approaches to solve it: a monolithic Constraint Programming (CP) approach and a Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach. The latter combines a master problem handling the energy cost solved using Integer Linear Programming (ILP) with a subproblem handling the RCPSP, resolved using CP. Both approaches outperform the monolithic compact ILP counterpart, but the LBBD significantly outperforms the monolithic CP in most cases, especially when the makespan criterion is not included in the objective function, solving to optimality instances with up to 480 tasks. Finally, we propose a way to generalize our LBBD approach to other problems sharing similar characteristics, and applied it to various problems, such as an RCPSP with blocking times & total weighted tardiness criterion, or a flexible job shop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：考虑分时电价与机器状态的资源受限项目调度：基于逻辑的Benders分解方法
本文研究了考虑分时电价（TOU）和机器状态的资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP），这是生产调度中RCPSP的一种变体，其中电价是优化目标的一部分，且一台高能耗机器可处于proc、idle或off三种状态之一。该问题需在满足任务优先约束和资源限制的前提下，调度所有任务，以最小化总工期与总能耗成本（TEC）的组合，而TEC随TOU电价变化，电价可为负值。我们提出两种新方法求解该问题：一种是整体约束规划（CP）方法，另一种是基于逻辑的Benders分解（LBBD）方法。后者将处理能耗成本的主问题（使用整数线性规划ILP求解）与处理RCPSP的子问题（使用CP求解）相结合。两种方法均优于整体紧凑ILP方法，但LBBD在大多数情况下显著优于整体CP方法，尤其当目标函数不包含工期时，可求解至最优解，任务数高达480个。最后，我们提出将LBBD方法推广至具有相似特性的其他问题，并将其应用于多种问题，如带阻塞时间和加权总延迟的RCPSP，或柔性作业车间调度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize production scheduling under fluctuating energy costs and machine state constraints, this study addresses a variant of the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) that incorporates Time-of-Use energy tariffs and machine states (proc, idle, off). The authors propose two solution methods: a monolithic Constraint Programming (CP) model and a Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach, which decomposes the problem into an ILP master problem for energy cost and a CP subproblem for scheduling. Experimental results show that both methods outperform a monolithic ILP model, with LBBD significantly superior—especially when makespan is not part of the objective—solving optimally instances with up to 480 tasks. The LBBD framework is also generalized to solve related scheduling problems with different criteria, such as total weighted tardiness or blocking times.
为应对生产调度中能源价格波动与机器状态约束的优化需求，本文研究了一种结合分时电价与机器状态（运行、空闲、关闭）的资源约束项目调度问题（RCPSP）变体。作者提出两种求解方法：一种是单体约束规划（CP）模型，另一种是逻辑基础Benders分解（LBBD）方法，后者将问题分解为处理能源成本的整数线性规划主问题和处理调度的CP子问题。实验表明，两种方法均优于单体整数线性规划模型，其中LBBD在多数情况下显著优于单体CP，尤其当目标函数不含完工时间时，可求解最多480任务的实例达到最优解。该LBBD框架还被推广应用于其他具有相似特征的调度问题，如带阻塞时间和加权延迟目标的RCPSP或柔性作业车间调度。

Abstract
Fetal health classification is a critical task in obstetrics, enabling early identification and management of potential health problems. However, it remains challenging due to data complexity and limited labeled samples. Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision
Authors: Guorun Wang, Simone Foti, Andreas D. Demou, Leonidas Kotoulas, Theodoros Christoudias, Alexandros Koliousis, Mihalis Nicolaou, Stefanos Zafeiriou
First: 2026-07-06T16:32:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:32:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Super-resolving coarse atmospheric fields to local PM$_{2.5}$ variations is uniquely challenged by a mismatch in spatial support: while pixels represent regional averages, ground-truth observations are discrete, unaligned samples of a continuous spatial signal. To bridge this gap, we present a station-guided framework for high-resolution PM$_{2.5}$ downscaling over Europe. Taking coarse CAMS atmospheric composition fields alongside heterogeneous side information (i.e., human activity, land cover, elevation, satellite aerosol observations, and wind fields) our framework jointly super-resolves ($\times 40$, $\approx$ 1 km) and bias-corrects CAMS rasters, without relying on temporal sequence modelling. To address the challenge of densely supervising our multi-scale transformer network with sparse in-situ data, we introduce a time-agnostic propagation strategy that utilises spatial Gaussian blending of interpolated OpenAQ observations. Extensive qualitative and station-level evaluations across Europe demonstrate that our model recovers fine-grained spatial structures and effectively mitigates localised CAMS biases.

Motivated by the challenge of aligning coarse atmospheric fields with fine-grained ground-truth PM2.5 measurements, this work proposes a station-guided pseudo-supervision framework to downscale CAMS atmospheric data to 1 km resolution across Europe. ChatImage: Navigating Long-Form LLM Answers through Interactive Images
Authors: Wencan Jiang, Jiangning Zhang, Yong Liu
First: 2026-07-06T16:30:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:30:41+00:00
Comments: Project:https://wencanjiang.github.io/ChatImage
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce detailed answers to complex queries, but these answers are typically presented as dense linear text, which makes fine-grained inspection, navigation, and return visits difficult. We present ChatImage, a system that converts long-form LLM answers into interactive visual images. Given a textual answer, ChatImage first normalizes its content into structured visual modules, plans a visual layout, and renders a coherent image. It then applies a second grounding pass to the rendered image with vision grounding models such as LocateAnything and MiMo-Vision, with optional SAM-style mask refinement, to identify the visible regions that should support interaction. From these grounded regions, ChatImage overlays transparent clickable hotspots on the image. Each hotspot opens a detail panel and a region-scoped follow-up thread, allowing the user to inspect and query a specific part of the answer without re-reading the full response. Instead of treating planned coordinates as the final interaction geometry, ChatImage uses them as priors and grounds the interaction targets after rendering, which improves consistency between visual content and clickable regions. We release a reference implementation and introduce a 30-question benchmark covering infographic, map, and scene-based answer formats. Evaluation with configured external models reports interaction-loop completion, a strict visual-alignment gate, and a SAM-based mask-completeness diagnostic.

标题：ChatImage：通过交互式图像导航长文本大语言模型答案
大语言模型（LLM）可生成复杂查询的详细答案，但这些答案通常以密集线性文本形式呈现，难以进行细粒度检查、导航和回访。我们提出ChatImage系统，将长文本LLM答案转换为交互式视觉图像。给定文本答案，ChatImage首先将其内容标准化为结构化视觉模块，规划视觉布局并渲染连贯图像；随后使用视觉定位模型（如LocateAnything和MiMo-Vision），可选地结合SAM风格掩码优化，识别应支持交互的可见区域。在这些定位区域上，ChatImage叠加透明可点击热点，每个热点打开详情面板和局部范围的后续对话线程，使用户无需重读全文即可检查和查询答案的特定部分。ChatImage不将规划坐标视为最终交互几何，而是将其作为先验，在渲染后进行交互目标定位，从而提升视觉内容与可点击区域的一致性。我们发布参考实现，并引入涵盖信息图、地图和场景式答案格式的30题基准测试。评估使用配置的外部模型，报告交互循环完成率、严格的视觉对齐门控及基于SAM的掩码完整性诊断。

Wavelet Scattering Transform for Interpretable Schizophrenia Biomarker Discovery and Classification from Resting-State EEG
Authors: Md. Taksimul Ahsan Tawhid, Nasif Ahmed Rafe, Alif Tahmid Priyom, K. M. Mustafizur Rahman
First: 2026-07-06T16:27:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:27:26+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Schizophrenia is a debilitating neuropsychiatric disorder characterized by profound cortical network dysregulation, for which objective, clinically translatable EEG based biomarkers remain underdeveloped. Existing automated classification pipelines rely predominantly on static power spectral density features inherently blind to amplitude modulation dynamics and cross-frequency coupling, phenomena central to schizophrenia pathophysiology, while adopting epoch level cross validation strategies that introduce temporal data leakage, artificially inflate reported performance. This study introduces a mathematically principled diagnostic framework integrating the multi-order Wavelet Scattering Transform(WST), strict Leave One Subject Out (LOSO) cross-validation, and SHAP explainability for simultaneous EEG classification and biomarker discovery. Hierarchical WST coefficients capturing multi-scale amplitude modulation structure were extracted from resting state multichannel EEG. Subject-level ANOVA with Benjamini Hochberg false discovery rate correction identified significant biomarkers, with Random Forest and SVM classifiers evaluated under strict LOSO cross validation and subject-level majority voting. Second-order scattering coefficients encoding cross frequency coupling dominated the discriminative biomarker set, with gamma-band features most prevalent, demonstrating that temporal amplitude modulation constitutes the primary electrophysiological signature of schizophrenia. Electrode P3 was identified as the single most discriminative site. Under rigorous subject independent evaluation, the Random Forest achieved 90.48% accuracy (AUC = 0.9339; sensitivity = 95.56%). The proposed WST framework establishes a rigorous, interpretable standard for EEG-driven psychiatric biomarker discovery that can also be applicable in the detection of schizophrenia subtypes in the future.
