Routing Anonymity and Identifiability of Noisy Quantum Hardware
Authors: Ben Priestley, Mina Doosti
First: 2026-07-06T16:25:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:25:09+00:00
Comments: 22+30 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Present-day quantum computing is cloud-based, where a user submits a circuit to a service provider's proprietary backend hardware. While providers may wish to hide implementation details, scheduling choices, or even which physical device was used, noisy finite-shot outputs can carry backend-specific fingerprints: information imprinted in the classical output distribution that can reveal the backend identity. So far, such fingerprints have mostly been studied from a benchmarking perspective, with limited attention to privacy considerations for users and providers. This work develops the first formal framework for backend identifiability and its privacy implications. We introduce a backend-identifiability game and use it to formalise routing anonymity as a security notion for quantum cloud services. We show that backend identifiability is a hypothesis-testing problem and prove that, under passive i.i.d. access to a single backend, routing anonymity decays exponentially at the Chernoff rate. We also establish a utility-anonymity trade-off, imposing fundamental limits on how much backend-specific information can be removed from classical outputs without degrading their usefulness. In addition, we observe that, for noisy quantum hardware, identifying fingerprints are inherently an intermediate-depth phenomenon, and establish a depth principle using Pauli-transfer-matrix tools. We complement the theory with experiments on Amazon Braket on AWS, using ion-trap and superconducting quantum processors. We observe 87-90% classification between superconducting backends and 96-100% classification across physical platforms, and find that identifiability can survive natural forms of post-processing. Overall, these results establish routing anonymity as a distinct security requirement for quantum cloud computing, and provide a framework for quantifying and controlling the utility-anonymity trade-off.
中文标题/摘要
标题：噪声量子硬件的路由匿名性与可识别性
当前量子计算基于云服务，用户将电路提交给服务提供商的专有后端硬件。尽管提供商可能希望隐藏实现细节、调度选择或所用物理设备，但噪声有限次输出可能携带后端特定指纹：印在经典输出分布中的信息，可揭示后端身份。此前此类指纹主要从基准测试角度研究，对用户和提供商隐私关注较少。本工作首次建立后端可识别性的形式化框架及其隐私影响。我们引入后端可识别性博弈，将其形式化为量子云服务的路由匿名性安全概念。我们证明后端可识别性是一个假设检验问题，并在被动独立同分布访问单个后端条件下，证明路由匿名性以切尔诺夫速率呈指数衰减。同时，我们建立效用-匿名性权衡，确立从经典输出中移除后端特定信息的固有上限，而不降低其效用。此外，我们观察到，对噪声量子硬件而言，识别指纹本质上是中间深度现象，并利用泡利转移矩阵工具建立深度原理。我们通过AWS上的Amazon Braket实验验证理论，使用离子阱与超导量子处理器。实验观察到超导后端分类准确率87-90%，跨物理平台分类准确率96-100%，且可识别性可耐受自然形式的后处理。总体而言，这些结果确立路由匿名性为量子云计算的独立安全需求，并提供量化与控制效用-匿名性权衡的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect backend identity in quantum cloud services, this work introduces a formal framework for backend identifiability and routing anonymity. It defines a hypothesis-testing game to model privacy risks and proves that routing anonymity decays exponentially under passive i.i.d. access, at the Chernoff rate. The study establishes a fundamental utility-anonymity trade-off, showing that removing backend-specific fingerprints from outputs degrades their usefulness. Using Pauli-transfer-matrix tools, it identifies identifiability as an intermediate-depth phenomenon and validates findings through experiments on Amazon Braket, achieving 87–90% classification between superconducting backends and 96–100% across platforms, with identifiability persisting under common post-processing. These results frame routing anonymity as a critical security requirement in quantum cloud computing.
为保护量子云服务中后端硬件的特定信息，该研究首次提出后端可识别性与路由匿名性的形式化框架。通过假设检验游戏，证明在被动独立同分布访问单个后端时，路由匿名性以切尔诺夫速率呈指数衰减，并确立了效用与匿名性之间的根本性权衡。在Amazon Braket上的实验显示，超导后端间分类准确率达87–90%，跨物理平台达96–100%，且匿名性在常规后处理后仍可保持。研究还指出，后端指纹在中间深度电路中自然显现，借助泡利转移矩阵工具建立深度原理，确立了路由匿名性作为量子云计算关键安全需求的地位。
Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations
Authors: Benjamin Walker
First: 2026-07-06T16:25:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:25:03+00:00
Comments: DPhil thesis, University of Oxford, 188 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability. This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance. Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经控制微分方程的进展
许多现实世界系统呈连续演化，但大多数机器学习模型将时间序列视为离散序列。连续时间方法则将时间序列视为来自潜在输入路径的采样，这种表述天然支持不规则采样或过采样数据。其中，神经控制微分方程（NCDEs）是一类表达能力最强的模型，通过神经网络参数化向量场，并通过求解由输入路径驱动的动力系统演化其隐藏状态。NCDEs通常使用非线性向量场，因此其表达能力和连续时间灵活性以计算代价高昂且固有顺序化的前向传播为代价，限制了其可扩展性和实际应用。 本论文通过三项互补贡献推进NCDEs的训练与可扩展性。首先，基于神经粗糙微分方程，Log-NCDEs应用Log-ODE方法在训练期间高效近似NCDE解，提升计算速度和实证性能。其次，线性NCDEs将非线性向量场替换为线性向量场，实现闭式解和时间并行计算，而不牺牲理论表达能力。第三，结构化线性NCDEs使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率，同时保持理论表达能力和实证性能。 综合而言，这些方法将NCDE每步训练时间降低高达三个数量级，同时在多种时间序列基准测试中达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model continuous-time systems more effectively than discrete-time machine learning models, this thesis introduces three innovations to improve Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs). First, Log-NCDEs use the Log-ODE method to approximate NCDE solutions, speeding up training. Second, Linear NCDEs replace non-linear vector fields with linear ones, enabling parallel computation and closed-form solutions without losing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs further optimize efficiency using structured linear vector fields. Together, these methods reduce training time per step by up to three orders of magnitude while maintaining or improving performance across multiple time series benchmarks.
为更有效地建模连续时间系统，本研究提出三项创新以提升神经控制微分方程（NCDE）的训练效率与可扩展性。首先，Log-NCDEs采用Log-ODE方法在训练中近似NCDE解，提升计算速度与实证表现；其次，线性NCDEs将非线性向量场替换为线性结构，支持并行时间计算而不牺牲理论表达能力；第三，结构化线性NCDEs进一步通过结构化线性向量场提升效率，同时保持理论表达力与实证性能。三者结合，使NCDE每步训练时间降低三个数量级，同时在多个时间序列基准测试中达到最先进水平。
Algorithmic Shortlisting in Participatory Budgeting
Authors: Juan Zambrano, Clément Contet, Jairo Gudiño-Rosero, Felipe Garrido-Lucero, Umberto Grandi, César Hidalgo
First: 2025-08-07T15:26:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:24:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Participatory budgeting is a democratic innovation that allows citizens to propose and vote on public investment projects. To help organizers manage large volumes of submissions, we design and test privacy-preserving methods for algorithmic shortlisting. These algorithms predict which projects are likely to be funded using only project features and anonymous historical voting data. We demonstrate the limitations of a naive approach that uses a large language model to rank projects based on past success and propose a vote-based pipeline that enables state-of-the-art LLMs to perform on par with classical machine learning. Our findings indicate that user preferences in participatory budgeting are stable enough to allow algorithmic shortlisting to approximate an initial selection of projects effectively.

Motivated by the need to efficiently manage large volumes of citizen-submitted projects in participatory budgeting, the authors develop privacy-preserving algorithmic shortlisting methods that predict funding likelihood using only project features and anonymous historical voting data. They compare a naive approach relying on large language models to rank projects based on past success with a vote-based pipeline that enables state-of-the-art LLMs to match classical machine learning performance. 为应对参与式预算中大量公民提案的管理难题，作者设计了基于项目特征和匿名历史投票数据的隐私保护算法，用于预测项目获资助可能性。他们对比了直接使用大语言模型的朴素排名方法与结合投票数据的改进流程，发现后者可与传统机器学习方法媲美。实验表明，参与式预算中的用户偏好具有足够稳定性，使算法能有效近似初始项目筛选结果。
Untrusted Content Masking for Web Agents with Security Guarantees
Authors: Kristina Nikolić, Egor Zverev, Javier Rando, Matthew Jagielski, Edoardo Debenedetti, Florian Tramèr
First: 2026-07-06T16:22:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:22:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Defenses that provide security guarantees against prompt injection attacks rely on strict isolation between trusted instructions and untrusted data. In text-based environments such as tool-use APIs, this separation arises naturally: agents can reason from interface definitions without ever processing untrusted content. Extending these guarantees to web agents faces a fundamental challenge: to perceive and interact with their environment, web agents must first observe the rendered page, which intermingles trusted content with untrusted content. This structural entanglement removes the trust boundary on which security guarantees depend, undermining provable defenses for web agents. In this paper, we present Untrusted Content Masking (UCM), a simple and effective approach that restores this boundary in web environments. We leverage a key structural insight: a webpage's Document Object Model (DOM) encodes sufficient information to distinguish trusted from untrusted regions without reading their content. Our framework exploits this by redacting untrusted regions before they reach the agent and routing interaction through a sandboxed interface with strict privilege separation, thereby enabling agents to observe and interact with their environment while remaining isolated from adversarial content. The code is publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向具有安全保证的网络代理的不可信内容屏蔽
提供针对提示注入攻击安全保证的防御机制依赖于可信指令与不可信数据之间的严格隔离。在基于文本的环境（如工具使用API）中，这种分离自然产生：代理可从接口定义中推理，而无需处理不可信内容。将这些保证扩展到网络代理面临根本性挑战：为感知和交互环境，网络代理必须首先观察渲染的页面，而页面中可信内容与不可信内容相互混杂。这种结构上的纠缠消除了安全保证所依赖的信任边界，削弱了网络代理的可证明防御。本文提出不可信内容屏蔽（UCM），一种简单有效的方案，可在网络环境中恢复该边界。我们利用关键结构洞察：网页的文档对象模型（DOM）足以在不读取内容的情况下区分可信与不可信区域。框架通过在内容传给代理前屏蔽不可信区域，并通过沙箱化接口进行交互，实现严格权限分离，从而让代理能观察和交互环境，同时与对抗性内容隔离。代码公开可用。

Motivated by the challenge of securing web agents against prompt injection attacks, this paper addresses the problem that rendered web pages inherently mix trusted and untrusted content, breaking the isolation needed for provable security. The authors propose Untrusted Content Masking (UCM), a method that leverages the Document Object Model (DOM) to identify and redact untrusted regions before they reach the agent, while routing interactions through a sandboxed interface with strict privilege separation. Experimental results demonstrate that UCM successfully restores the trust boundary, enabling agents to safely perceive and interact with web environments without exposure to adversarial content.
为了解决网络代理在渲染页面中不可避免地混合可信与不可信内容导致安全边界失效的问题，本文提出Untrusted Content Masking（UCM）方法，通过在代理交互前屏蔽不可信的DOM区域，恢复安全边界。UCM利用DOM结构特性识别并遮蔽不可信内容，不读取其文本，再通过沙箱化接口实现权限隔离。实验结果表明，UCM能有效隔离代理与对抗性内容，同时保持功能正常，使网络代理在传统防御失效的环境中仍能获得可证明的安全保障。
ProPS: Prompted Profile Synthesis for Natural Language-Conditioned Speaker Embedding Distributions
Authors: Thomas Thebaud, Junhyeok Lee, Laureano Moro-Velazquez, Jesus Villalba Lopez, Najim Dehak
First: 2026-07-06T16:21:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:21:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speaker embeddings, or x-vectors, are widely used to represent speaker identity and speaker-related attributes, but existing embedding extractors are typically descriptive rather than generative: they map an observed speech segment to an x-vector, which is then used for downstream applications. We introduce ProPS, Prompted Profile Synthesis, a framework for generating distributions of speaker embeddings conditioned on natural language prompts such as "a thirties male speaker with an Indian accent". ProPS converts human-written profile descriptions into sentence embeddings and uses a mixture density network trained on a large-scale dataset to predict a Gaussian mixture model in the x-vector space. The model is trained by maximizing the likelihood that real speaker embeddings match the requested profile, and its generated distributions are evaluated by negative log-likelihood on held-out x-vectors and by attribute classification accuracies on sampled synthetic x-vectors. Experiments show that ProPS produces profile-conditioned distributions and generates x-vectors that preserve requested speaker attributes such as age, gender, accent, and prosodic characteristics. This design enables controllable speaker-profile synthesis for speech generation systems like Text-To-Speech (TTS) or Voice Conversion (VC) while anchoring generated distributions in observed speaker-embedding structure.

标题：ProPS：基于自然语言提示的说话人嵌入分布合成框架

Motivated by the need for controllable speaker synthesis in speech applications, ProPS introduces a framework that generates distributions of speaker embeddings conditioned on natural language prompts. 为实现语音生成系统中的可控说话人合成，ProPS提出一种基于自然语言提示生成说话人嵌入分布的框架。该方法将文本描述转化为句子嵌入，并利用在大规模数据集上训练的混合密度网络，在x向量空间建模高斯混合分布，通过最大化真实嵌入与提示的对齐概率进行优化。实验表明，ProPS能有效生成保留年龄、性别、口音和语调等属性的合成x向量，其性能通过负对数似然和采样向量的属性分类准确率得到验证。
Erasing Without Collateral Damage: Precise Concept Removal in Diffusion Models
Authors: Parth Upman, Nishita Jain, Shreyank N Gowda
First: 2026-07-06T16:21:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:21:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Training-free concept erasure is an attractive mechanism for controlling text-to-image diffusion models, but precise erasure often comes at the cost of damaging semantically related non-target concepts. Existing value-space methods remove the component of each cross-attention value along the target concept direction, implicitly treating target identity and shared visual structure as the same signal. We argue that this is the source of much of the collateral damage in prior preservation. We introduce CARE, a closed-form concept erasure operator that replaces the raw target direction with a kept-subspace-aware direction computed from a small bank of retained concept anchors. The resulting edit is applied directly in cross-attention value space, requires no model fine-tuning, and adds only a negligible offline computation. A single shrinkage parameter controls the erase-preserve trade-off. We further show that the operator admits a minimum-disturbance interpretation and, in its projection form, leaves the kept subspace invariant. Experiments under the standard concept-erasure protocol show that our method preserves non-target concepts more faithfully while maintaining competitive erasure across instance, style, and celebrity concepts. Code: https://github.com/parthupman/care
中文标题/摘要
标题：无副作用的概念擦除：扩散模型中的精确概念移除
无需训练的概念擦除是控制文本到图像扩散模型的一种有吸引力的机制，但精确擦除往往以损害语义相关的非目标概念为代价。现有值空间方法沿目标概念方向移除每个交叉注意力值的分量，隐式地将目标身份与共享视觉结构视为同一信号。我们认为，这是先前保真度方法中大量副作用的根源。我们提出了CARE，一种闭式概念擦除算子，它用从少量保留概念锚点计算出的保留子空间感知方向替代原始目标方向。该编辑直接应用于交叉注意力值空间，无需模型微调，仅增加微不足道的离线计算。一个收缩参数控制擦除与保留的权衡。我们进一步表明，该算子具有最小扰动解释，在投影形式下保持保留子空间不变。在标准概念擦除协议下的实验表明，我们的方法在保留非目标概念方面更加忠实，同时在实例、风格和名人概念的擦除效果上保持竞争力。代码：https://github.com/parthupman/care
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to remove specific concepts from text-to-image diffusion models without damaging related visual structures, the authors propose CARE, a training-free concept erasure method that avoids the collateral damage of existing value-space approaches. CARE replaces the target concept direction in cross-attention values with a direction computed from retained concept anchors, preserving the subspace of non-target concepts while enabling precise erasure. Experiments demonstrate that CARE achieves superior preservation of non-target concepts across diverse categories while maintaining competitive erasure performance, all without requiring model fine-tuning or significant computational overhead.
为在消除扩散模型中特定概念的同时避免损害相关视觉元素，作者提出CARE方法，该方法在交叉注意力值空间中用保留概念锚点计算出的方向替代目标概念方向，从而实现更精准的保留。该方法无需模型微调，仅用一个收缩参数控制消除与保留的平衡。实验表明，CARE在实例、风格和名人概念上保持了对非目标概念的更忠实保留，同时消除效果具有竞争力，且计算开销极小。
Adaptive Inference Batching using Policy Gradients
Authors: Ruslan Sharifullin
First: 2026-07-06T16:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:20:19+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 5 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inference serving systems must balance throughput and latency under bursty, heterogeneous workloads, yet the industry standard remains static batching policies that require manual tuning and cannot adapt to shifting traffic. We investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) can learn adaptive batching and routing policies that outperform these heuristics, training REINFORCE and PPO agents on a discrete-event simulator validated against queuing theory and production traces (Azure Functions, BurstGPT). We formulate the problem as an MDP over queue state, request type and GPU availability, evaluating across standard Poisson traffic, extreme bursts, real-world traces and heterogeneous multi-GPU routing. Our central finding is a clear boundary condition for RL's value in systems problems. In single-GPU settings, a well-tuned static batching policy is already near-optimal under Poisson-like arrivals and RL offers only marginal gains (+0.1% to +1.0%). In multi-GPU heterogeneous routing, however, where fast and slow requests compete for shared resources, the agent discovers a workload-segregation policy that eliminates Head-of-Line blocking, yielding a 3.5x (348%) improvement over Round-Robin and a 48% improvement over the strongest heuristic baseline (Shortest-Queue), with 60% higher throughput and 25% lower latency while respecting SLA constraints. The policy generalizes to unseen bursty and real-world traffic despite training only on synthetic Poisson arrivals and an attention-augmented policy network converges roughly 20% faster than an MLP baseline. These results suggest RL's advantage over engineered heuristics concentrates in combinatorial, multi-resource decisions rather than single-resource temporal scheduling, a practical distinction for deciding where learned policies justify their engineering cost in production inference infrastructure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用策略梯度的自适应推理批处理
推理服务系统必须在突发且异构的工作负载下平衡吞吐量与延迟，但业界标准仍是静态批处理策略，需手动调优且无法适应流量变化。我们研究强化学习（RL）是否能学习优于这些启发式方法的自适应批处理与路由策略，通过离散事件模拟器训练REINFORCE和PPO代理，该模拟器经排队论和生产轨迹（Azure Functions、BurstGPT）验证。我们将问题形式化为状态空间包含队列状态、请求类型和GPU可用性的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），评估标准泊松流量、极端突发、真实世界轨迹和异构多GPU路由。 我们的核心发现是RL在系统问题中的价值存在明确的边界条件。在单GPU设置下，经过良好调优的静态批处理策略在泊松式到达下已近最优，RL仅带来微小收益（+0.1%至+1.0%）。但在多GPU异构路由中，快慢请求竞争共享资源时，代理发现一种工作负载隔离策略，消除Head-of-Line阻塞，相较轮询调度提升3.5倍（348%），相较最强启发式基线（最短队列）提升48%，吞吐量提高60%，延迟降低25%，同时满足SLA约束。该策略即使仅在合成泊松到达上训练，仍能泛化至未见过的突发与真实流量；注意力增强的策略网络收敛速度比MLP基线快约20%。 这些结果表明，RL相较于人工启发式方法的优势集中于组合型、多资源决策，而非单资源时序调度，这对判断在生产推理基础设施中何处值得投入学习策略的工程成本具有实际意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static batching policies in handling bursty and heterogeneous inference workloads, this work explores whether reinforcement learning can learn adaptive policies that outperform manual heuristics. Using REINFORCE and PPO agents trained on a discrete-event simulator validated against queuing theory and real-world traces, the study frames the problem as an MDP over queue state, request type, and GPU availability. Results show that in single-GPU settings, RL offers only marginal gains over well-tuned static policies, but in multi-GPU heterogeneous routing, RL discovers a workload-segregation policy that eliminates Head-of-Line blocking, achieving 3.5x throughput improvement over Round-Robin and 48% over the best heuristic, with 60% higher throughput and 25% lower latency while respecting SLAs.
为应对突发且异构的推理负载，传统静态批处理策略存在手动调优且无法自适应的问题，本文探索强化学习是否能学习优于人工启发式策略的自适应批处理与路由策略。通过在符合排队论和真实生产数据（Azure Functions、BurstGPT）验证的离散事件模拟器上训练REINFORCE和PPO智能体，将问题建模为队列状态、请求类型与GPU可用性构成的MDP。结果表明，在单GPU场景下，RL对已调优的静态策略仅提供微小提升，但在多GPU异构路由场景中，RL发现一种工作负载隔离策略，消除Head-of-Line阻塞，相较轮询策略提升3.5倍，较最优启发式策略提升48%，同时吞吐量提升60%、延迟降低25%且满足SLA约束。该策略即使在训练仅使用合成泊松流量的情况下，仍能泛化至未见的突发与真实流量，且注意力增强的策略网络收敛速度比MLP快约20%。
Target-Guided Selective Reweighting for Physics-Informed Neural Network Inverse Problems: A Transfer Learning Approach
Authors: Qian Hu, Bin Fan, Yao Xiao, Zhicheng Lin, Meixin Xiong
First: 2026-07-06T16:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:20:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) encounter ill-posed optimization, loss competition, and parameter compensation in partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems. Transfer learning can reuse representations from source tasks, but direct fine-tuning may introduce negative transfer when dominant physical mechanisms, governing parameters, or observation noise differ between source and target domains: the model achieves low field error yet recovers incorrect target physical parameters. To mitigate, we propose Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN), a target-evidence-driven representation correction method for PINN inverse transfer learning. TGSR-PINN transfers only the weights and biases from the source PINN, while target physical parameters are independently initialized; after a short target-adaptation phase, the method computes neuron target scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance on fixed scoring batches, and converts evidence associated with low-scoring neurons into continuous weak-adaptation signals via a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) with rank fallback. TGSR-PINN then applies selective soft decay to input weight rows and biases of low-scoring neurons instead of hard pruning or random resetting. In experiments, TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery while maintaining comparable field accuracy in the high-Péclet 2D advection-diffusion task and in the Allen--Cahn to Burgers cross-PDE-family transfer task; a 5%-noise reaction--diffusion case provides supplementary evidence under milder source-target mismatch. Ablation studies suggest that neuron target scoring, weak-adaptation signal estimation, layer protection, and selective soft decay jointly contribute to the benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向物理信息神经网络逆问题的目标引导选择性重加权：一种迁移学习方法
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在偏微分方程（PDE）逆问题中面临病态优化、损失竞争和参数补偿等问题。迁移学习可复用源任务的表示，但直接微调可能在源域与目标域主导物理机制、控制参数或观测噪声不同时引入负迁移：模型虽获得低场误差，却恢复错误的目标物理参数。为缓解此问题，本文提出目标引导选择性重加权PINN（TGSR-PINN），一种针对PINN逆问题迁移学习的目标证据驱动表示校正方法。TGSR-PINN仅迁移源PINN的权重与偏置，目标物理参数独立初始化；经过短暂目标适应阶段后，该方法利用一阶泰勒敏感度与预激活方差在固定评分批次上计算神经元目标得分，并通过高斯混合模型（GMM）结合秩回退机制，将低得分神经元关联的证据转化为连续弱适应信号。随后，TGSR-PINN对低得分神经元的输入权重行与偏置施加选择性软衰减，而非硬剪枝或随机重置。实验表明，在高佩克莱数二维对流扩散任务及艾伦-查恩至伯格斯跨PDE族迁移任务中，TGSR-PINN提升目标参数恢复精度，同时保持相近场精度；5%噪声反应-扩散案例在源-目标不匹配较轻时提供补充证据。消融研究表明，神经元目标评分、弱适应信号估计、层保护及选择性软衰减共同贡献于性能提升。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenges of ill-posed optimization and loss competition in physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for inverse PDE problems, the paper proposes TGSR-PINN, a transfer learning method that selectively reweights neurons based on target-evidence-driven scores. It transfers only weights and biases from a source PINN while initializing target parameters independently, then computes neuron scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance. Low-scoring neurons are softened via a Gaussian mixture model to generate weak-adaptation signals, and their input weights and biases are decayed selectively rather than pruned. Experiments show TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery without sacrificing field accuracy in high-Péclet advection-diffusion and cross-PDE-family tasks, with ablation studies confirming the joint contribution of scoring, signal estimation, layer protection, and soft decay.
为解决物理信息神经网络（PINN）在偏微分方程逆问题中的病态优化与损失竞争问题，本文提出TGSR-PINN方法，通过目标证据驱动的神经元重加权实现迁移学习。该方法仅迁移源PINN的权重与偏置，目标参数独立初始化，随后进行短时目标适应，再基于一阶泰勒敏感度与预激活方差计算神经元得分，对低分神经元施加高斯混合模型生成的弱适应信号，避免硬剪枝或随机重置。实验表明，TGSR-PINN在高佩克莱数对流扩散及跨PDE族任务中显著提升目标参数恢复精度，同时保持场精度相当，且在源目标差异较小时亦有补充验证。
Statistical Taylor Expansion: A New and Path-Independent Method for Uncertainty Analysis
Authors: Chengpu Wang
First: 2024-10-02T04:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:18:37+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 43 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Statistical Taylor expansion is a rigorous extension of conventional Taylor expansion that replaces each precise input variable with a random variable of known distribution and sample count, then computes the mean, deviation, and a bounding reliability of every result. By tracking the propagation of input uncertainties through all intermediate steps, it renders the final result path-independent, with precise quantification of the tracking quality. This path-independence sets it fundamentally apart from conventional numerical approaches, which are path-dependent. This study presents an implementation called variance arithmetic and demonstrates its performance across diverse mathematical applications. This study also reveals the potentially substantial impact of numerical errors in library functions, the defect of applying input uncertainties as weights in conventional regression, and the modeling error of the discrete Fourier transformation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：统计泰勒展开：一种新的路径无关的不确定性分析方法
统计泰勒展开是对传统泰勒展开的严谨扩展，将每个精确输入变量替换为具有已知分布和样本数的随机变量，然后计算每个结果的均值、偏差及置信下界。通过追踪输入不确定性在所有中间步骤中的传播，该方法使最终结果路径无关，并精确量化追踪质量。这种路径无关性使其与路径依赖的传统数值方法根本不同。本研究提出了一种名为方差算术的实现方法，并展示了其在多种数学应用中的表现。此外，本研究还揭示了库函数中数值误差的潜在重大影响、传统回归中将输入不确定性作为权重的缺陷，以及离散傅里叶变换的建模误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a path-independent uncertainty analysis method, this study introduces statistical Taylor expansion, which replaces deterministic inputs with random variables and computes mean, deviation, and reliability bounds for outputs. Implemented as variance arithmetic, the method tracks uncertainty propagation through all computational steps without path dependence, distinguishing it from conventional numerical approaches. Experimental results across diverse mathematical applications confirm its accuracy and robustness, while also exposing significant numerical errors in standard library functions, flaws in using input uncertainties as regression weights, and modeling inaccuracies in discrete Fourier transforms.
为解决传统数值方法路径依赖导致的不确定性分析不一致问题，该研究提出统计泰勒展开法，将确定性输入变量替换为已知分布的随机变量，并计算输出的均值、偏差与可靠性边界。该方法以方差算术形式实现，能全程追踪不确定性传播而不受路径影响，显著区别于传统数值方法。实验结果在多种数学应用中验证了其精度与鲁棒性，同时揭示了标准库函数中的数值误差、输入不确定性作为回归权重的缺陷，以及离散傅里叶变换的建模误差。
SAVER: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Style-Aware Visual Early Revision
Authors: Zhaoxu Li, Chenqi Kong, Yi Yu, Qiangqiang Wu, Xinghao Jiang, Ngai-Man Cheung, Bihan Wen, Alex Kot, Xudong Jiang
Venue: AAAI 2026
First: 2025-08-05T07:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:18:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted at AAAI 2026. 24 pages, 10 figures. Code: https://github.com/llizhaoxu/SAVER
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) recently achieve significant breakthroughs in understanding complex visual-textual contexts. However, hallucination issues still limit their real-world applicability. Although previous mitigation methods effectively reduce hallucinations in photographic images, they largely overlook the potential risks posed by stylized images, which play crucial roles in critical scenarios such as game scene understanding, art education, and medical analysis. In this work, we first construct a dataset comprising photographic images and their corresponding stylized versions with carefully annotated caption labels. We then conduct head-to-head comparisons on both discriminative and generative tasks by benchmarking 13 advanced LVLMs on the collected datasets. Our findings reveal that stylized images tend to induce significantly more hallucinations than their photographic counterparts. To address this issue, we propose Style-Aware Visual Early Revision SAVER, a novel mechanism that dynamically adjusts LVLMs' final outputs based on the token-level visual attention patterns, leveraging early-layer feedback to mitigate hallucinations caused by stylized images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAVER achieves state-of-the-art performance in hallucination mitigation across various models, datasets, and tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAVER：通过风格感知的视觉早期修订缓解大型视觉-语言模型中的幻觉
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）最近在理解复杂视觉-文本上下文方面取得了显著突破。然而，幻觉问题仍限制了其在现实世界中的应用。尽管先前的缓解方法能有效减少摄影图像中的幻觉，但它们大多忽视了风格化图像所潜藏的风险，而风格化图像在游戏场景理解、艺术教育和医学分析等关键场景中起着重要作用。在本研究中，我们首先构建了一个包含摄影图像及其对应风格化版本的数据集，并对这些图像进行了精心标注的标题标签。随后，我们在收集的数据集上对13个先进的LVLMs进行了判别和生成任务的头对头比较。我们的发现表明，风格化图像比摄影图像更容易引发显著更多的幻觉。为解决此问题，我们提出了风格感知视觉早期修订（SAVER），一种新颖机制，该机制根据词级视觉注意力模式动态调整LVLMs的最终输出，利用早期层反馈缓解由风格化图像引起的幻觉。大量实验表明，SAVER在各种模型、数据集和任务中均实现了幻觉缓解的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent hallucination problem in large vision-language models (LVLMs) when processing stylized images—often overlooked in prior work—the authors construct a dataset pairing photographic and stylized images with annotated captions. They evaluate 13 LVLMs on discriminative and generative tasks, finding stylized images induce significantly more hallucinations. To address this, they propose SAVER, a style-aware mechanism that revises LVLM outputs early based on token-level visual attention, using feedback from lower layers to reduce hallucinations. Experiments show SAVER outperforms existing methods across multiple models, datasets, and tasks in hallucination mitigation.
鉴于现有方法在处理风格化图像时易引发幻觉且常被忽视，作者构建了包含照片与风格化图像配对及标注语义的基准数据集。通过在13个先进LVLM上进行判别与生成任务对比，发现风格化图像显著加剧幻觉问题。为此，提出SAVER机制，利用早期视觉注意力模式动态修正输出，有效缓解风格化图像导致的幻觉。实验表明，SAVER在多种模型、数据集与任务中均达到最优幻觉抑制效果。
Is the Geometry Doing the Work? An Operating-Point Audit of Hierarchy in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models
Authors: Jaeyoung Kim, Eunseok Kim, Dongsuk Jang
First: 2026-07-06T16:18:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:18:05+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 5 figures, Under review at TMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether a hyperbolic representation model uses its geometry cannot be read off its curvature parameter: what matters is the dimensionless operating point $\sqrt{c}ρ$ and whether the radial and cone machinery is active there. We develop a battery of necessary-condition diagnostics and audit three published hyperbolic vision-language families -- MERU, HyCoCLIP, and PHyCLIP -- across released checkpoints and controlled interventions on a fixed GRIT snapshot, identifying three failure modes. First, curvature is not an active resource: the operating point stays near-Euclidean ($H(u)\approx 1$; no audited converged checkpoint reaches $\sqrt{c}ρ>1$), and releasing the curvature floor moves curvature and norms but keeps the operating point near-Euclidean, without substantial downstream degradation. Second, the cone and traversal machinery is measured inoperative: entailment cones are inactive, saturated, or misaligned, and graded traversal fails under controlled readouts, while directed radial depth is a bounded non-detection above shuffle-null controls at quantified sensitivity; the one surviving native-relation residual remains non-operative. Third, hierarchy-looking evaluations are underdetermined: taxonomy correlations are carried by angular distance, and coarse-retrieval gains track box/compositional supervision, not curvature. A mechanistic account explains why: the entailment objective admits a low-curvature, wide-cone shortcut, and a parameter-free aperture identity (cones saturate iff $\sqrt{c}ρ\le 2K$) locates the edge where every entailment-trained unclamped run settles; entailment-off runs show no arrest there. The shortcut is the dominant accelerator of collapse, not its sole cause. These formulations, as released, do not instantiate the radial/cone mechanism their geometry motivates; we distill the audit into a five-number geometry report for future hierarchy claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：几何是否在发挥作用？对超曲面视觉-语言模型层级结构的运行点审计
超曲面表示模型是否使用其几何结构，不能仅从曲率参数判断：关键在于无量纲运行点 $\sqrt{c}ρ$ 以及该点处径向和锥体机制是否激活。我们开发了一系列必要条件诊断工具，并在三个已发表的超曲面视觉-语言模型家族——MERU、HyCoCLIP 和 PHyCLIP——的发布检查点及固定 GRIT 数据集上的受控干预中进行审计，识别出三种失效模式。第一，曲率并非活跃资源：运行点始终接近欧氏空间（$H(u)\approx 1$；所有审计的收敛检查点均未达到 $\sqrt{c}ρ>1$），释放曲率下限虽改变曲率与范数，但运行点仍保持近欧氏，且下游性能无显著退化。第二，锥体与遍历机制被测为无效：蕴含锥体不活跃、饱和或错位，受控读取下分级遍历失败，定向径向深度在量化敏感度下仅高于洗牌-零控制，且唯一存活的原生关系残差仍不运作。第三，看似层级的评估是欠定的：分类学相关性由角度距离承载，粗粒度检索增益与方框/组合监督相关，而非曲率。机制解释如下：蕴含目标允许低曲率、宽锥体捷径，且无参数的孔径恒等式（锥体饱和当且仅当 $\sqrt{c}ρ\le 2K$）标定出所有无约束训练的蕴含模型收敛的边界；关闭蕴含训练的模型在此处无停滞。该捷径是崩溃的主要加速器，而非唯一原因。这些模型在发布形式下，并未实现其几何结构所激励的径向/锥体机制；我们将审计结果浓缩为一份五项几何报告，以供未来层级结构主张参考。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether hyperbolic vision-language models genuinely leverage their geometric structure for hierarchical representation, finding that curvature alone does not determine geometric usage; instead, the dimensionless operating point √cρ and activation of radial/cone mechanisms are critical. The authors audit three models — MERU, HyCoCLIP, and PHyCLIP — across checkpoints and controlled interventions, revealing three failure modes: (1) curvature remains near-Euclidean despite parameter changes, with no significant downstream degradation; (2) entailment cones are inactive or misaligned, and radial depth fails to detect hierarchy under controlled readouts; and (3) hierarchy claims rely on angular distance or compositional supervision, not curvature. A mechanistic explanation shows that entailment objectives admit a low-curvature shortcut that dominates collapse, preventing true geometric hierarchy from emerging.
该论文探讨超几何视觉语言模型是否真正利用其几何结构实现层次化表征，发现曲率参数本身无法决定几何作用，关键在于无量纲操作点√cρ及径向/圆锥机制是否激活。作者对MERU、HyCoCLIP和PHyCLIP三个模型在多个检查点和控制干预下进行审计，发现三类失效模式：（1）曲率虽可调整，但操作点始终近似欧氏，无显著下游性能下降；（2）蕴含圆锥机制不活跃或错位，径向深度在控制读取下无法超越基线；（3）层次评估指标与角距离或监督类型相关，而非曲率。机制上，蕴含目标存在低曲率捷径主导坍缩，且参数无关的开口恒等式解释了模型为何在特定操作点稳定，但无模型真正激活其几何所预设的径向/圆锥机制。
Learning to Discover Iterative Spectral Algorithms
Authors: Zihang Liu, Oleg Balabanov, Yaoqing Yang, Michael W. Mahoney
First: 2026-02-10T08:39:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:15:39+00:00
Comments: Code available at: https://github.com/zihanghliu/AutoSpec
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce AutoSpec, a neural network framework for discovering iterative spectral algorithms for large-scale numerical linear algebra and numerical optimization. Our self-supervised models adapt to input operators using coarse spectral information (e.g., eigenvalue estimates and residual norms), and predict recurrence coefficients for computing or applying a matrix polynomial tailored to a downstream task. The effectiveness of AutoSpec relies on three ingredients: an architecture whose inference pass implements short, executable numerical linear algebra recurrences; efficient training on small synthetic problems with transfer to large-scale real-world operators; and task-defined objectives that enforce the desired approximation or preconditioning behavior across the range of spectral profiles represented in the training set. We apply AutoSpec to discovering algorithms for representative tasks on spd matrices: accelerating matrix function approximation; accelerating sparse linear solvers; and spectral filtering/preconditioning for eigenvalue computations. On real-world matrices, the learned procedures deliver up to order-of-magnitude improvements in accuracy and/or reductions in iteration count, relative to spectrum-agnostic baselines. We find clear connections to classical theory: the induced polynomials may exhibit equioscillation behavior characteristic of Chebyshev polynomial approximation. The code is available at: https://github.com/zihanghliu/AutoSpec .
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习发现迭代谱算法
我们提出了AutoSpec，一个用于发现大规模数值线性代数和数值优化中迭代谱算法的神经网络框架。我们的自监督模型利用粗略的谱信息（如特征值估计和残差范数）适应输入算子，并预测递推系数，以计算或应用针对下游任务定制的矩阵多项式。AutoSpec的有效性依赖于三个要素：一种其推理过程实现简短、可执行数值线性代数递推的架构；在小型合成问题上高效训练并迁移到大规模真实算子；以及任务定义的目标，强制在训练集中表示的各类谱分布上实现所需的近似或预处理行为。我们将AutoSpec应用于对称正定矩阵代表性任务：加速矩阵函数近似；加速稀疏线性求解器；以及特征值计算的谱滤波/预处理。在真实矩阵上，所学习的程序相对于谱无关基线，在精度或迭代次数上可实现数量级的提升。我们发现其与经典理论存在明确联系：所诱导的多项式可能表现出切比雪夫多项式近似特有的等振荡行为。代码可在：https://github.com/zihanghliu/AutoSpec 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptive spectral algorithms in large-scale numerical linear algebra, AutoSpec employs self-supervised neural networks that learn to predict recurrence coefficients for matrix polynomials based on coarse spectral information such as eigenvalue estimates and residual norms. The framework is designed with an architecture that executes short numerical recurrences during inference, trains efficiently on small synthetic problems with transfer to real-world operators, and uses task-specific objectives to enforce desired spectral behavior. Experimental results show that AutoSpec achieves up to one order of magnitude improvement in accuracy or iteration reduction over spectrum-agnostic baselines across tasks like matrix function approximation, sparse linear solver acceleration, and spectral preconditioning for eigenvalue computations, with learned polynomials exhibiting equioscillation properties reminiscent of Chebyshev approximations.
为解决大规模数值线性代数中谱算法的自适应需求，AutoSpec 是一种神经网络框架，通过利用粗略谱信息（如特征值估计和残差范数）学习发现迭代谱算法。该模型预测针对下游任务定制的矩阵多项式递推系数，其架构支持推理阶段执行短小的数值递推。通过在小规模合成问题上训练并迁移到大规模真实算子，AutoSpec 相较于谱无关基线方法，在精度或迭代次数上提升达一个数量级，所学多项式常表现出类似切比雪夫多项式的等振荡特性，与经典逼近理论形成明显关联。
Creating Group Rules with AI: Human-AI Collaboration in WhatsApp Moderation
Authors: Gauri Nayak, Farhana Shahid, Kiran Garimella, Aditya Vashistha
First: 2026-05-12T18:02:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:14:02+00:00
Comments: CSCW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
WhatsApp is one of the most widely used messaging platforms globally, with billions of users sharing information in private groups. Yet, it offers little infrastructure to support moderation and group governance. In the absence of platform-level oversight, group admins bear the responsibility of governing group behavior. In this paper, we explore how WhatsApp group admins collaborate with AI tools to create, enforce, and maintain group rules. Drawing on a two-phase speculative design study with 20 admins in India, we examine how participants interacted with an AI assistant (Meta AI) to co-create rules and responded to a series of probes illustrating AI-assisted moderation features. Our findings show that while admins appreciated the AI's ability to surface overlooked rules and reduce their moderation burden, they were highly sensitive to issues of relational trust, data privacy, tone, and social context. We identify how group type and admin style shaped their willingness to delegate authority, and surface the limitations of current chatbot interfaces in supporting collaborative rule-making. We conclude with design implications for building moderation tools that center human judgment, relational nuance, contextual adaptability, and collective governance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用人工智能创建群组规则：WhatsApp管理中的人机协作
WhatsApp是全球使用最广泛的即时通讯平台之一，拥有数十亿用户在私密群组中分享信息。然而，该平台缺乏支持管理和群组治理的基础设施。在缺乏平台级监管的情况下，群组管理员承担着规范群组行为的责任。本文探讨了WhatsApp群组管理员如何与AI工具协作，共同制定、执行和维护群组规则。基于对印度20名管理员的两阶段推测性设计研究，我们考察了参与者如何与AI助手（Meta AI）共同制定规则，并对一系列展示AI辅助管理功能的探针作出反应。研究发现，尽管管理员赞赏AI能揭示被忽视的规则并减轻其管理负担，但他们对关系信任、数据隐私、语气及社会语境等问题极为敏感。我们识别出群组类型和管理员风格如何影响其授权意愿，并揭示当前聊天机器人界面在支持协作规则制定方面的局限性。最后，我们提出设计建议，旨在构建以人类判断为核心、重视关系微妙性、情境适应性及集体治理的管理工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of built-in moderation tools in WhatsApp groups, this study investigates how human admins collaborate with AI assistants to co-create and enforce group rules. Through a two-phase speculative design study with 20 Indian admins, researchers observed how participants interacted with Meta AI to draft rules and responded to simulated moderation scenarios. Results showed admins valued AI for surfacing overlooked rules and reducing workload, but remained cautious about trust, privacy, tone, and social context. Admins’ willingness to delegate varied by group type and personal style, and current chatbot interfaces were found insufficient for nuanced collaborative rule-making, suggesting future tools must prioritize human judgment, relational sensitivity, and contextual adaptability.
鉴于WhatsApp群组缺乏内置管理工具，本研究探讨了群组管理员如何与AI助手协作制定和执行群规。通过对20名印度管理员进行两阶段的推测性设计研究，研究人员观察了他们如何使用Meta AI起草规则并回应模拟的管理场景。结果表明，管理员认可AI在揭示被忽略规则和减轻工作负担方面的价值，但对信任、隐私、语气及社交语境极为敏感。管理员是否愿意授权取决于群组类型和个人风格，现有聊天机器人界面在支持协作制规方面存在不足，提示未来工具应优先考虑人类判断、关系敏感性和情境适应性。
AnchorDream: Repurposing Video Diffusion for Embodiment-Aware Robot Data Synthesis
Authors: Junjie Ye, Rong Xue, Basile Van Hoorick, Pavel Tokmakov, Muhammad Zubair Irshad, Yue Wang, Vitor Guizilini
First: 2025-12-12T18:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:12:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://jay-ye.github.io/AnchorDream/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The collection of large-scale and diverse robot demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for imitation learning, as real-world data acquisition is costly and simulators offer limited diversity and fidelity with pronounced sim-to-real gaps. While generative models present an attractive solution, existing methods often alter only visual appearances without creating new behaviors, or suffer from embodiment inconsistencies that yield implausible motions. To address these limitations, we introduce AnchorDream, an embodiment-aware world model that repurposes pretrained video diffusion models for robot data synthesis. AnchorDream conditions the diffusion process on robot motion renderings, anchoring the embodiment to prevent hallucination while synthesizing objects and environments consistent with the robot's kinematics. Starting from only a handful of human teleoperation demonstrations, our method scales them into large, diverse, high-quality datasets without requiring explicit environment modeling. Experiments show that the generated data leads to consistent improvements in downstream policy learning, with relative gains of 36.4% in simulator benchmarks and nearly double performance in real-world studies. These results suggest that grounding generative world models in robot motion provides a practical path toward scaling imitation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnchorDream：利用视频扩散模型实现具身感知的机器人数据合成
大规模、多样化的机器人演示数据收集仍是模仿学习的主要瓶颈，因为真实世界数据采集成本高昂，而模拟器在多样性与保真度方面有限，且存在显著的仿真到现实差距。尽管生成模型提供了有吸引力的解决方案，但现有方法要么仅改变视觉外观而未生成新行为，要么因具身不一致导致运动不真实。为解决这些问题，我们提出了AnchorDream，一种具身感知的世界模型，重新利用预训练的视频扩散模型进行机器人数据合成。AnchorDream将机器人运动渲染作为扩散过程的条件，锚定具身以防止幻觉，同时合成与机器人运动学一致的物体和环境。仅从少量人类遥操作演示出发，我们的方法即可将其扩展为大规模、多样且高质量的数据集，无需显式环境建模。实验表明，生成的数据在下游策略学习中带来持续改进，模拟器基准相对提升36.4%，真实世界实验性能几乎翻倍。这些结果表明，将生成世界模型锚定于机器人运动，是扩展模仿学习的实用路径。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the scarcity and cost of real robot demonstrations, AnchorDream repurposes pretrained video diffusion models to synthesize diverse, embodiment-consistent robot data by conditioning generation on motion renderings, thereby anchoring physical plausibility and preventing hallucinations. Starting from only a few human teleoperation examples, it scales them into large, high-fidelity datasets without explicit environment modeling. Experimental results show that policies trained on this synthetic data achieve 36.4% relative improvement in simulation and nearly double performance in real-world tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-grounded generative models for imitation learning.
为解决真实机器人数据获取成本高、仿真环境与现实差距大等问题，AnchorDream 利用预训练视频扩散模型，结合机器人运动渲染图约束生成过程，确保合成动作符合机器人本体动力学，无需显式建模环境。仅从少量人类遥操作演示出发，即可扩展生成大规模、多样、高质量的机器人数据集，显著提升下游策略学习效果：仿真环境中提升36.4%，真实世界任务性能近翻倍。
SteelBench: Evaluating Vision-Language Models in Real-World Industrial Environments
Authors: Suryanarayana Reddy Yarrabothula, Manisha Chawla, Kunal Sinha, Gagan Raj Gupta, Sashank Lekkala, Ashirvadhan Dosapati, Saikamal Nannuri, Katragadda Ajay RamaSwamy Chowdary Gowtham
First: 2026-07-06T16:11:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:11:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing video benchmarks evaluate action recognition on consumer videos, egocentric recordings, or simulated industrial environments. They do not test vision-language models under the visual and procedural conditions of real industrial CCTV, where workers appear as distant figures amid dust, steam, low light, glare, occlusion, and overlapping activities. We introduce STEELBENCH, a diagnostic benchmark for industrial surveillance that jointly evaluates per-worker activity recognition, safety-rule reasoning, and annotation provenance. SteelBench contains 1,345 densely annotated clips, curated from 149 hours of operational plant footage and 10,024 candidate clips using temporal deduplication, class balancing, and visibility-aware stratified sampling. Each clip includes dense per-worker action labels, PPE attributes, spatial context, and safety-rule annotations. Because model-assisted annotation can shape the labels later used for model evaluation, SteelBench includes a provenance-aware audit protocol. The protocol measures label influence, evaluates sensitivity to ground-truth provenance, and reports a human reference from expert-reviewed labels. Applying this audit, we find that unaudited VLM-sourced ground truth can inflate same-family model accuracy by up to 17 percentage points. Across nine VLMs from four architectural families, the best model reaches only 42.6% action accuracy, compared with an 84.6% human benchmark. Performance also fragments across recognition, robustness, calibration, and safety reasoning. Even when models predict the correct action, 37-58% of cases still yield incorrect safety judgments, and no model passes more than 2 of 5 diagnostic checks. The dataset is publicly available on Hugging Face.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SteelBench：在真实工业环境中评估视觉-语言模型
现有视频基准测试主要评估消费级视频、自拍视频或模拟工业环境中的动作识别，但未在真实工业CCTV环境下测试视觉-语言模型，该环境存在灰尘、蒸汽、低光、反光、遮挡及重叠活动等视觉与程序条件，工人常以远距离身影出现。我们提出STEELBENCH，一个用于工业监控的诊断基准，联合评估每位工人的活动识别、安全规则推理及标注溯源。SteelBench包含1,345个密集标注片段，从149小时的工厂运行视频及10,024个候选片段中精选，通过时间去重、类别平衡及可见性感知分层采样构建。每个片段包含每位工人的密集动作标签、个人防护装备属性、空间上下文及安全规则标注。因模型辅助标注可能影响后续模型评估所用标签，SteelBench包含溯源感知审计协议，用于测量标签影响、评估对真实溯源的敏感性，并报告专家审核标签的人类参考。应用该审计发现，未经审计的VLM来源真实标签可使同家族模型准确率虚高17个百分点。在四类架构的九个VLM中，最佳模型仅达42.6%的动作准确率，远低于人类基准84.6%。性能在识别、鲁棒性、校准及安全推理方面呈现碎片化。即使模型预测正确动作，37-58%的案例仍导致错误安全判断，且无模型通过5项诊断检查中的2项。该数据集已在Hugging Face公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of real-world industrial benchmarks for vision-language models, this paper introduces SteelBench, a diagnostic dataset derived from 149 hours of plant CCTV footage, designed to evaluate per-worker activity recognition, safety rule reasoning, and annotation provenance under challenging visual conditions like dust, glare, and occlusion. The dataset comprises 1,345 densely annotated clips, curated via temporal deduplication and visibility-aware sampling, each labeled with per-worker actions, PPE, spatial context, and safety rules. An audit protocol reveals that unverified VLM-generated labels can inflate model accuracy by up to 17 percentage points, and despite the best model achieving 42.6% action accuracy (vs. 84.6% human baseline), performance is fragmented: models often fail safety reasoning even when predicting correct actions, and none pass more than two of five diagnostic checks.
为填补视觉语言模型在真实工业环境中的评估空白，该研究构建了SteelBench数据集，用于在尘雾、低光、遮挡等真实工厂CCTV条件下测试模型性能。数据集包含1345个经过密集标注的视频片段，源自149小时工厂运行录像，采用可见性感知采样与标注溯源机制确保标注质量。实验表明，未经审核的VLM生成标注可使模型准确率虚高17个百分点，最优模型仅达到42.6%的动作识别准确率（人类基准为84.6%），且在安全推理方面表现不佳——即使动作预测正确，仍有37%-58%案例安全判断错误，无模型通过五项诊断中的两项以上。
Towards Generalizable Deepfake Image Detection with Vision Transformers
Authors: Kaliki V Srinanda, M Manvith Prabhu, Hemanth K Mogilipalem, Jayavarapu S Abhinai, Vaibhav Santhosh, Aryan Herur, Deepu Vijayasenan
Venue: ICASSP 2025
First: 2026-04-19T10:59:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:10:31+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 9 figures, SP Cup - ICASSP 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In today's day and age, we face a challenge in detecting deepfake images because of the fast evolution of modern generative models and the poor generalization capability of existing methods. In this paper, we use an ensemble of fine-tuned vision transformers like DINOv2, AIMv2 and OpenCLIP's ViT-L/14 to create generalizable method to detect deepfakes. We use the DF-Wild dataset released as part of the IEEE SP Cup 2025, because it uses a challenging and diverse set of manipulations and generation techniques. We started our experiments with CNN classifiers trained on spatial features. Experimental results show that our ensemble outperforms individual models and strong CNN baselines, achieving an AUC of 96.77% and an Equal Error Rate (EER) of just 9% on the DF-Wild test set, beating the state-of-the-art deepfake detection algorithm Effort by 7.05% and 8% in AUC and EER respectively. This was the winning solution for SP Cup, presented at ICASSP 2025.

鉴于生成模型的快速演进和现有检测方法泛化能力不足，本研究提出融合DINOv2、AIMv2及OpenCLIP的ViT-L/14等微调视觉Transformer的集成模型，以提升检测鲁棒性。在包含多样复杂篡改的DF-Wild数据集上，该方法超越单模型及CNN基线，测试集AUC达96.77%，EER为9%，较此前最优方法Effort分别高出7.05%和8%，并荣获IEEE SP Cup 2025冠军。
Learning Probabilistic Embeddings for Unsupervised Action Segmentation
Authors: Shuai Li, Duc Manh Vu, Juergen Gall
First: 2026-07-06T16:10:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:10:25+00:00
Comments: ECCV2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper concerns the problem of unsupervised temporal action segmentation for long, untrimmed videos. Recent successful approaches follow a joint representation learning and clustering paradigm, where optimal transport (OT) is adopted to produce pseudo labels for learning frame representations. These approaches alternate between estimating pseudo labels using OT and optimizing the parameters with gradient descent during training, where OT is used for obtaining the final temporal action segmentation. A major limitation of these works is that they learn a deterministic embedding for frame representations. The iterative procedure between learning deterministic embeddings based on pseudo labels and estimating pseudo labels from the learned embedding can thus get quickly stuck in a local optimum. As an alternative, we thus propose to learn a probabilistic embedding for frame representations. The embeddings are modeled by Gaussian distributions and we sample from the distributions before estimating the pseudo labels. We evaluate our approach on several challenging temporal action segmentation datasets and achieve results comparable to, and in some cases, better than the state of the art. Compared to baselines with deterministic embeddings, our approach improves MoF up to 20.7\% and F1-score up to 19.0\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/derkbreeze/PEOT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于无监督动作分割的概率嵌入学习
本文关注长时未剪辑视频的无监督时序动作分割问题。近期成功方法采用联合表示学习与聚类范式，利用最优传输（OT）生成伪标签以学习帧表示。这些方法在训练中交替进行：利用OT估计伪标签，再通过梯度下降优化参数，其中OT用于获得最终的时序动作分割。这些方法的主要局限在于学习帧表示的确定性嵌入。基于伪标签学习确定性嵌入与从学习到的嵌入估计伪标签的迭代过程，易陷入局部最优。为此，我们提出学习帧表示的概率嵌入。嵌入建模为高斯分布，采样后再估计伪标签。我们在多个具有挑战性的时序动作分割数据集上评估该方法，结果与当前最优方法相当，部分情况下更优。相比确定性嵌入基线，我们的方法将MoF提升最高达20.7%，F1分数提升最高达19.0%。代码见https://github.com/derkbreeze/PEOT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of deterministic embeddings in unsupervised temporal action segmentation, this paper proposes learning probabilistic embeddings via Gaussian distributions to avoid local optima in iterative pseudo-label estimation. Instead of using fixed frame representations, the method samples from learned Gaussian distributions before generating pseudo labels, enabling more flexible and robust optimization. Evaluated on multiple challenging datasets, the approach achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, improving MoF by up to 20.7% and F1-score by up to 19.0% over deterministic baselines.
为解决现有方法中确定性帧嵌入易陷入局部最优的问题，本文提出通过高斯分布建模帧表示的随机嵌入，采样后再估计伪标签以提升优化灵活性。在多个挑战性数据集上评估，该方法表现优于或持平当前最优，MoF提升最高达20.7%，F1分数最高提升19.0%。
Reasoning4Sciences: Bridging Reasoning Language Models to All Scientific Branches
Authors: Teddy Ferdinan, Bartłomiej Koptyra, Mikołaj Langner, Tomasz Adamczyk, Łukasz Radliński, Maciej Markiewicz, Aleksander Szczęsny, Stanisław Woźniak, Tymoteusz Romanowicz, Dzmitry Pihulski, Mateusz Zbrocki, Mateusz Śmigielski, Michał Rajkowski, Mateusz Biedka, Konrad Kiełczyński, Konrad Wojtasik, Jacek Duszenko, Jan Eliasz, Piotr Matys, Michał Bernacki-Janson, Maria Bellaniar Ismiati, Latius Hermawan, Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz, Anna Kubicka-Sowińska, Grzegorz Chodak, Karol Postawa, Paweł Zyblewski, Tomasz Szandała, Łukasz Sterczewski, Adrian Chajec, Paweł Niewiadomski, Piotr Gruber, Marcin Wdowikowski, Sławomir Czarnecki, Bartłomiej Kryszak, Dominik Drabik, Tomasz Kajdanowicz, Kamil Mamak, Paweł Preś, Katarzyna Paczkowska, Joachim Sobczuk, Tomasz Zięba, Jan Kocoń, Maciej Piasecki, Przemysław Kazienko
First: 2026-05-31T10:27:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:09:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) are rapidly emerging as powerful tools for scientific research, their impact is primarily concentrated in "hard science" fields. The slow -- or lack of -- adoption of RLMs in other branches of science is causing a widening gap in research productivity. In this survey, we provide the first comprehensive analysis of RLM adoption across 28 scientific disciplines following the classification used by the European Research Council (ERC), spanning the Social Sciences and Humanities, Physical Sciences and Engineering, and Life Sciences. We examine how RLMs are developed, evaluated, and applied across disciplines. Furthermore, we introduce a maturity-oriented assessment framework based on available domain-specific development and evaluation resources, revealing substantial disparities in RLM maturity that become even more pronounced when only publicly available resources are considered. Finally, we highlight current implementation paradigms that are gaining popularity across disciplines, current challenges, and future directions in enabling RLM adoption across science.
中文标题/摘要

鉴于推理语言模型（RLM）在科学领域应用不均，本调查基于欧洲研究委员会的学科分类，对28个学科的RLM应用情况进行了全面分析，发现RLM主要集中在硬科学领域，而社会科学与人文学科应用滞后。研究评估了RLM在各学科中的开发、评估与部署方式，并引入一个基于领域资源成熟度的评估框架，揭示了RLM在不同学科间的显著发展差距，尤其在仅考虑公开资源时更为明显。研究还指出了当前流行的应用范式、现存挑战及未来推动RLM全面融入科学各分支的方向。
FlowMark: Mask-Guided Video Watermarking
Authors: Vishal Asnani, Shruti Agarwal, John Collomosse
First: 2026-07-06T16:08:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:08:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present FlowMark, a video watermarking framework guided by automatically predicted object masks. In contrast to prior region-based approaches that require user-supplied mask guidance, FlowMark learns to identify optimal regions for watermark embedding through a dedicated Mask Predictor network. Our end-to-end trainable architecture combines region-aware encoding with noise-augmented training to ensure robustness against compression, geometric transformations, and content variation, while preserving high perceptual quality. Our content-adaptive masking keeps watermark signals coherent with natural video dynamics, effectively eliminating perceptual flicker. Beyond compression robustness, FlowMark maintains reliable watermark recovery under video-native temporal edits (e.g., frame swap, insertion, deletion, resampling, and interpolation) and real-world social media distribution pipelines (e.g., YouTube and Facebook re-encoding). Experimental results on both image and video datasets show that FlowMark reliably embeds $128$-bit messages with up to $50.08$ dB PSNR, offering strong performance for content provenance, temporal authenticity verification, and video integrity protection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowMark：基于掩码引导的视频水印
我们提出了FlowMark，一种由自动预测的物体掩码引导的视频水印框架。与先前需要用户提供掩码引导的区域化方法不同，FlowMark通过专用的掩码预测网络学习确定水印嵌入的最佳区域。我们的端到端可训练架构结合区域感知编码与噪声增强训练，确保在压缩、几何变换和内容变化下仍具备鲁棒性，同时保持高感知质量。我们的内容自适应掩码使水印信号与自然视频动态保持一致，有效消除感知闪烁。除压缩鲁棒性外，FlowMark在视频原生时间编辑（如帧交换、插入、删除、重采样和插值）及现实社交媒体分发流程（如YouTube和Facebook重编码）下仍能可靠恢复水印。实验结果表明，FlowMark在图像和视频数据集上可稳定嵌入128位消息，峰值信噪比高达50.08 dB，适用于内容溯源、时间真实性验证和视频完整性保护。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for automated, perceptually invisible video watermarking without manual mask input, FlowMark introduces a mask-guided framework that learns optimal embedding regions via a dedicated Mask Predictor. Its end-to-end architecture integrates region-aware encoding with noise-augmented training to enhance robustness against compression, geometric distortions, and content changes while preserving visual quality. Experimental results demonstrate that FlowMark successfully embeds 128-bit watermarks with up to 50.08 dB PSNR, maintaining reliable recovery even under temporal edits and social media re-encoding pipelines, making it suitable for provenance tracking and integrity verification.
为实现无需人工标注掩码的自动化视频水印，FlowMark提出一种基于自动预测对象掩码的框架，通过专用掩码预测器自主确定最佳嵌入区域。该端到端可训练系统结合区域感知编码与噪声增强训练，提升对压缩、几何变换和内容变化的鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉质量。实验表明，FlowMark可在50.08 dB PSNR下嵌入128位水印，即使在时间编辑和社交媒体重编码场景下仍能可靠恢复，适用于来源追踪与视频完整性保护。
Shifting from Discrete to Continuous Reference Data: QSM-Derived Horizontal Tree Biomass Distribution for Deep Learning Biomass Estimation
Authors: Nils Griese, Christoph Kleinn, Nils Nölke
First: 2026-07-06T16:08:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:08:16+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional modeling approaches for LiDAR-based above-ground biomass (AGB) estimation rely on discrete plot-level inventory aggregates. This methodology introduces boundary-effect uncertainties that may severely degrade model performance within small field plots. To solve this limitation, we evaluate a Horizontal Biomass Distribution (HBD) reference mapped continuously from Quantitative Structure Models (QSMs). We trained a sparse 3D U-Net on simulated broadleaved forest structures using three AGB reference types: a standard forest inventory (FI) plot-level aggregate, an edge-effect-free QSM plot-level aggregate, and a continuous HBD mapping. Evaluating training plot sizes scaling from 100 to 2500 $m^2$ , QSM-based models systematically outperformed FI approaches at small plot sizes. Specifically, for 100 $m^2$ plots, the HBD reference reduced the relative root mean square error (RRMSE) by 16.84 $\pm$ 4.37 % and increased $R^2$ by 0.22 $\pm$ 0.05 against the FI baseline. By replacing plot level aggregates with HBDs as AGB reference, this methodology corrects for edge-effects and shows that using an HBD-based reference enhances model performance for small plot sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从离散到连续的参考数据转换：基于QSM的水平树生物量分布用于深度学习生物量估算
传统基于LiDAR的地上生物量（AGB）建模方法依赖于离散的样地级库存汇总，该方法引入边界效应不确定性，可能严重降低小样地内模型性能。为解决此局限，我们评估了从定量结构模型（QSM）连续映射的水平生物量分布（HBD）参考数据。我们使用三种AGB参考类型训练稀疏3D U-Net：标准森林调查（FI）样地级汇总、无边缘效应的QSM样地级汇总，以及连续HBD映射。在训练样地面积从100至2500平方米变化的评估中，基于QSM的模型在小样地尺寸下系统性优于FI方法。具体而言，对于100平方米样地，HBD参考将相对均方根误差（RRMSE）降低16.84±4.37%，R²提升0.22±0.05（相对于FI基线）。通过将样地级汇总替换为HBD作为AGB参考，该方法可校正边缘效应，并表明使用HBD参考可提升小样地尺寸下的模型性能。
Summary / 总结
To address boundary-effect uncertainties that degrade biomass estimation in small LiDAR plots, this study introduces a continuous Horizontal Biomass Distribution (HBD) derived from Quantitative Structure Models (QSMs) as a reference for deep learning. A sparse 3D U-Net was trained using simulated broadleaved forest data with three AGB reference types: traditional plot-level inventory, edge-effect-free QSM aggregates, and the continuous HBD. Across plot sizes from 100 to 2500 m², models using HBD consistently outperformed those using plot-level inventory, especially at small scales. For 100 m² plots, HBD reduced relative root mean square error by 16.84 ± 4.37% and increased R² by 0.22 ± 0.05, demonstrating that continuous biomass distribution improves accuracy by mitigating edge effects.
为解决小面积LiDAR样地因边界效应导致的生物量估算精度下降问题，本研究提出利用基于定量结构模型（QSM）生成的连续水平生物量分布（HBD）作为深度学习的参考数据。研究训练了一个稀疏3D U-Net模型，使用模拟的阔叶林数据，并对比了三种生物量参考类型：传统样地级林分调查数据、无边缘效应的QSM样地聚合数据和连续HBD分布。在100至2500平方米的样地尺度下，基于QSM的模型表现始终优于传统方法，尤其在小样地表现突出。在100平方米样地中，HBD参考将相对均方根误差降低了16.84±4.37%，R²提升了0.22±0.05，表明连续HBD参考能有效消除边缘效应，提升小样地模型精度。
SalAngaBhava: A Sinhala Market Dataset for Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis
Authors: Lakshani Galwatta, Nisansa de Silva, Sarangi Aththanayake, Adithya Galwatta
First: 2026-07-06T16:05:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:05:26+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sentiment analysis has been a primary domain under Natural Language Processing (NLP) from its inception as it plays a vital role in both real-world and research applications. In high-resource languages, this has been extended a step further, and instead of predicting sentiment at the sentence level, models have been developed to detect more fine-grained sentiments at aspect level. However, in order to conduct this fine-grained Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA), datasets annotated with aspects and sentiments toward the said aspects is required. Such datasets are lacking for low-resources languages among which, we can count Sinhala, an Indo-Aryan languages used primarily in Sri Lanka. In this work, we introduce, SalAngaBhava, a new Sinhala Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis dataset which contains Sinhala product reviews that are manually labeled with aspect terms and the associated sentiments (positive, negative, neutral). The data was collected from domain-relevant sources such as user-generated reviews and comments, and was annotated following carefully defined guidelines to ensure consistency and quality. The dataset consists of sentences and aspect-sentiment pairs, encompassing a considerable range of aspects from several domains. The analysis confirms that the dataset is well-structured and sufficiently balanced for ABSA research. This dataset can be used as a benchmark and facilitates further studies related to Sinhala natural language processing, and low-resource sentiment analysis tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SalAngaBhava：用于基于方面的 sentiment 分析的僧伽罗语市场数据集
情感分析自自然语言处理（NLP）诞生以来一直是其主要研究领域，因其在现实世界和研究应用中具有重要作用。在高资源语言中，该领域进一步发展，模型已从预测句子层面的情感扩展到检测更细粒度的方面层面情感。然而，为了进行这种细粒度的基于方面的 sentiment 分析（ABSA），需要标注方面及其对应情感的数据集。对于低资源语言，此类数据集稀缺，其中僧伽罗语（主要在斯里兰卡使用的印度-雅利安语系语言）尤为明显。本文引入了 SalAngaBhava，一个全新的僧伽罗语基于方面情感分析数据集，包含人工标注方面术语及其对应情感（正面、负面、中性）的僧伽罗语产品评论。数据来源于相关领域，如用户生成的评论和评论，并遵循严格定义的标注指南进行标注，以确保一致性和质量。该数据集包含句子和方面-情感对，涵盖多个领域的广泛方面。分析表明，该数据集结构良好且足够平衡，适用于 ABSA 研究。该数据集可作为基准，促进僧伽罗语自然语言处理及低资源情感分析任务的进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of annotated datasets for low-resource languages like Sinhala, this work introduces SalAngaBhava, a manually curated Sinhala product review dataset for Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis. The dataset was constructed by collecting reviews from domain-relevant sources and annotating them with aspect terms and associated sentiment labels (positive, negative, neutral) using standardized guidelines to ensure quality. Experimental analysis confirms the dataset's well-structured nature and balanced distribution across aspects and sentiments, making it suitable as a benchmark for ABSA research in Sinhala and low-resource NLP tasks.
为解决斯里兰卡主要使用的印地语系低资源语言——僧伽罗语缺乏面向方面的情感分析标注数据的问题，本研究构建了SalAngaBhava数据集。该数据集通过收集领域相关评论并按统一标准人工标注方面词与情感标签（正面、负面、中性）而成，确保了数据质量和一致性。实验分析表明，该数据集结构合理、情感分布均衡，适合作为僧伽罗语及低资源语言情感分析研究的基准数据。
PuzzleMoE: Efficient Compression of Large Mixture-of-Experts Models via Sparse Expert Merging and Bit-packed inference
Authors: Yushu Zhao, Zheng Wang, Minjia Zhang
First: 2025-11-06T20:53:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:04:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have shown strong potential in scaling language models efficiently by activating only a small subset of experts per input. However, their widespread deployment remains limited due to the high memory overhead associated with storing all expert parameters, particularly as the number of experts increases. To address this challenge, prior works have explored expert dropping and merging strategies, yet they often suffer from performance drop at high compression ratios. In this paper, we introduce PuzzleMoE, a training-free MoE compression method that achieves both high accuracy and efficient inference through two key innovations: First, PuzzleMoE performs sparse expert merging by identifying element-wise weight redundancy and specialization. It uses a dual-mask to capture both shared and expert-specific parameters. Second, to avoid the overhead of storing binary masks and signs, PuzzleMoE introduces a bit-packed encoding scheme that reuses underutilized exponent bits, enabling efficient MoE inference on GPUs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PuzzleMoE can compress MoE models by up to 50% while maintaining accuracy across various tasks. Specifically, it outperforms prior MoE compression methods by up to 16.7% on MMLU at 50% compression ratio, and achieves up to 1.28\times inference speedup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PuzzleMoE：通过稀疏专家合并与位打包推理高效压缩大型专家混合模型
专家混合（MoE）模型通过仅激活每个输入的小部分专家，在扩展语言模型时展现出强大潜力。然而，由于存储所有专家参数带来的高内存开销，其广泛应用仍受限，尤其当专家数量增加时。为解决此问题，先前工作探索了专家丢弃与合并策略，但常在高压缩比下导致性能下降。本文提出PuzzleMoE，一种无需训练的MoE压缩方法，通过两项创新实现高精度与高效推理：首先，PuzzleMoE通过识别元素级权重冗余与专业化进行稀疏专家合并，使用双掩码捕捉共享与专家特有参数；其次，为避免存储二进制掩码与符号的开销，PuzzleMoE引入位打包编码方案，重用未充分利用的指数位，实现GPU上的高效MoE推理。大量实验表明，PuzzleMoE可将MoE模型压缩达50%并保持各任务精度。具体而言，在50%压缩比下，其在MMLU上相较先前MoE压缩方法最高提升16.7%，并实现最高1.28倍推理加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory cost of storing all experts in MoE models, PuzzleMoE introduces a training-free compression method that combines sparse expert merging with bit-packed inference. It identifies weight redundancy and specialization using a dual-mask to separate shared and expert-specific parameters, then encodes the masks efficiently by repurposing exponent bits in floating-point representations. Experimental results show that PuzzleMoE achieves up to 50% model compression with minimal accuracy loss, outperforming prior methods by 16.7% on MMLU at 50% compression and delivering up to 1.28x faster inference.
为降低MoE模型存储所有专家带来的高内存开销，本文提出PuzzleMoE，一种无需训练的压缩方法，兼顾高精度与高效推理。其通过稀疏专家合并，识别权重冗余与专业化，利用双掩码分离共享与专家特有参数；同时引入位打包编码方案，复用未被充分利用的指数位，避免存储二进制掩码与符号，实现GPU高效推理。实验表明，PuzzleMoE可将MoE模型压缩达50%且保持性能，于50%压缩率下MMLU任务上优于现有方法16.7%，推理速度提升最高达1.28倍。
GeoFlow: Geo-Aware Modeling of Inter-Area Relationships in Origin-Destination Flow Prediction and Generation
Authors: Zherui Huang, Guanjie Zheng, Hao Xue, Linghe Kong
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-06T16:04:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:04:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Origin-destination (OD) flow modeling underpins urban planning and mobility analysis, but prevailing graph-based methods often neglect salient geographic attributes, limiting their ability to model long-range and multi-area dependencies. In this paper, we introduce GeoFlow, a novel framework that (i) augments area representations with geospatial attributes, including relative positions, k-hop and geodesic distances, (ii) employs a specialized geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder design that combines graph attention for intrinsic area signals with coordinate-aware encoders for global structure, and (iii) adopts an axial-global attention decoder to capture OD-specific competitive dependencies. For OD flow generation, GeoFlow is paired with flow matching models to produce more authentic and diverse mobility samples. Empirically, GeoFlow achieves superior performance in predictive accuracy, while substantially improving generative fidelity and diversity. Ablation and analytical studies confirm the contribution of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoFlow：面向起讫点流预测与生成的地理感知区域关系建模
起讫点（OD）流建模是城市规划与交通分析的基础，但现有基于图的方法常忽略关键地理属性，限制其建模长距离及多区域依赖的能力。本文提出GeoFlow框架，其（i）通过相对位置、k跳及测地距离等地理属性增强区域表示；（ii）采用专门的几何内禀融合编码器，结合图注意力机制处理区域内在信号与坐标感知编码器处理全局结构；（iii）采用轴向-全局注意力解码器捕捉OD特异性竞争依赖关系。在OD流生成中，GeoFlow与流匹配模型结合，生成更真实多样化的移动样本。实证表明，GeoFlow在预测精度上表现更优，同时显著提升生成保真度与多样性。消融与分析研究验证各组件贡献。代码见https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing graph-based OD flow models that overlook geographic context, GeoFlow introduces a framework that enriches area representations with geospatial features like relative positions and distances, employs a geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder combining graph attention with coordinate-aware modules, and uses an axial-global attention decoder to model OD-specific dependencies. Experiments show GeoFlow outperforms baselines in prediction accuracy and significantly enhances the authenticity and diversity of generated flows, with ablation studies validating each component’s contribution.
鉴于现有图结构OD流模型忽略地理属性导致难以建模长距离多区域依赖，GeoFlow提出一种融合地理特征的框架，通过引入相对位置与距离度量增强区域表示。其采用几何-内在融合编码器结合图注意力与坐标感知模块，并使用轴向全局注意力解码器捕捉OD特异性竞争依赖。实验表明，GeoFlow在预测精度上优于基线方法，同时显著提升生成流的真实性与多样性，消融实验验证各组件的有效性。
DreamShot: Personalized Storyboard Synthesis with Video Diffusion Prior
Authors: Junjia Huang, Binbin Yang, Pengxiang Yan, Jiyang Liu, Bin Xia, Zhao Wang, Yitong Wang, Liang Lin, Guanbin Li
First: 2026-04-19T01:51:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:02:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026 as a Highlight paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Storyboard synthesis plays a crucial role in visual storytelling, aiming to generate coherent shot sequences that visually narrate cinematic events with consistent characters, scenes, and transitions. However, existing approaches are mostly adapted from text-to-image diffusion models, which struggle to maintain long-range temporal coherence, consistent character identities, and narrative flow across multiple shots. In this paper, we introduce DreamShot, a video generative model based storyboard framework that fully exploits powerful video diffusion priors for controllable multi-shot synthesis. DreamShot supports both Text-to-Shot and Reference-to-Shot generation, as well as story continuation conditioned on previous frames, enabling flexible and context-aware storyboard generation. By leveraging the spatial-temporal consistency inherent in video generative models, DreamShot produces visually and semantically coherent sequences with improved narrative fidelity and character continuity. Furthermore, DreamShot incorporates a multi-reference role conditioning module that accepts multiple character reference images and enforces identity alignment via a Role-Attention Consistency Loss, explicitly constraining attention between reference and generated roles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DreamShot achieves superior scene coherence, role consistency, and generation efficiency compared to state-of-the-art text-to-image storyboard models, establishing a new direction toward controllable video model-driven visual storytelling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamShot：基于视频扩散先验的个性化分镜合成
分镜合成在视觉叙事中起着关键作用，旨在生成视觉上连贯的镜头序列，以一致的角色、场景和转场来叙事电影事件。然而，现有方法大多借鉴文本到图像扩散模型，难以维持长时序一致性、角色身份一致性和多镜头间的叙事连贯性。本文提出DreamShot，一种基于视频生成模型的分镜框架，充分利用强大的视频扩散先验，实现可控的多镜头合成。DreamShot支持文本到镜头和参考图像到镜头生成，以及基于前帧的续写，实现灵活且上下文感知的分镜生成。通过利用视频生成模型固有的时空一致性，DreamShot生成视觉与语义上连贯的序列，提升叙事忠实度与角色连续性。此外，DreamShot引入多参考角色条件模块，接受多个角色参考图像，并通过角色注意力一致性损失强制角色身份对齐，显式约束参考角色与生成角色间的注意力关系。大量实验表明，DreamShot在场景连贯性、角色一致性及生成效率方面优于现有最先进的文本到图像分镜模型，为可控视频模型驱动的视觉叙事开辟新方向。
