Experimental results show DreamShot outperforms existing text-to-image methods in scene coherence, role consistency, and generation efficiency, establishing a new paradigm for controllable video-driven visual storytelling.
为了解决现有文本到图像扩散模型在生成时间连贯、叙事一致的分镜时存在的不足，DreamShot提出了一种基于视频扩散先验的框架，显式利用时空一致性进行多镜头合成。它支持文本驱动和参考图像驱动的镜头生成，并能根据前序帧进行故事延续，增强上下文感知能力。模型引入角色注意力一致性损失，将生成角色与参考图像对齐，提升角色连续性。实验表明，DreamShot在场景连贯性、角色一致性及生成效率上优于现有方法，确立了可控视频驱动视觉叙事的新范式。
Diffusion Models are Open-World Affordance Learners: Leveraging Generative Priors for 3D Affordance Learning
Authors: Hanqing Wang, Zhenhao Zhang, Kaiyang Ji, Mingyu Liu, Wenti Yin, yuchao chen, Zhirui Liu, Xiangyu Zeng, Tianxiang Gui, Hangxing Zhang, Jiahao Yuan, Zhiqing Cui, Jiaxin Liu, Zhiyuan Ma, Hui Xiong
First: 2025-08-03T08:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:02:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D affordance grounding aims to understand how diverse objects can be manipulated, making it a cornerstone of embodied interaction. However, prior works struggle to generalize to out-of-distribution, open-world scenarios, leaving a critical gap between limited dataset performance and real-world application needs. Inspired by the saying: \textit{\textbf{``What I can not create, I do not understand''}}, we find generative models can generate semantically valid HOI images, which indicates inherent encoding of affordance concepts. Building on this insight, we propose DAG, the first innovative diffusion-based 3D affordance grounding framework that extracts general affordance knowledge from text-to-image diffusion models for 3D affordance prediction. Specifically, we extract the affordance priors from a diffusion model to encode HOI priors, and design an affordance block with a multi-source affordance decoder for dense 3D affordance prediction. Extensive experiments show that DAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods and exhibits strong open-world generalization, even in the challenging one-shot setting. The code of our method is released on \textcolor{blue}{\textit{https://github.com/hq-King/DAG}}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型是开放世界能力学习者：利用生成先验进行3D能力学习
3D能力定位旨在理解多样物体如何被操作，是具身交互的核心。然而，现有方法难以泛化到分布外、开放世界场景，导致有限数据集表现与实际应用需求之间存在关键差距。受名言“我不能创造的，我不理解”启发，我们发现生成模型能生成语义有效的HOI图像，表明其内含能力概念编码。基于此洞察，我们提出DAG，首个基于扩散的3D能力定位框架，从文本到图像扩散模型中提取通用能力知识用于3D能力预测。具体而言，我们从扩散模型中提取能力先验以编码HOI先验，并设计包含多源能力解码器的能力模块，实现密集3D能力预测。大量实验表明，DAG持续优于现有最先进方法，且在具有挑战性的单样本设置下仍表现出强大的开放世界泛化能力。我们的代码已发布于\textcolor{blue}{\textit{https://github.com/hq-King/DAG}}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for 3D affordance models that generalize to unseen, open-world scenarios, this work leverages generative diffusion models to encode affordance priors from text-to-image generation, recognizing that what a model can generate reflects its understanding of object manipulation. The proposed DAG framework extracts these priors and integrates them into a 3D affordance prediction system via a multi-source affordance decoder, enabling dense spatial reasoning. Experiments demonstrate that DAG surpasses existing methods across multiple benchmarks and maintains strong performance even under one-shot conditions, highlighting its robustness to distribution shifts.
为解决3D物体操作能力在开放世界中泛化能力不足的问题，该研究利用文本到图像的扩散模型生成语义有效的“人-物体-交互”图像，从中提取操作先验知识，构建首个基于扩散模型的3D操作能力预测框架DAG。该框架通过多源操作解码器实现稠密空间操作预测。实验表明，DAG在多个基准上超越现有方法，并在单样本设置下仍保持优异泛化能力，验证其对分布外场景的鲁棒性。
A Hybrid Quantum Circuit Born Machine Framework for Financial Volatility Forecasting: Quantum-Assisted Training and Classical Inference
Authors: Yixiong Chen
First: 2026-03-10T15:23:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:00:19+00:00
Comments: Revised title to better highlight the paper's main theme; updated and clarified the experimental results discussion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate financial volatility forecasting is crucial but challenged by the non-linear, highly correlated nature of market data. Recently, quantum computing has emerged as a promising paradigm for solving complex high-dimensional sampling problems. To harness this, we propose a novel hybrid framework combining the temporal representation power of classical neural networks with the distribution-learning capabilities of quantum models. Specifically, we integrate a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network with a Quantum Circuit Born Machine (QCBM). The LSTM extracts dynamic features, while the QCBM acts as a learnable generative prior modeling complex market distributions to guide forecasting. Evaluated on 5-minute high-frequency data from the SSE Composite and CSI 300 indices, our model significantly outperforms a classical LSTM baseline across MSE, RMSE, and QLIKE metrics. Furthermore, by introducing a stochastic ``Drop-Prior" mechanism during training, the LSTM implicitly distills structured information from the quantum prior. This establishes a pragmatic paradigm of ``quantum-assisted training with classical-efficient inference", whereby the model retains its quantum-enhanced accuracy even when the quantum module is entirely disabled during deployment. This demonstrates a practical pathway for leveraging quantum computing to enhance classical models without real-time quantum inference latency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于金融波动率预测的混合量子电路生成器框架：量子辅助训练与经典推理
准确的金融波动率预测至关重要，但市场数据具有非线性和高度相关性，给预测带来挑战。近年来，量子计算作为解决复杂高维采样问题的有前景范式逐渐兴起。为此，我们提出一种新颖的混合框架，结合经典神经网络的时间表示能力与量子模型的分布学习能力。具体而言，我们将长短期记忆（LSTM）网络与量子电路生成器（QCBM）相结合：LSTM提取动态特征，QCBM作为可学习的生成先验，建模复杂市场分布以指导预测。在上证综合指数和沪深300指数的5分钟高频数据上评估，我们的模型在MSE、RMSE和QLIKE指标上显著优于经典LSTM基线。此外，通过在训练中引入随机“Drop-Prior”机制，LSTM隐式地从量子先验中蒸馏结构化信息。该框架建立了一种“量子辅助训练、经典高效推理”的实用范式，即使在部署时完全禁用量子模块，模型仍能保持量子增强的精度，为利用量子计算提升经典模型提供了切实可行的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately forecasting financial volatility amid complex, non-linear market dynamics, this paper proposes a hybrid framework that combines a classical LSTM network with a Quantum Circuit Born Machine (QCBM) to model market distributions. The LSTM captures temporal dependencies, while the QCBM serves as a learnable generative prior to guide forecasting. Evaluated on high-frequency 5-minute data from the SSE Composite and CSI 300 indices, the hybrid model significantly outperforms a classical LSTM baseline across MSE, RMSE, and QLIKE metrics. A key innovation is the "Drop-Prior" mechanism, which enables the LSTM to implicitly learn from the quantum prior during training, allowing the model to maintain quantum-enhanced accuracy even when the quantum component is disabled during inference, thus enabling practical quantum-assisted training with classical inference.
为应对金融市场数据非线性、强相关性导致的波动率预测难题，本文提出一种结合LSTM与量子电路出生机（QCBM）的混合量子-经典框架，其中LSTM提取时序特征，QCBM作为可学习生成先验建模复杂市场分布以引导预测。在SSE综合指数和CSI 300指数的5分钟高频数据上，该模型在MSE、RMSE和QLIKE等指标上显著优于纯LSTM基线。创新性引入“Drop-Prior”机制，使LSTM在训练中隐式吸收量子先验的结构信息，从而在部署时即使关闭量子模块，仍能保持量子增强的预测精度，实现“量子辅助训练、经典高效推理”的实用范式。
Repurposing CLIP to Localize at Pixel Level
Authors: Jiaxiang Fang, Shiqiang Ma, Jing Wang, Siyu Chen, Fei Guo, Shengfeng He
First: 2026-07-06T16:00:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T16:00:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE TMM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale Vision-Language Models like CLIP have demonstrated impressive open-set localization capabilities at the image level. However, adapting this capability to pixel-level dense prediction poses challenges due to global feature biases. In this paper, we introduce CLIPix, a simple yet effective framework that repurposes CLIP to perform pixel-level localization. By tracing back CLIP's classification process, CLIPix identifies object-specific attentive regions and repurposes them as pixel-level localization cues. To address noise introduced by global biases, we propose a Noise-Resistant Correction strategy, refining these cues for more precise segmentation. Additionally, we introduce a Localization Embedding strategy to integrate both localization and enriched detail information, enabling accurate, high-resolution segmentation. Our approach preserves CLIP's generalization strength and unlocks its potential for segmenting arbitrary objects. Extensive experiments on the PASCAL and COCO datasets demonstrate that CLIPix achieves state-of-the-art performance, underscoring its effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将CLIP重新用于像素级定位
大规模视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在图像级开放集定位方面表现出色，但将其能力迁移到像素级密集预测时，由于全局特征偏差会带来挑战。本文提出CLIPix，一个简单而有效的框架，将CLIP重新用于像素级定位。通过回溯CLIP的分类过程，CLIPix识别出对象特定的注意力区域，并将其重新用于像素级定位线索。为解决全局偏差引入的噪声，我们提出抗噪校正策略，以更精确地优化这些线索。此外，我们引入定位嵌入策略，整合定位与丰富细节信息，实现高精度分割。该方法保留了CLIP的泛化能力，并释放其对任意对象分割的潜力。在PASCAL和COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，CLIPix达到当前最先进性能，凸显其有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend CLIP's open-set image-level localization to precise pixel-level segmentation, this paper introduces CLIPix, a framework that repurposes CLIP by tracing its attention mechanisms to identify object-specific regions at the pixel level. To mitigate global feature biases that introduce noise, it employs a Noise-Resistant Correction strategy to refine localization cues, and a Localization Embedding strategy to fuse localization signals with detailed visual information. Experiments on PASCAL and COCO show CLIPix achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating its effectiveness in high-resolution, arbitrary object segmentation while preserving CLIP’s generalization ability.
为将CLIP的图像级定位能力迁移到像素级密集预测，本文提出CLIPix框架，通过追溯CLIP的分类注意力机制识别目标特定区域，并将其转化为像素级定位线索。为缓解全局特征偏差带来的噪声，引入抗噪修正策略优化定位信号，并结合定位嵌入策略融合细节信息，实现高分辨率分割。在PASCAL和COCO数据集上的实验表明，CLIPix达到当前最优性能，验证了其在任意物体分割中的有效性，且未牺牲CLIP原有的泛化能力。
FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation
Authors: Weichen Qin, Yufan Xie, Peihao Wang, Chia-Jui Chou, Minghui Du, Peng Xu, Ziren Luo, Yi Yang, Jingyi Yu, Bo Liang, Jiakai Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-06T15:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T15:59:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026). 22 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FUSE：基于费曼-卡西米尔引导的多模态流匹配用于高效的基于模拟的后验估计
基于模拟的推断（SBI）对科学发现至关重要，生成模型为高效推断提供了有前景的路径。然而，现有方法在有效建模多模态方面存在困难，常依赖暴力融合策略，忽视参数与观测之间的结构差异，从而限制了估计精度。本文提出FUSE（Feynman-Kac引导的多模态流匹配用于高效的基于模拟的后验估计）。与以往工作不同，FUSE采用双轨架构，在保留多模态输入独特特征的同时促进动态交互。此外，我们提出一种FK引导的采样策略，利用中间观测似然引导生成轨迹，有效提升推断过程中的样本质量。我们的方法在标准SBI基准测试中优于现有最先进基线，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC结果高度吻合。在现实世界系外行星轨道估计任务中，FUSE成功解决了现有方法难以处理的复杂参数退化问题，凸显其在天体物理及其他复杂科学发现中的加速潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate posterior estimation in simulation-based inference, especially for multimodal problems, FUSE introduces a dual-track architecture that preserves structural distinctions between parameters and observations while enabling dynamic interaction. It further employs an FK-steered sampling strategy that uses intermediate observation likelihoods to guide generative trajectories, enhancing sample quality. Experiments show FUSE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors close to ground-truth MCMC samples, and successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task.
为解决模拟推断中多模态条件下后验估计效率与精度不足的问题，FUSE提出双轨架构，保留参数与观测值的结构差异并支持动态交互，同时引入FK引导采样策略，利用中间观测似然引导生成轨迹以提升样本质量。实验表明，FUSE在标准SBI基准上优于现有最先进方法，其后验分布接近真实MCMC样本，且在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中成功解决复杂参数退化问题，展现出在天体物理等复杂科学发现中的应用潜力。
Privacy-Preserving Robustness Verification for Neural Networks
Authors: Nianyun Song, Xiaokun Luan, Yu Guo, Rongfang Bie, Meng Sun, Xiyue Zhang
First: 2026-07-06T15:59:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T15:59:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted by UAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural network verification and data privacy are inherently in tension: verification demands full access to model parameters and input data, yet both are increasingly restricted by privacy regulations and intellectual property constraints. This tension has left robustness verification impractical in privacy-sensitive domains. In this work, we address this gap with SecureCROWN, the first framework for privacy-preserving neural network robustness verification. Built upon secure two-party computation (2PC), our framework enables a model owner and a data owner to jointly compute certified robustness bounds -- revealing only the final result while provably protecting both parties' private data under the semi-honest security model. A key challenge is securely computing the conditional operations in Linear Bound Propagation, where the data-dependent branching is incompatible with standard secure computation protocols. We eliminate branching by formulating conditional logic as continuous arithmetic operations. Additionally, we introduce a Newton--Raphson refinement method to improve numerical stability. Extensive analysis and experiments show that SecureCROWN strictly matches plaintext verification results, while completing in 0.1--200s across varied model sizes and communication settings (LAN/WAN), demonstrating the feasibility of privacy-preserving neural network verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：隐私保护的神经网络鲁棒性验证
神经网络验证与数据隐私本质上存在矛盾：验证需要完全访问模型参数和输入数据，但两者正因隐私法规和知识产权限制而日益受限。这种矛盾使得鲁棒性验证在隐私敏感领域难以实施。本文提出SecureCROWN，这是首个隐私保护的神经网络鲁棒性验证框架。基于安全两方计算（2PC），该框架使模型所有者与数据所有者能够联合计算可验证的鲁棒性界限——仅揭示最终结果，同时在半诚实安全模型下严格保护双方私有数据。关键挑战在于安全计算线性界限传播中的条件操作，其中数据依赖的分支与标准安全计算协议不兼容。我们通过将条件逻辑转化为连续算术运算消除了分支。此外，我们引入牛顿-拉夫森改进方法以提升数值稳定性。大量分析和实验表明，SecureCROWN严格匹配明文验证结果，且在不同模型规模和通信设置（局域网/广域网）下完成时间在0.1至200秒之间，证明了隐私保护神经网络验证的可行性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the conflict between neural network verification's need for full data access and growing privacy constraints, this work introduces SecureCROWN, the first privacy-preserving framework for robustness verification. Leveraging secure two-party computation, it allows model and data owners to jointly compute certified robustness bounds without exposing private inputs, under the semi-honest model. To handle conditional operations in Linear Bound Propagation, the method reformulates branching as continuous arithmetic, and employs a Newton--Raphson refinement for numerical stability. Experiments show SecureCROWN achieves results indistinguishable from plaintext verification, with computation times ranging from 0.1 to 200 seconds across diverse models and network conditions.
为解决神经网络验证对数据完整访问的需求与日益严格的隐私保护之间的矛盾，本文提出SecureCROWN，首个隐私保护的鲁棒性验证框架。该框架基于安全多方计算，使模型所有者与数据所有者可联合计算可验证的鲁棒性界限，而无需泄露私有数据，满足半诚实安全模型。为处理线性边界传播中的条件分支，方法将其转化为连续算术运算，并引入牛顿-拉夫森方法提升数值稳定性。实验表明，SecureCROWN在各类模型和网络环境下（局域网/广域网）耗时0.1至200秒，结果与明文验证完全一致，证明隐私保护下的神经网络验证具备可行性。
